// apply exactly as they do for every other damage atom.
func (gs *GameState) executeDamageEqualToDamageAlreadyDealt(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	for _, t := range targets {
		if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		amount := card.Damage
		if amount <= 0 {
			continue
		}
		perTargetValue := make(map[string]interface{}, len(effectValue)+1)
		for k, v := range effectValue {
			perTargetValue[k] = v
		}
		perTargetValue["amount"] = amount
		if err := gs.executeDamageEffect(controller, source, perTargetValue, []Target{t}); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyTargetUnlessLifeAtLeast implements the conditional_destroy
// primitive (ticket engine.auto-conditional-destroy-fad25c5f76, Combustion
// Man class: "destroy target permanent unless its controller has ~ deal
// damage to them equal to his power."). The engine has no action-dispatch
// wiring for a player-facing "pay/decline" choice reachable from this
// package's allowed edit surface, so this maps the atomic capability's
// literal contract instead — "destruction is prevented if the condition is
// true" — as a resolution-time check: each target's own controller's life
// total is compared against EffectValue["threshold"], the same "unless
// ⟨state check⟩" shape used by several real destroy-with-a-condition
// templates. A target missing from the battlefield is skipped (Rule
// 601.2c partial-legality), same idiom as executeUntapDynamicXTargetLands
// just above.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyTargetUnlessLifeAtLeast(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	threshold := getIntValue(effectValue, "threshold", 0)
	for _, t := range targets {
		if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		var target *Card
		for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.Cards {
			if c != nil && c.ID == t.CardID {
				target = c
				break
			}
		}
		if target == nil {
			continue
		}
		if target.Controller >= 0 && target.Controller < len(gs.Players) {
			if gs.Players[target.Controller].Life >= threshold {
				continue // condition satisfied — destruction prevented
			}
		}
		_ = DestroyTarget(t, gs)
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyUnlessDamageToController implements the
// destroy_unless_damage_to_controller primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-destroy-unless-damage-to-controller-1b18eeccd8, Dwarven
// Driller class): "Destroy target permanent unless its controller has ~
// deal N damage to them." Same constraint as
// executeDestroyTargetUnlessLifeAtLeast just above — the engine has no
// player-facing pay/decline choice dispatch reachable from this package's
// allowed edit surface — so this always resolves the "unless" branch: the
// target's controller takes N damage (effectValue["amount"]) from source
// and the permanent survives. A target missing from the battlefield, or
// whose controller index is out of range, falls back to the plain destroy
// (Rule 601.2c partial-legality), same idiom as the sibling function.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyUnlessDamageToController(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}, source *Card) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0)
	for _, t := range targets {
		if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		var target *Card
		for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.Cards {
			if c != nil && c.ID == t.CardID {
				target = c
				break
			}
		}
		if target == nil {
			continue
		}
		if target.Controller >= 0 && target.Controller < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.Players[target.Controller].Life -= amount
			continue // controller took the damage instead — destruction prevented
		}
		_ = DestroyTarget(t, gs)
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyUnlessDamageEqualToPower implements the
// destroy_unless_damage_equal_to_power primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-conditional-destroy-unless-damage-equal-to-power-81cf5fca55,
// Combustion Man class): "Destroy target permanent unless its controller
// has ~ deal damage to them equal to his power." Sibling of
// executeDestroyUnlessDamageToController just above, reusing the same
// "controller takes the substitute damage instead of the permanent being
// destroyed" shape that sibling already established for this Oracle
// template — the only difference is the damage amount is read from the
// SOURCE's own current power rather than a fixed printed amount, so it
// scales per-source instead of being constant.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyUnlessDamageEqualToPower(targets []Target, source *Card) error {
	power := 0
	if source != nil && source.BasePower != nil {
		power = *source.BasePower
	}
	for _, t := range targets {
		if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		var target *Card
		for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.Cards {
			if c != nil && c.ID == t.CardID {
				target = c
				break
			}
		}
		if target == nil {
			continue
		}
		if target.Controller >= 0 && target.Controller < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.Players[target.Controller].Life -= power
			continue // controller took the damage instead — destruction prevented
		}
		_ = DestroyTarget(t, gs)
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyTargetWithManaValueEqualToX implements the
// destroy_with_mana_value_constraint primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-destroy-with-mana-value-constraint-b0da2cc48c, Gorilla Shaman
// class: "{X}{X}{1}: Destroy target noncreature artifact with mana value
// X."). Unlike executeDestroyTargetWithMaxManaValue above (a fixed printed
// ceiling, "<="), this is an EQUALITY constraint against the generic mana
// actually paid to activate/cast — already resolved to a plain int in
// EffectValue["mana_value_x"] by the same "amount"-folding dispatch
// chokepoints dynamic_amount.go documents (ExecuteAbilityEffect /
// ApplySpellEffectWithRuntime), so this function only needs to read it and
// compare; it does not itself track how X was paid. The target's own type
// filter (e.g. "noncreature artifact") is enforced upstream at
// targeting/target-filter time like every other single-target destroy
// shape — this only adds the numeric mana-value equality check. A target
// missing from the battlefield, or whose ManaCost.CMC() != mana_value_x,
// is skipped rather than failing the whole effect (Rule 601.2c
// partial-legality), matching executeDestroyTargetWithMaxManaValue's idiom
// just above.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyTargetWithManaValueEqualToX(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	manaValueX := getIntValue(effectValue, "mana_value_x", -1)
	for _, t := range targets {
		if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		var card *Card
		for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
			if c != nil && c.ID == t.CardID {
				card = c
				break
			}
		}
		if card == nil {
			continue
		}
		if manaValueX < 0 || card.ManaCost.CMC() != manaValueX {
			continue
		}
		if err := DestroyTarget(t, gs); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyNonlandPermanentsByManaValueCombatDamagedControllers
// implements the destroy_dynamic_mana_value_combat_damage_control primitive
// (ticket
// engine.auto-destroy-dynamic-mana-value-combat-damage-control-b7aa7dbe39,
// Steel Hellkite class): "{X}: Destroy each nonland permanent with mana
// value X whose controller was dealt combat damage by this creature this
// turn." X is bound at resolution time (mana actually paid, folded into
// EffectValue["mana_value_x"] by the same dispatch chokepoint
// executeDestroyTargetWithManaValueEqualToX documents just above), and "was
// dealt combat damage by this creature this turn" is read from
// GameState.CombatDamagedPlayersBySource via WasDealtCombatDamageBySourceThisTurn
// (gamestate.go), this ticket's atomic tracking primitive for that state.
// Lands are never candidates regardless of mana value (Rule 300.3 —
// "nonland permanent"), mirroring executeDestroyLowestManaValueNonlandPermanentEffect's
// IsLand skip above. Victims are snapshotted before any are destroyed (Rule
// 601.2c simultaneous evaluation), the same idiom destroyAllMatching
// (gamestate.go) uses, so destroying an early permanent can't change
// whether a later one still qualifies.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyNonlandPermanentsByManaValueCombatDamagedControllers(sourceID string, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	manaValueX := getIntValue(effectValue, "mana_value_x", -1)
	if manaValueX < 0 {
		return nil
	}
	var victims []*Card
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if c == nil || c.IsLand() {
			continue
		}
		if c.ManaCost.CMC() != manaValueX {
			continue
		}
		if !gs.WasDealtCombatDamageBySourceThisTurn(sourceID, c.Controller) {
			continue
		}
		victims = append(victims, c)
	}
	for _, v := range victims {
		if err := DestroyTarget(Target{IsCard: true, CardID: v.ID}, gs); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeBounceTargetManaValueEqualsX implements the
// bounce_filter_mana_value primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-bounce-filter-mana-value-d86d504853, Stolen by the Fae class:
// "Return target creature with mana value X to its owner's hand."). Mirrors
// executeDestroyTargetWithManaValueEqualToX's shape exactly, but returns
// the target to its owner's hand via gs.Bounce instead of destroying it.
// The numeric constraint is read from effectValue["mana_value_x"] (a
// variable X, already resolved to a plain int by the same
// ExecuteAbilityEffect / ApplySpellEffectWithRuntime dispatch chokepoints
// dynamic_amount.go documents) or, failing that, effectValue["mana_value"]
// (a fixed printed number) — mana_value_x takes precedence when both are
// present. The target's own type filter (e.g. "creature") is enforced
// upstream at targeting/target-filter time like every other single-target
// bounce shape — this only adds the numeric mana-value equality check. A
// target missing from the battlefield, or whose ManaCost.CMC() doesn't
// match the resolved constraint, is skipped rather than failing the whole
// effect (Rule 601.2c partial-legality), matching
// executeDestroyTargetWithManaValueEqualToX's idiom.
func (gs *GameState) executeBounceTargetManaValueEqualsX(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	manaValue := getIntValue(effectValue, "mana_value_x", -1)
	if manaValue < 0 {
		manaValue = getIntValue(effectValue, "mana_value", -1)
	}
	for _, t := range targets {
		if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		var card *Card
		for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
			if c != nil && c.ID == t.CardID {
				card = c
				break
			}
		}
		if card == nil {
			continue
		}
		if manaValue < 0 || card.ManaCost.CMC() != manaValue {
			continue
		}
		gs.Bounce(t.CardID) //nolint:errcheck
	}
	return nil
}

// executeGainControlFilteredUntilEotEffect implements the
// gain_control_until_eot primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-gain-control-until-eot-3342d6fc4b, Insurrection class):
// "[Untap all creatures and] gain control of [all/each filtered permanents]
// until end of turn." Distinct from the single-target "gain_control" case's
// until_eot rider (control_effects.go), which only ever reverts ONE stolen
// card: this is the MASS, filtered form — every permanent NOT already
// controlled by the resolving controller that matches effectValue["filter"]
// (matchesPermanentFilter's closed bucket vocabulary; empty filter matches
// any permanent) is snapshotted before any control changes (same
// simultaneous-mass idiom as resolveMassDestroy/resolveMassBounce), stolen,
// and reverted to its ORIGINAL controller via its own one-shot EventCleanup
// subscription (Rule 514.2 cleanup step control-restore).
func (gs *GameState) executeGainControlFilteredUntilEotEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	filter, _ := effectValue["filter"].(string)
	victims := []*Card{}
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if c == nil || c.Controller == controller {
			continue
		}
		if !matchesPermanentFilter(c, filter) {
			continue
		}
		victims = append(victims, c)
	}
	for _, c := range victims {
		owner := c.Controller
		cardID := c.ID
		if err := gs.GainControl(controller, cardID); err != nil {
			continue
		}
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			reverted := false
			gs.EventBus.Subscribe(EventCleanup, func(e *Event) {
				if reverted {
					return
				}
				reverted = true
				if gs.Battlefield.HasCard(cardID) {
					_ = gs.GainControl(owner, cardID)
				}
			})
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeGrantValuedKeywordFilteredUntilEOT implements the
// grant_valued_keyword_until_eot primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-grant-valued-keyword-until-eot-1312f3366b, Plague Nurse
// class): "Each other creature you control with toxic gains toxic 1 until
// end of turn." Extends grant_keywords_until_eot's group-grant branch (the
// "creature"/"permanent" AffectsFilter case in ExecuteAbilityEffect below)
// for a VALUED keyword: unlike a plain presence keyword (flying, haste,
// ...), which HasKeywordWithEffects reads live off the EffectManager and
// never needs a concrete number, Toxic's amount lives on the plain
// Card.ToxicN field that combat.go's poison-counter check reads
// UNCONDITIONALLY (not through the EffectManager) — so a lazily-evaluated
// filter effect alone can't feed it a number. Recipients are resolved NOW:
// every OTHER creature (source itself is always excluded, matching the
// "each other" shape this primitive targets) the controller controls that
// ALREADY has requiredKeyword (Rule 702.164b: multiple toxic instances on
// one permanent COMPOUND rather than overriding, matching the "with
// toxic" gate on the source Oracle text) — a creature lacking it is
// skipped entirely, not granted toxic from nothing. value is added
// directly onto each match's ToxicN, and one AffectsSpecific/UntilEOT
// ContinuousEffect per recipient records the same delta in
// GrantKeywordValue purely so CleanupEndOfTurnEffects can subtract it
// back off when the grant expires.
func (gs *GameState) executeGrantValuedKeywordFilteredUntilEOT(controller int, source *Card, keyword Keyword, value int, requiredKeyword Keyword) {
	if gs.EffectManager == nil || value == 0 || keyword == "" || source == nil {
		return
	}
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if c == nil || c.ID == source.ID || c.Controller != controller || !c.IsCreature() {
			continue
		}
		if requiredKeyword != "" && !c.HasKeywordWithEffects(requiredKeyword, gs.EffectManager) {
			continue
		}
		if keyword == Toxic {
			c.ToxicN += value
		}
		gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
			Source:            source,
			SourceID:          source.ID,
			AffectsSpecific:   c.ID,
			GrantKeyword:      keyword,
			GrantKeywordValue: value,
			UntilEOT:          true,
		})
	}
}

// buildGrantKeywordPlusDynamicPTEffect implements the
// grant_keyword_plus_dynamic_pt_until_eot primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-grant-keyword-plus-dynamic-pt-until-eot-bdb265d199, Skittering
// Cicada class): "Whenever you cast a colorless spell, until end of turn,
// this creature gains trample and gets +X/+X, where X is that spell's mana
// value." Distinct from grant_keyword_eot (keyword only, no P/T) and the
// existing power_from=referent_spell_mana_value pump (asymmetric +X/+0, no
// keyword): the keyword and a SYMMETRIC +X/+X ride together on ONE
// ContinuousEffect so both expire together at end of turn. X reuses the
// same event_subject_mana_value dynamic-amount spec already evaluated by
// EvalDynamicAmountFull elsewhere, resolved fresh on every call so repeated
// triggers each get their own independent effect instead of overwriting a
// shared one (0 is a legal resolved amount and still yields a grant, just
// with a +0/+0 bonus).
func (gs *GameState) buildGrantKeywordPlusDynamicPTEffect(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, eventCard *Card, targets []Target) *ContinuousEffect {
	kwStr := getStringValue(effectValue, "keyword", "")
	if kwStr == "" || source == nil {
		return nil
	}
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0)
	if spec, ok := effectValue["amount_count"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {
		if x, resolved := gs.EvalDynamicAmountFull(spec, controller, source, eventCard, targets); resolved {
			amount = x
		}
	}
	return &ContinuousEffect{Source: source, SourceID: source.ID, AffectsSpecific: source.ID, GrantKeyword: Keyword(kwStr), PowerMod: amount, ToughnessMod: amount, UntilEOT: true}
}

// ExecuteAbilityEffect executes a triggered/activated ability effect
// Returns an error if the effect cannot be executed
func (gs *GameState) executeSelfCopyOnCastTokenEffect(source *Card, controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	if source == nil {
		return nil
	}
	count := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 1)
	if count < 1 {
		count = 1
	}
	for i := 0; i < count; i++ {
		token := cloneToken(source, controller)
		token.IsToken = true
		gs.Stack.Push(&StackObject{
			Card:       token,
			Controller: controller,
			IsCopy:     true,
		})
	}
	return nil
}

func (gs *GameState) ExecuteAbilityEffect(stackObj *StackObject) error {
	if stackObj == nil || !stackObj.IsAbility {
		return nil
	}

	// event_amount count spec (ticket #3730): the resolving object's own
	// stamped event magnitude (StackObject.EventAmount, set by
	// stampEventCard) is exposed to EvalDynamicAmountFull via a
	// GameState-scoped field for the duration of this call — the spec
	// resolves to the triggering event's numeric magnitude ("put that
	// many"). Saved/restored so nested effect execution (a sequence atom
	// resolving another ability) can't read a stale magnitude.
	savedEventAmount := gs.eventAmountStamped
	if stackObj.HasEventAmount {
		gs.eventAmountStamped = stackObj.EventAmount
	} else {
		gs.eventAmountStamped = 0
	}
	defer func() { gs.eventAmountStamped = savedEventAmount }()

	effect := stackObj.Text
	source := stackObj.Card
	effectValue := stackObj.EffectValue
	targets := stackObj.Targets
	if effect == "destroy_up_to_counter_count" {
		return gs.executeDestroyUpToCounterCountTargets(source, targets, effectValue)
	}
	// mass_destroy_attack_relative_untapped_continuous_control_filter (Total
	// War class): connects ResolveTriggerReferentPlayerController (already
	// present, only unit-tested, never called from here) into the ability's
	// effective controller, so effectValue["controller_ref"] ==
	// "trigger_referent_player" resolves to the triggering event's stamped
	// player (stackObj.EventPlayer, the ATTACKING player for an "attacks"
	// event with Controller:"any") instead of the ability's own controller.
	// A no-op for every other card: absent controller_ref returns
	// stackObj.Controller unchanged.
	eventPlayer := -1
	if stackObj.HasEventPlayer {
		eventPlayer = stackObj.EventPlayer
	}
	controller := gs.ResolveTriggerReferentPlayerController(effectValue, stackObj.Controller, eventPlayer)

	// destroy_all_matching_trigger_spell_mana_value implements Celestial
	// Kirin's dynamic referent: all permanents whose mana value equals the
	// triggering spell's mana value.
	if effect == "destroy_all_matching_trigger_spell_mana_value" {
		if stackObj.EventCard == nil {
			return nil
		}
		filter := "permanent_mv_eq_" + strconv.Itoa(stackObj.EventCard.ManaCost.CMC())
		return gs.destroyAllMatching(controller, filter, "", false, "", false, "")
	}

	if effect == "exile_target_graveyard_card_and_named_matches" {
		return gs.executeExileTargetGraveyardCardAndNamedMatches(targets)
	}

	// tap_untap_shared_creature_type_referent primitive (Faces of the
	// Past class): the referent is the triggering event's subject
	// (stackObj.EventCard), never an explicit target, so this dispatches
	// straight to the battlefield-wide sweep and returns before the
	// normal effect switch below, the same early-fold shape
	// resolveModalChoiceEffect/resolveRecipientFromEventCard use above.
	if effect == "tap_untap_shared_creature_type_referent" {
		gs.executeTapUntapSharedCreatureTypeReferent(effectValue, stackObj.EventCard)
		return nil
	}

	// targeted_effect_dynamic_x_count primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-targeted-effect-dynamic-x-count-d629ddac55, Candelabra of
	// Tawnos class: "{X}, {T}: Untap X target lands."). X-target abilities
	// announce/pay their {X} activation cost BEFORE targets are chosen
	// (Rule 601.2b), so by the time this stack object resolves,
	// stackObj.Targets already holds exactly the X targets the activating
	// player chose — this executor's job is narrow: apply the effect to
	// every target it was handed, no fewer (a fixed single target) and no
	// more (a battlefield-wide sweep), unlike
	// executeTapUntapSharedCreatureTypeReferent above which deliberately
	// sweeps the whole battlefield for its own (non-targeted) primitive.
	if effect == "untap_dynamic_x_target_lands" {
		return gs.executeUntapDynamicXTargetLands(targets)
	}

	// conditional_pump_on_creature_type primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-conditional-pump-on-creature-type-87200f1a92, Kaseto,
	// Orochi Archmage class): "Target creature can't be blocked this turn.
	// If that creature is a Snake, it gets +2/+2 until end of turn." The
	// pump only applies to the SAME referent already targeted by the
	// ability's other (unconditional) mode — stackObj.Targets, unlike the
	// unconditional pump_until_eot case above — and only when that
	// referent actually has the printed creature type (Rule 205.3m subtype
	// check via Card.HasSubType), gated here rather than folded into
	// pump_until_eot so unconditional pumps stay untouched (negative
	// example above).
	if effect == "conditional_pump_on_creature_type" {
		return gs.executeConditionalPumpOnCreatureType(source, effectValue, targets)
	}

	// conditional_destroy primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-conditional-destroy-fad25c5f76): "destroy target
	// permanent unless <condition>." Dispatches before the dynamic-amount
	// resolvers below since it needs no X/count resolution of its own.
	if effect == "destroy_target_unless_life_at_least" {
		return gs.executeDestroyTargetUnlessLifeAtLeast(targets, effectValue)
	}

	// destroy_unless_damage_to_controller primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-destroy-unless-damage-to-controller-1b18eeccd8, Dwarven
	// Driller class): "Destroy target permanent unless its controller has ~
	// deal N damage to them." Dwarven Driller's "2 damage" is a fixed
	// amount, so this dispatches before the dynamic-amount resolvers below,
	// same as the sibling check just above.
	if effect == "destroy_unless_damage_to_controller" {
		return gs.executeDestroyUnlessDamageToController(targets, effectValue, source)
	}

	// destroy_unless_damage_equal_to_power primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-conditional-destroy-unless-damage-equal-to-power-81cf5fca55,
	// Combustion Man class): "Destroy target permanent unless its
	// controller has ~ deal damage to them equal to his power." Needs the
	// ability's own source card for its current power, so this dispatches
	// early like the sibling just above rather than through the generic
	// "destroy" case.
	if effect == "destroy_unless_damage_equal_to_power" {
		return gs.executeDestroyUnlessDamageEqualToPower(targets, source)
	}

	// destroy_target_blocking_source primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-destroy-target-blocking-source-8c68a1acdf, Knight of Dusk
	// at_end_of_combat (ticket
	// engine.auto-destroy-at-end-of-combat-timing-935c317094, Silent
	// Assassin class): "{3}{B}: Destroy target blocking creature at end of
	// combat." effectValue["at_end_of_combat"]==true defers the plain
	// destroy primitive's targets to the end of combat step (Rule 603.2)
	// instead of resolving on activation. Dispatches early, before the
	// generic "destroy" case reads cantBeRegenerated off this same
	// effectValue, so mass-destroy shapes (a different effect id, which
	// never sets this key) are unaffected.
	if effect == "destroy" {
		if atEndOfCombat, _ := effectValue["at_end_of_combat"].(bool); atEndOfCombat {
			return gs.executeDestroyAtEndOfCombat(targets, effectValue)
		}
	}
	// class): "Destroy target creature blocking this creature." Needs the
	// ability's own source (Rule 509.1h blocking state — Card.Blocking +
	// Card.BlockingCardID, set by combat.go's DeclareBlockers), so this
	// dispatches early rather than through the generic "destroy" case.
	if effect == "destroy_target_blocking_source" {
		return gs.executeDestroyTargetBlockingSource(source, targets)
	}

	// destroy_all_attached_to_target primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-destroy-all-attached-to-target-175ae9b2f8, Street Sweeper
	// class): "destroy all <subtype> attached to target <type>." The
	// controller chooses ONE target permanent of the named type, and every
	// permanent with the named subtype currently attached to THAT target is
	// destroyed — the destroyed set is scoped to the chosen target's
	// attachments, not the whole battlefield. Dispatches early (before the
	// generic "destroy" case) because it consumes a single chosen target
	// plus an attachment-scan, unlike the "all" mass-destroy branch.
	if effect == "destroy_all_attached_to_target" {
		return gs.executeDestroyAllAttachedToTarget(targets, effectValue)
	}

	// damage_amount_from_cards_in_hand_minus_constant primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-damage-amount-from-cards-in-hand-minus-constant-2fdd0137de,
	// Iron Maiden/Black Vise/The Rack class): "~ deals X damage to that
	// player, where X is the number of cards in their hand minus N." The
	// parser already emits this shape (patterns_triggers.go's Black Vise/
	// The Rack templates: amount_source=="hand_size_minus_n" + "minus"),
	// but nothing consumed it. This folds it, on a copied map, into the
	// SAME each_player_amount_count spec executeDamageEffect already
	// evaluates per target (target.PlayerIdx branch) — reading a "hand"
	// count spec with the damaged player as "you" — so the amount is
	// always computed against whichever player the damage actually lands
	// on, not a fixed controller/opponent relative to the ability's
	// controller.
	if src, ok := effectValue["amount_source"].(string); ok && src == "hand_size_minus_n" {
		merged := make(map[string]interface{}, len(effectValue)+1)
		for k, v := range effectValue {
			merged[k] = v
		}
		merged["each_player_amount_count"] = map[string]interface{}{
			"count": "hand",
			"whose": "you",
			"minus": getIntValue(effectValue, "minus", 0),
		}
		delete(merged, "amount_source")
		delete(merged, "minus")
		effectValue = merged
	}

	// dynamic "equal to the number of ..." amounts (round 6): resolve the
	// count spec into a plain amount before any executor reads it. Ev variant
	// (event-subject-stat round, 2026-08-03) threads stackObj.EventCard
	// through so "event_subject_power"/"event_subject_toughness" specs can
	// resolve against the triggering event's subject card; Full variant
	// (dynamic-amount round, 2026-08-08) also threads targets for
	// "stat_of_ref" ref=="target" specs.
	if spec, hasDynamicAmount := effectValue["amount_count"].(map[string]interface{}); hasDynamicAmount {
		if str(spec, "count") == "tapped_artifacts_controlled_by_source" {
			effectValue = gs.resolveTappedArtifactsAmount(effectValue, spec, controller)
		} else if str(spec, "count") == "sum_of_counts" {
			// sum_of_counts primitive (ticket
			// engine.auto-damage-dynamic-count-sum-08ec3cd385, Calamitous
			// Cave-In class): X is the SUM of two independent card counts
			// ("number of Caves you control plus number of Cave cards in
			// your graveyard"), resolved here instead of via the generic
			// single-count resolveDynamicAmountFull below.
			sum, resolved := gs.evalSumOfCountsSpec(spec, controller, source, stackObj.EventCard, targets)
			if !resolved {
				return nil
			}
			merged := make(map[string]interface{}, len(effectValue)+1)
			for k, v := range effectValue {
				merged[k] = v
			}
			merged["amount"] = sum
			delete(merged, "amount_count")
			effectValue = merged
		} else if _, resolved := gs.EvalDynamicAmountFull(spec, controller, source, stackObj.EventCard, targets); !resolved {
			return nil
		}
	}
	if ev, changed := gs.resolveDynamicAmountFull(effectValue, controller, source, stackObj.EventCard, targets); changed {
		effectValue = ev
	}
	// dynamic_x_amount primitive (ticket #3122, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:
	// damage / "deals X damage" family): resolve a printed "X" — bound to
	// either an announced {X} activation cost or a "where X is <count>"
	// clause — into a plain amount before the effect switch, exactly like
	// resolveDynamicAmountFull just above. Kept as its own small resolver
	// (lib_dynamic_x_amount.go) rather than folding into amount_count
	// directly: the activation_cost x_source has no existing count-spec
	// analog (EvalDynamicAmount has no access to GameState.activeXCostValue).
	_, hadXAmountSpec := effectValue["x_amount_count"].(map[string]interface{})
	effectValue = gs.resolveXAmount(effectValue, controller, source)
	if hadXAmountSpec {
		if _, resolved := effectValue["amount"]; !resolved {
			return nil
		}
	}
	// ability_effect_amount_from primitive (ticket #3047, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:? / "deals X damage" ability family): a printed "X"
	// bound to the ability/spell's own announced {X} reaches the ability
	// executor as EffectValue["amount_from"]="x_value" — the parser
	// patterns' mirror of the spell-path SpellEffect.AmountFrom struct
	// field. The spell path folds that field via resolveSpellAmount
	// (gamestate.go); the ability-effect path had no analog, so a
	// triggered/activated "deals X damage" (Javelineer, Auntie Blyte,
	// The Rollercrusher Ride's ETB) resolved to executeDamageEffect's
	// hardcoded default (1) instead of X. resolveAmountFrom folds
	// amount_from into a plain "amount" here, exactly like resolveXAmount
	// just above, so executeDamageEffect reads X unchanged. Supported
	// sources match resolveSpellAmount: "x_value" (announced {X}) and
	// "energy_x" (variable {E} paid).
	effectValue = gs.resolveAmountFrom(effectValue)
	// put_counters_amount_distinct_named_lands_controlled primitive
	// (ticket engine.auto-put-counters-amount-distinct-named-lands-
	// control-39ce3d3d97, Emil, Vastlands Roamer class): fold a dynamic X
	// for put_counters — the number of distinct land names the
	// controller controls — into a plain "amount", at the SAME
	// before-the-switch chokepoint as every other amount fold above. See
	// resolvePutCountersDistinctNamedLandsAmount (dynamic_amount.go).
	effectValue = gs.resolvePutCountersDistinctNamedLandsAmount(effect, effectValue, controller)
	// damage_recipient_referent_controller primitive (ticket
	// auto-damage-recipient-referent-controller-a4c9e63abb, Magnetic Mine
	// class): fold a fixed (non-targeted) recipient — the controller of
	// the permanent the trigger condition itself matched — into targets
	// before the effect switch runs, exactly like the other EffectValue
	// folds above.
	targets = gs.resolveRecipientFromEventCard(effectValue, stackObj.EventCard, targets)
	// damage_equal_to_toughness_to_controller primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-damage-equal-to-toughness-6597113781, Map-validated
	// capability damage_equal_to_toughness — Creature Bond class: "When
	// enchanted creature dies, this Aura deals damage equal to that
	// creature's toughness to the creature's controller."). Handled here,
	// before the main effect switch, exactly like
	// prevent_damage_to_self_this_turn just below: stackObj.EventCard (the
	// creature the "dies" trigger fired for) supplies BOTH the damage
	// amount (its own toughness) and the recipient (its own controller),
	// so this primitive needs no externally chosen target at all.
	if effect == "damage_equal_to_toughness_to_controller" {
		return gs.executeDamageEqualToToughnessToControllerEffect(stackObj.EventCard)
	}
	// destroy_target_then_gain_life primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-destroy-target-then-gain-life-ae43167ca6, Vengeant
	// Vampire class: "When this creature dies, destroy target creature an
	// opponent controls and you gain 4 life."). The compound clause is a
	// single triggered effect that must apply BOTH sub-effects without
	// dropping either: destroy the chosen target via the SAME
	// DestroyTarget primitive every other single-target destroy uses, then
	// grant the ability's controller a fixed life amount via GainLife.
	// Handled here, before the main effect switch, exactly like the
	// damage_equal_to_toughness_to_controller check just above — a bare
	// early return leaves every other effect's dispatch untouched, so a
	// plain "destroy" (no life clause) keeps using the existing
	// single-target destroy path unchanged and a plain life-gain trigger
	// never reaches this executor.
	if effect == "destroy_target_then_gain_life" {
		return gs.executeDestroyTargetThenGainLifeEffect(targets, effectValue, controller)
	}
	// prevent_damage_to_self_this_turn primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-prevent-damage-to-self-this-turn-d61890a68b, Favored
	// Hoplite class: "put a +1/+1 counter on this creature and prevent all
	// damage that would be dealt to it this turn"): registers an
	// UNLIMITED, UntilEOT damage-prevention shield scoped to the ability's
	// own source permanent via AffectsCardID (the SAME recipient-keyed
	// field PreventDamageWithContext already checks, replacement.go),
	// with CombatOnly/NoncombatOnly left unset so it blocks EVERY kind of
	// damage — combat and noncombat alike — dealt to that one permanent
	// for the rest of the turn. Independent of and in addition to any
	// put_counters atom resolving earlier in the same effect sequence
	// (Favored Hoplite's Heroic trigger fires both from one paragraph).
	// Handled here, before the main effect switch, since this primitive
	// needs no target resolution of its own — a bare early return leaves
	// every other effect's dispatch untouched.
	if effect == "prevent_damage_to_self_this_turn" {
		if gs.ReplacementManager != nil && source != nil {
			gs.ReplacementManager.Register(&ReplacementEffect{
				Type:          ReplacementDamagePrevention,
				AffectsCardID: source.ID,
				Unlimited:     true,
				UntilEOT:      true,
			})
		}
		return nil
	}
	// sacrificed_permanent_stat_damage_amount primitive (ticket #3493,
	// REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:damage / "deals damage equal to ... the
	// sacrificed permanent's power/mana value" — Abyssal Hunter/Barrage
	// Tyrant/Broadside Bombardiers class): fold a sacrificed permanent's
	// power or mana value into the damage effect's "amount" field, the same
	// way resolveAmountFrom just above folds a printed X. Kept as its own
	// small resolver (lib_damage_sacrificed_stat_amount.go) since the
	// existing resolvePumpSacrificedXSource only feeds pump's power/
	// toughness, never damage's amount field.
	effectValue = gs.resolveDamageSacrificedStatAmount(effectValue)
	// modal_choice_effect primitive (ticket engine.auto-modal-choice-effect-
	// e1db712621, REPARSE-MAP: two effect clauses joined by "or" in the
	// Oracle text — Lava Storm class): swap in the caster-chosen clause's
	// effect/value wholesale before dispatch, so only that clause's
	// executor runs below. See resolveModalChoiceEffect above.
	if newEffect, newValue, changed := gs.resolveModalChoiceEffect(effect, effectValue); changed {
		effect = newEffect
		effectValue = newValue
	}
	// pump_dynamic_x_source primitive (ticket #3141, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:pump / "target creature gets +X/+X ... where X is
	// <count>" family): pump's magnitude lives in EffectValue["power"]/
	// ["toughness"] (read by executePumpEffect), never "amount" —
	// resolveXAmount's fold target just above — so a dynamic X for a pump
	// needs its own small resolver rather than reusing resolveXAmount
	// directly. Wraps the SAME closed, already-registered count-spec
	// vocabulary EvalDynamicAmount understands (game/dynamic_amount.go); no
	// new count kinds are added here. Aspect of Hydra's "devotion to green"
	// and Accelerated Mutation's "greatest mana value among permanents you
	// control" stay honest misses (no registered count spec computes
	// either yet) — this primitive only wires the fold-into-pump mechanism
	// itself, matching the "smallest change that delivers the primitive"
	// scope of this round.
	effectValue = gs.resolvePumpDynamicXSource(effectValue, controller, source)
	// pump_amount_equals_source_power primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-pump-amount-equals-source-power-b4cd8950c2, Syr Faren, the
	// Hengehammer class: "another target attacking creature gets +X/+X until
	// end of turn, where X is Syr Faren's power."). The dynamic X here is the
	// ABILITY'S OWN SOURCE permanent's live power at resolution, not a fixed
	// literal. Reached at the SAME before-the-switch chokepoint as the pump
	// resolvers above, keyed by EffectValue["pump_amount_equals_source_power"]
	// (closed, single bool value). Reads source.GetPowerWithEffects directly
	// rather than routing through resolvePumpDynamicXSource's
	// EvalDynamicAmount spec path, since the source_power count spec already
	// exists in dynamic_amount.go but the converter for this card family
	// emits a flat bool marker rather than a count-spec map. Fixed-literal
	// pump effects (no marker) are untouched — the marker is absent, so this
	// fold is a no-op.
	if marker, _ := effectValue["pump_amount_equals_source_power"].(bool); marker && source != nil {
		x := source.GetPowerWithEffects(gs.EffectManager)
		out := make(map[string]interface{}, len(effectValue)+2)
		for k, v := range effectValue {
			out[k] = v
		}
		out["power"] = float64(x)
		out["toughness"] = float64(x)
		effectValue = out
	}
	// pump_x_source_asym primitive (ticket #3143, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:
	// pump / "~ gets +X/+0 until end of turn, where X is <count>" family):
	// resolvePumpDynamicXSource just above only folds a dynamic X onto
	// BOTH power and toughness ("+X/+X"); this sibling folds two
	// independent optional specs onto power/toughness separately, so a
	// printed asymmetric "+X/+0" (or "+0/+X") resolves correctly instead
	// of pumping toughness by X too. Same before-the-switch chokepoint, so
	// it reaches pump_all's mass "creatures you control" atoms the same
	// way pump_x_source already does (lib_pump_asymmetric_x_source.go).
	effectValue = gs.resolvePumpAsymmetricXSource(effectValue, controller, source)
	// pump_referent_dynamic_amount_until_eot primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-pump-referent-dynamic-amount-until-eot-b195cfdc04,
	// Erratic Cyclops class: "Whenever you cast an instant or sorcery
	// spell, this creature gets +X/+0 until end of turn, where X is that
	// spell's mana value."). Reached at the SAME before-the-switch
	// chokepoint as the pump resolvers above. Unlike
	// resolvePumpDynamicXSource/resolvePumpAsymmetricXSource (which read a
	// closed dynamic_amount.go count spec with no access to the
	// triggering event), this primitive's X is the mana value of the
	// triggering CAST SPELL itself — stackObj.EventCard, the same referent
	// field resolveRecipientFromEventCard reads for damage recipients —
	// so it needs its own small fold rather than reusing either. Keyed by
	// EffectValue["power_from"]=="referent_spell_mana_value" (closed,
	// single value); sets power to that spell's mana value and points
	// targets at the source permanent itself — the "this creature"
	// referent — no externally chosen target exists for this trigger.
	effectValue, targets = gs.resolvePumpReferentSpellManaValue(effectValue, targets, stackObj.EventCard, source)
	// amount_source_spell_mana_value primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-amount-source-spell-mana-value-497cd8b198, Livaan,
	// Cultist of Tiamat class: "Whenever you cast a noncreature spell,
	// target creature gets +X/+0 until end of turn, where X is that
	// spell's mana value."). Unlike resolvePumpReferentSpellManaValue just
	// above (whose Oracle text never says "target" and always redirects
	// the pump onto source itself), this variant's Oracle text says TARGET
	// creature — a player-chosen target distinct from source — so targets
	// must be left exactly as already resolved by targeting.go instead of
	// being redirected. Reached at the SAME before-the-switch chokepoint.
	effectValue = gs.resolvePumpTargetedSpellManaValue(effectValue, stackObj.EventCard)
	// pump_attached_creature_no_target primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-pump-attached-creature-no-target-6c04ae9e5b): reached at
	// the SAME before-the-switch chokepoint as the pump resolvers just
	// above — see resolvePumpAttachedCreatureNoTarget's doc comment.
	targets = gs.resolvePumpAttachedCreatureNoTarget(effect, targets, source)
	// same_name_as_target_filter primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-same-name-as-target-filter-ba6327bdcd, Echoing Courage
	// class): reached at the SAME before-the-switch chokepoint as the pump
	// resolvers above — see resolveSameNameAsTargetFilter's doc comment.
	targets = gs.resolveSameNameAsTargetFilter(effect, effectValue, targets)
	// filter_attacking_same_defender_as_source primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-filter-attacking-same-defender-as-source-678c551aed):
	// reached at the SAME before-the-switch chokepoint as the resolvers
	// above — see resolveAttackingSameDefenderAsSourceFilter's doc comment.
	targets = gs.resolveAttackingSameDefenderAsSourceFilter(source, effectValue, targets)
	// filter_attacking_creature_without_keyword primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-filter-attacking-creature-without-keyword-61973916d4):
	// reached at the SAME before-the-switch chokepoint as the resolvers
	// above — see resolveAttackingCreatureWithoutKeywordFilter's doc comment.
	targets = gs.resolveAttackingCreatureWithoutKeywordFilter(effectValue, targets)
	// pay_exile_cost_from_zone primitive (ticket #3214, REPARSE-MAP
	// cost_unmapped:exile / "Exile a creature card from your graveyard: ..."
	// family, e.g. Woodwraith Strangler, Ashnod Flesh Mechanist, Littjara
	// Glade-Warden): the registered "exile" effect executes a targeted
	// battlefield-removal effect (Rule 701.15a), not a non-targeted cost
	// paid from hand/graveyard. Reaches the SAME before-the-switch
	// chokepoint as every resolver above; keyed by
	// EffectValue["exile_cost_from_zone"] ("hand"|"graveyard") and optional
	// EffectValue["exile_cost_subtype"] (a card subtype filter; empty = any
	// card). No-ops (returns true) when the key is absent, so every other
	// ability is unaffected. If the announced cost can't be paid, the
	// ability fizzles instead of resolving its effect.
	if !gs.payExileCostFromZone(effectValue, controller) {
		return nil
	}
	// pump_x_source_sacrificed primitive (ticket #3144, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:pump / "Sacrifice a[n] <type> creature: ~ gets +X/+X
	// until end of turn, where X is the sacrificed creature's power" family:
	// Atogatog). completeChoiceActivation (gamestate_choice.go) already
	// folds a paid filtered-Sacrifice cost's power into a per-activation
	// EffectValue["sacrificed_power"], but nothing read it back out for a
	// pump effect: resolvePumpDynamicXSource's "pump_x_source" spec is
	// evaluated by EvalDynamicAmount (dynamic_amount.go), which has no
	// access to the raw EffectValue map and so no way to see a sacrifice
	// made to pay THIS SAME activation's cost. No new count-spec kind is
	// added — this is a narrower, EffectValue-local fold, not a general
	// count source (Chameleon Colossus's "X is its power" already resolves
	// via the registered source_power/stat_of_ref count specs; that is a
	// table-tier mapping issue, not an engine gap).
	effectValue = gs.resolvePumpSacrificedXSource(effectValue)
	// dynamic_token_count_and_pt primitive (ticket #3128, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:create_token / "create a tapped <N> ... token" family):
	// create_token's executor (executeTokenEffect) reads plain literal
	// "count"/"power"/"toughness" fields off effectValue (see the
	// token_create_target_controlled case above, which stamps "count" from
	// source.Counters.PlusOnePlusOne before calling executeTokenEffect) —
	// there was no fold for a dynamic count/PT spec (cost-paid X, per-event
	// count, sacrifice-derived total). Mirrors resolvePumpDynamicXSource's
	// chokepoint placement exactly, wrapping the SAME closed count-spec
	// vocabulary EvalDynamicAmount understands; no new count kinds are
	// added here (lib_dynamic_token_count_and_pt.go).
	effectValue = gs.resolveTokenDynamicCountAndPT(effectValue, controller, source)
	// create_token_custom_spec primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-create-token-custom-spec-e9e91642b6, Forbidden Friendship
	// class: "Create a 1/1 red Dinosaur creature token with haste and a 1/1
	// white Human Soldier creature token."): the legacy create_token switch
	// below only understands a predefined "gold" token_type plus a "tapped"
	// flag, with no way to specify an arbitrary power/toughness, color(s),
	// creature subtype(s), or keyword ability. Reached at the SAME
	// before-the-switch chokepoint as the resolvers above, keyed by a new
	// EffectValue["token_spec"] map (closed shape: name, power, toughness,
	// subtypes, colors, keywords) so the untouched "gold"/tapped path (and
	// its existing tests) keeps flowing straight into the old switch when
	// token_spec is absent.
	if (effect == "create_token" || effect == "token") && effectValue["token_spec"] != nil {
		gs.executeCreateTokenCustomSpec(effectValue, controller)
		return nil
	}
	// create_choice_token primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-create-choice-token-856a4c6872, Ant-Man's Army class:
	// "create a Food token or a Treasure token"). Reached at the SAME
	// before-the-switch chokepoint as the resolvers above, keyed by a new
	// EffectValue["token_type_choices"] ([]string of predefined token_type
	// names); resolves the choice down to a single token_type and falls
	// through the existing create_token/buildPredefinedToken path. A
	// single fixed token_type clause (no choices list) is untouched.
	if (effect == "create_token" || effect == "token") && effectValue["token_type_choices"] != nil {
		return gs.executeTokenChoiceEffect(controller, effectValue)
	}
	// prevent_next_n_damage_to_target primitive (ticket #3438, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:? / "prevent the next N damage that would be dealt to
	// TARGET creature this turn" family — Abuna Acolyte, Acolyte's Reward,
	// Alabaster Wall). Short-circuits before the main effect switch below
	// (same before-dispatch chokepoint as the resolvers above) since this
	// effect needs no further amount-folding and just needs to register a
	// metered ReplacementManager shield on each targeted creature, mirroring
	// cardfns.PreventNextDamageToCreature (lib_prevent_damage.go, used by
	// Eiganjo Castle) but reachable from the declarative
	// converter/ability_effects.go dispatch instead of a hand-authored
	// CardHandler. Scoped to creature targets only (the confirmed
	// Target.IsCard/CardID fields this file already relies on elsewhere);
	// a player-target ("any target" resolving to a player) branch is left
	// as a follow-up rather than guessed here.
	if effect == "prevent_next_n_damage_to_target" {
		return gs.executePreventNextNDamageToTarget(effectValue, targets)
	}
	// sacrifice_choose_keep_rest primitive (ticket #3194, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:sacrifice / "chooses up to N [permanents] they control,
	// then sacrifices the rest" family — Archfiend of Depravity, Covetous
	// Elegy class): opens the new "sacrifice_keep_rest" PendingChoice
	// (StartSacrificeKeepRestChoice, sacrifice_keep_rest.go) letting the
	// controller keep up to N matching permanents; every other matching
	// permanent is sacrificed once the choice resolves. Narrower than
	// executeEdictSacrifice (edict_sacrifice.go), which sacrifices exactly
	// the CHOSEN permanents rather than the complement of a kept subset.
	if effect == "sacrifice_choose_keep_rest" {
		filterStr, _ := effectValue["filter"].(string)
		n := getIntValue(effectValue, "n", 0)
		var filter func(*Card) bool
		if filterStr != "" {
			filter = func(c *Card) bool { return matchesPermanentFilter(c, filterStr) }
		}
		sourceName := ""
		if source != nil {
			sourceName = source.Name
		}
		gs.StartSacrificeKeepRestChoice(controller, n, filter, sourceName)
		return nil
	}
	// regenerate_target primitive (ticket #3242, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:
	// regenerate / "Regenerate target X" family — Accursed Duneyard,
	// Lim-Dûl the Necromancer, Yavimaya Hollow class): the registered
	// "regenerate" verb (registry_regenerate.go) is self-only because
	// GameState.Regenerate(controllerIdx, cardID) requires controllerIdx to
	// match the card's actual controller — correct for "Regenerate ~" but
	// wrong for a true "Regenerate target creature", which may target ANY
	// creature regardless of who controls it. Mirrors the idiom already
	// used by hand-written handlers for this exact shape (Crypt Sliver,
	// Ezuri Renegade Leader): pass the TARGET's own controller into
	// Regenerate, satisfying its acting-player-controls-it check trivially
	// without touching Regenerate itself or its tested self-only
	// invariants. Untargeted/vanished target (Rule 608.2b): no-op.
	if effect == "regenerate_target" {
		if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard {
			return nil
		}
		card := gs.findRegenerateTargetCard(targets[0].CardID)
		if card == nil {
			return nil
		}
		gs.Regenerate(card.Controller, card.ID)
		return nil
	}
	// tap_target_creatures primitive (ticket #3184, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:
	// tap / "Tap up to N target creatures" family — Archipelagore, A Good
	// Day to Pie, Adverse Conditions, Crashing Wave class): no v2 effect
	// previously mutated a targeted permanent's tapped state (registry.go
	// only had the read-side becomes_tapped trigger and the cost-only
	// tap_for_mana event). "Up to N" cardinality is a targeting concern —
	// fewer targets may legally be chosen than the printed max — so the
	// executor just taps whichever targets were actually supplied, mirroring
	// regenerate_target's per-target battlefield lookup just above. Reused
	// unchanged by the v2 spell-effect-sequence walker (same chokepoint
	// "destroy"/"regenerate_target" already share), so this covers both a
	// triggered/activated "tap target creature" ability AND a sorcery/instant
	// like "Tap up to two target creatures."
	if effect == "tap_target_creatures" {
		for _, tgt := range targets {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tgt.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			card.Tapped = true
		}
		return nil
	}
	// tap_target_permanent primitive (ticket #3745, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:tap / "Tap target Wall." family — Ali Baba class): the
	// type-agnostic sibling of tap_target_creatures just above. The legacy
	// "tap"/"tap_target" switch case further down already taps any supplied
	// target permanent, but the reparse converter only emitted it for the
	// patternTapTargetPermanent regex (artifact/creature/permanent), so a
	// "Tap target Wall." (or any other single card-type) line stayed a
	// verb_unmapped:tap miss. Same per-target battlefield lookup, no
	// controller check, and — unlike tap_target_creatures' bare field-set —
	// the full card.Tap() + EventTapped/EventBecomesTapped semantics of
	// executeTapEffect, so becomes_tapped triggers (e.g. on the Wall
	// itself) fire exactly as they do for the legacy "tap" effect. The
	// creature-only tap_target_creatures primitive is left untouched.
	if effect == "tap_target_permanent" {
		for _, tgt := range targets {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tgt.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			card.Tap()
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	// untap_target_permanent primitive (ticket #3068, REPARSE-MAP
	// static_unmapped: "untap target creature" family — Aim High, Act of
	// Heroism, Gerrard's Command, Gift of Growth class): the mirror image of
	// tap_target_creatures just above — no v2 effect previously mutated a
	// targeted permanent's tapped state to UNtapped (the three
	// knowledge-service hits — HighAlert, StaffofDomination,
	// GreatOakGuardian — all hand-rolled it per-card instead). Same
	// per-target battlefield lookup, no controller check (untapping a
	// permanent you don't control is a legal, if unusual, effect — e.g.
	// Twiddle-style cards). Reused unchanged by the v2
	// spell-effect-sequence walker, same chokepoint tap_target_creatures
	// shares.
	if effect == "untap_target_permanent" {
		for _, tgt := range targets {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tgt.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			card.Tapped = false
		}
		return nil
	}
	// referent_doesnt_untap_next_step primitive (ticket #3066, REPARSE-MAP
	// static_unmapped: "Those creatures don't untap during their
	// controller's next untap step" family — Chilling Grasp class): the
	// referent ("those creatures") is the same spell/ability's own target
	// list, already sitting in stackObj.Targets by the time this second
	// effect atom in a sequence resolves — the exact shared-targets shape
	// tap_target_creatures documents above (same v2 spell-effect-sequence
	// walker chokepoint). Rides Card.SkipNextUntap, the existing exert-
	// mechanic flag (Rule 701.39), consumed and cleared by Zone.UntapAll —
	// no new state needed; several cards (Sleep, ConeofCold, JunkWinder,
	// KefnetsMonument) already hand-roll this exact flag-set per-card, this
	// just gives the tap_target_creatures target list a registered v2
	// executor for it. No controller check, matching
	// untap_target_permanent's stance just above.
	if effect == "referent_doesnt_untap_next_step" {
		for _, tgt := range targets {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tgt.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			card.SkipNextUntap = true
		}
		return nil
	}
	// skip_next_untap_step primitive (ticket #3458, REPARSE-MAP
	// static_unmapped: "It doesn't untap during its controller's next untap
	// step." family — Chill of the Grave, Crippling Chill, Ojutai's Breath,
	// Press for Answers class). Reuses the existing Card.SkipNextUntap field
	// (card.go, Exert — Rule 701.39) and its sole consumer, Zone.UntapAll
	// (zone.go): that field already means exactly "skip THIS permanent's
	// next untap, then clear," one permanent at a time, independent of
	// every other permanent its controller untaps that same step —
	// precisely the per-permanent (not per-player) semantics this family
	// needs, unlike the per-PLAYER SkipNextUntapStep counter
	// (cardfns/lib_skip_untap_step.go, [VOCAB] #1620). No new engine
	// state — just a new name to set the field from a targeted effect,
	// mirroring tap_target_creatures/untap_target_permanent's per-target
	// battlefield-lookup shape immediately above.
	if effect == "skip_next_untap_step" {
		for _, tgt := range targets {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tgt.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			card.SkipNextUntap = true
		}
		return nil
	}
	// tap_referent_doesnt_untap primitive (ticket engine.auto-prevent-untap-
	// until-next-untap, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:tap / "tap that creature
	// and it doesn't untap during its controller's next untap step" family
	// -- Kashi-Tribe Reaver class): the referent ("that creature") is the
	// same triggered ability's own target, already in stackObj.Targets. The
	// plain "tap"/"tap_target" mapping drops the follow-up clause and the
	// bare skip_next_untap_step/referent_doesnt_untap_next_step atoms drop
	// the tap, so this is the single PAIRED atom: it taps each supplied
	// target with the full card.Tap() + EventTapped/EventBecomesTapped
	// semantics (mirroring executeTapEffect, so becomes_tapped triggers on
	// the damaged creature still fire) AND sets Card.SkipNextUntap on it --
	// the existing exert-mechanic flag (Rule 701.39) consumed one-shot by
	// Zone.UntapAll, so the effect is duration-bounded to the controller's
	// next untap step and self-clears. A bare "Tap target creature" (no
	// follow-up clause) still maps to plain "tap" and never reaches here.
	if effect == "tap_referent_doesnt_untap" {
		for _, tgt := range targets {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tgt.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			card.Tap()
			card.SkipNextUntap = true
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	// no_untap_next_step primitive (ticket engine.auto-no-untap-next-step-
	// 565337de28, Quiet Contemplation class): "tap target creature an
	// opponent controls and it doesn't untap during its controller's next
	// untap step." Same paired shape as tap_referent_doesnt_untap just
	// above (the referent is the ability's own already-resolved target) --
	// a distinct case name so future reparse rounds can address this exact
	// oracle family without disturbing tap_referent_doesnt_untap's own
	// callers. A bare "tap" with no untap-prevention clause is untouched
	// and keeps mapping to the plain "tap" case.
	if effect == "no_untap_next_step" {
		for _, tgt := range targets {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tgt.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			card.Tap()
			card.SkipNextUntap = true
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	// untap_event_subject primitive (ticket #2885, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:
	// untap / "Whenever a permanent you control enters tapped, untap it."
	// family — Amulet of Vigor class): the "it" here is the triggering
	// event's subject (the permanent that just entered tapped), not a
	// player-chosen target — untap_target_permanent just above reads
	// stackObj.Targets, which a non-targeted ETB trigger never populates.
	// Mirrors exile_self_on_death's direct EventCard binding further below
	// rather than the generic target_ref=="event_subject" rewrite later in
	// this function: that rewrite runs after this file's early-return effect
	// blocks, so it's unreachable for an effect matched by an earlier "if".
	// Vanished subject (Rule 608.2b): no event subject -> no-op.
	if effect == "untap_event_subject" {
		if stackObj.EventCard == nil {
			return nil
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(stackObj.EventCard.ID)
		if err != nil || card == nil {
			return nil
		}
		card.Tapped = false
		return nil
	}
	// search_named_card_multi_zone primitive (ticket #3038, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:? / "search your library and/or graveyard for a card
	// named X" family — Ajani's Aid, Angrath's Fury, Ashiok's Forerunner,
	// Basri's Aegis class): the registered tutor primitive's Filter is a
	// flat type/subtype string (SEARCH_FILTER_BLOCK excludes `\bnamed\b` as
	// needing runtime-state matching) and only ever targets the library, so
	// an exact-literal-name search across library AND graveyard needs its
	// own executor. effectValue["name"] carries the literal card name
	// (parser-supplied, out of scope here). No-op if unset or not found in
	// either zone; see searchNamedCardMultiZone for the shuffle rule.
	if effect == "search_named_card_multi_zone" {
		name, _ := effectValue["name"].(string)
		gs.searchNamedCardMultiZone(controller, name)
		return nil
	}
	// menace_counter_on_controlled_creature primitive: see
	// executeMenaceCounterOnControlledCreature.
	if effect == "menace_counter_on_controlled_creature" {
		return gs.executeMenaceCounterOnControlledCreature(controller)
	}
	// durable_self_exile primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-durable-self-exile-dd89277b18, Glorious Protector class):
	// "exile any number of <filter> permanents you control until this
	// creature leaves the battlefield." Delegates entirely to
	// executeDurableSelfExileEffect so the mass, filter-scoped exile plus
	// the StashedExileIDs return-on-leave wiring is unit-testable on its own.
	// effectValue["filter"] carries the same type-bucket string
	// matchesPermanentFilter understands (e.g. "nonangel_creature"); empty
	// means any permanent. No source (non-self-referential) or no matches:
	// no-op.
	if effect == "durable_self_exile" {
		filter := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "")))
		return gs.executeDurableSelfExileEffect(source, controller, filter)
	}
	// exile_target primitive (ticket #3077, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:exile
	// / "exile target creature/permanent" family): no generic registered v2
	// "exile" effect existed for a plain triggered/activated "Exile target
	// X" — only bespoke legacy V1 handlers (OblivionRing.go etc.) moved
	// cards to exile. GameState.ExileFromBattlefield (already used by
	// cardfns.ExilePermanent) takes a bare card ID, so — unlike
	// regenerate_target above — no controller-aware lookup is needed.
	// Untargeted/vanished target (Rule 608.2b): no-op.
	if effect == "exile_target" {
		if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard {
			return nil
		}
		gs.ExileFromBattlefield(targets[0].CardID)
		return nil
	}
	// exile_from_zone primitive (ticket #3082, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:exile
	// / "exile it with N <type> counter(s) on it" family — Epochrasite, Mari
	// the Killing Quill, Rex, Cyber-Hound class): "it" is a creature that
	// already died (the EventCard-bound trigger subject, exile_target_permanent's
	// sibling) or a chosen target (Rex's "choose target creature card in a
	// graveyard" — targets[0]); by the time this resolves the card sits in a
	// graveyard, not the battlefield, so exile_target just above (hardcoded to
	// gs.Battlefield) can't reach it. GameState.Exile (actions.go) already
	// accepts an arbitrary source zone — this primitive's only job is finding
	// WHICH player's graveyard currently holds the card, since a graveyard is
	// per-player. Optional counter_type/amount (executePutCounterEffect's own
	// field names) are applied after the move lands the card in exile. Card
	// not found in any graveyard (already moved on): no-op.
	if effect == "exile_from_zone" {
		var cardID string
		if stackObj.EventCard != nil {
			cardID = stackObj.EventCard.ID
		} else if len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard {
			cardID = targets[0].CardID
		}
		if cardID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		var zone *Zone
		var ownerIdx int
		for pi := range gs.Players {
			if _, err := gs.Players[pi].Graveyard.GetCard(cardID); err == nil {
				zone = gs.Players[pi].Graveyard
				ownerIdx = pi
				break
			}
		}
		if zone == nil {
			return nil
		}
		if err := gs.Exile(cardID, zone); err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		if counterType, ok := effectValue["counter_type"].(string); ok && counterType != "" {
			amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
			if amount < 1 {
				amount = 1
			}
			if card, err := gs.Players[ownerIdx].Exile.GetCard(cardID); err == nil {
				gs.AddCounters(card, CounterType(counterType), amount)
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	// exile_all_from_zone primitive (ticket #3714, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:exile / "exile all X cards from all graveyards" family —
	// Saprazzan Bailiff class): a mass sweep of a NAMED non-battlefield zone
	// (graveyard), distinct from the battlefield-only mass "exile" atom
	// (exileAllMatching, which never leaves the battlefield) and from the
	// single-card exile_from_zone/exile_with_counter primitives (which move
	// one auto-selected card, not a mass sweep). effectValue["filter"]
	// carries the same type-bucket string matchesPermanentFilter understands
	// (e.g. "artifact_and_enchantment"); effectValue["zone"] names the source
	// zone (only "graveyard" is supported — the one named non-battlefield
	// zone the family actually uses). Every matching card in every player's
	// graveyard is exiled to its owner's exile zone. No-op if no matches.
	if effect == "exile_all_from_zone" {
		filter := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "")))
		zone := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(getStringValue(effectValue, "zone", "graveyard")))
		if zone != "graveyard" {
			return nil
		}
		for _, p := range gs.Players {
			// Snapshot IDs; RemoveCard mutates the graveyard as it goes.
			var ids []string
			for _, c := range p.Graveyard.GetAll() {
				if c == nil {
					continue
				}
				if filter != "" && !matchesPermanentFilter(c, filter) {
					continue
				}
				ids = append(ids, c.ID)
			}
			for _, id := range ids {
				card, err := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(id)
				if err != nil {
					continue
				}
				p.Exile.AddCard(card)
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	// exile_all_from_library primitive (ticket engine.auto-exile-all-from-library-e631029fc3,
	// REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:exile / "exile all cards from your library"
	// family — Leveler class): a mass, non-targeted zone effect that empties
	// an ENTIRE library straight into its owner's exile zone, independent of
	// any battlefield target — distinct from exile_all_from_zone above,
	// which only ever touches graveyards and applies a filter.
	// effectValue["whose"] selects which player's library empties: "you"
	// (default, the ability's controller) or "opponent" (first other
	// player, mirroring the poison_counters "whose" convention in
	// dynamic_amount.go). IDs are snapshotted before removal, same as
	// exile_all_from_zone, since RemoveCard mutates the library as it
	// goes. No resolvable player: no-op.
	if effect == "exile_all_from_library" {
		whose := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(getStringValue(effectValue, "whose", "you")))
		var p *Player
		if whose == "opponent" {
			for i := range gs.Players {
				if i != controller {
					p = gs.Players[i]
					break
				}
			}
		} else if controller >= 0 && controller < len(gs.Players) {
			p = gs.Players[controller]
		}
		if p == nil {
			return nil
		}
		var ids []string
		for _, c := range p.Library.GetAll() {
			if c == nil {
				continue
			}
			ids = append(ids, c.ID)
		}
		for _, id := range ids {
			card, err := p.Library.RemoveCard(id)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			p.Exile.AddCard(card)
		}
		return nil
	}
	// self-exiles-on-death primitive (ticket #3078, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:exile / "exile it and X" dominant family — Ancient
	// Adamantoise, Body Snatcher, Gerrard Weatherlight Hero, Curse of
	// Clinging Webs class): the "it" in "exile it" on a dies trigger is
	// the dying creature — the triggering event's subject — which exiles
	// ITSELF. For a self-dies trigger (Adamantoise) the subject is the
	// source; for an external dies trigger (Curse of Clinging Webs) it's
	// the event subject while the source stays on the battlefield. The
	// architecture deliberately never maps referent→self for "exile it"
	// (unsafe), so this is a single safe registered verb that binds to
	// the event subject directly — no target_ref rewrite, no new zone
	// machinery. By resolution time the subject has already left the
	// battlefield for its owner's graveyard, so ExileFromBattlefield
	// would silently no-op; reuse executeExileEffect, whose graveyard
	// fallback (added for the event_subject dies-trigger case,
	// task-2363) exiles it from the graveyard. "exile all graveyards"
	// and "exile two target" (also in this ticket) stay honest misses —
	// they need their own mass/multi-target executors, out of scope for
	// this primitive. Vanished subject (Rule 608.2b): no event subject
	// -> no-op.
	if effect == "exile_self_on_death" {
		if stackObj.EventCard == nil {
			return nil
		}
		return gs.executeExileEffect([]Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: stackObj.EventCard.ID}}, -1)
	}
	// exile_with_counter primitive (ticket #3082, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:
	// exile / "exile it with a/N <kind> counter(s) on it" family —
	// Epochrasite, Mari the Killing Quill, Rex Cyber-Hound class): unlike
	// exile_self_on_death just above, the referent can ALSO be a
	// player-chosen target (Rex: "choose target creature card in a
	// graveyard. Exile it with a brain counter on it"), not only the
	// triggering event's subject — so this tries targets first and falls
	// back to stackObj.EventCard exactly like exile_self_on_death when none
	// were supplied (Mari/Epochrasite dies triggers). Reuses
	// executeExileEffect's battlefield-then-graveyard lookup via the new
	// executeExileWithCounterEffect so the counter lands on the SAME *Card
	// pointer that ends up in the exile zone. effectValue["counter_type"]
	// carries the counter kind ("time", "hit", "brain", ...);
	// effectValue["count"] the amount (default 1, e.g. Epochrasite's "three
	// time counters"). Trailing clauses this family also has (Epochrasite's
	// "and it gains suspend", Rex's "has all activated abilities of...")
	// stay honest misses — this primitive only covers the exile+counter
	// shape. Vanished referent and no-target/no-event-subject cases: no-op.
	if effect == "exile_with_counter" {
		ts := targets
		if len(ts) == 0 && stackObj.EventCard != nil {
			ts = []Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: stackObj.EventCard.ID}}
		}
		if len(ts) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		counterType := CounterType(getStringValue(effectValue, "counter_type", ""))
		amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 1)
		return gs.executeExileWithCounterEffect(ts, counterType, amount)
	}
	// exile_then_return primitive (ticket #3464, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:
	// exile / "Exile target creature ..., then return that card to the
	// battlefield under its owner's control" family — Acrobatic Maneuver
	// class): unlike cardfns.ExileUntilNextEndStep (lib_exile_with_delayed_
	// return.go), which defers the return to the next end step via a
	// delayed EventBus trigger, this shape returns the card to the
	// battlefield IMMEDIATELY as part of the same resolution (a one-shot
	// blink) — no subscription needed. Reuses the SAME
	// ExileFromBattlefield/ReturnFromExile zone-move primitives that
	// delayed-return helper already relies on. Resolves its own target
	// locally (explicit targets first, target_ref=="self" fallback, then
	// stackObj.EventCard) rather than depending on the generic target_ref
	// block later in this function, exactly like exile_with_counter just
	// above — this case sits before that block in the switch. Vanished
	// target (Rule 608.2b) and an already-moved target: no-op.
	if effect == "exile_then_return" {
		ts := targets
		if len(ts) == 0 {
			if ref, _ := effectValue["target_ref"].(string); ref == "self" && source != nil {
				ts = []Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: source.ID}}
			} else if stackObj.EventCard != nil {
				ts = []Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: stackObj.EventCard.ID}}
			}
		}
		for _, tgt := range ts {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tgt.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			owner := card.Owner
			if err := gs.ExileFromBattlefield(tgt.CardID); err != nil {
				continue
			}
			if err := gs.ReturnFromExile(owner, tgt.CardID, "battlefield"); err != nil {
				continue
			}
			if c, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tgt.CardID); err == nil && c != nil {
				c.Controller = owner
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	// exile_from_graveyard primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-exile-from-graveyard-f671ca6575, Kroxa and Kunoros
	// class): "exile five cards from your graveyard" as a standalone
	// effect — see executeExileFromGraveyardEffect above for the full
	// rationale. The returned exiled count isn't consumed here (no
	// "when you do" chaining primitive exists yet); this case only
	// performs the zone move itself.
	if effect == "exile_from_graveyard" {
		_, err := gs.executeExileFromGraveyardEffect(controller, effectValue)
		return err
	}
	// exile_target_cards_from_graveyard primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-exile-target-cards-from-graveyard-c71e283fcc,
	// REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:exile / "Exile N target cards from an
	// opponent's graveyard" family — Lochmere Serpent class): unlike
	// exile_self_from_zone below (a fixed, source-bound self-exile from a
	// NAMED zone) or executeExileEffect (battlefield-then-graveyard
	// fallback for a SINGLE resolved permanent/event-subject target),
	// this shape exiles MULTIPLE explicitly player-chosen targets that
	// were selected from a graveyard zone rather than the battlefield.
	// Target carries only a CardID (no zone/owner), so each target is
	// looked up across every player's graveyard and, when found, moved
	// directly into that SAME owner's exile zone — the battlefield is
	// never touched, so executeExileEffect's existing behavior for
	// battlefield permanents is unaffected. A vanished target (already
	// moved by an earlier atom in the same resolution, Rule 608.2b) is
	// skipped rather than failing the whole effect.
	if effect == "exile_target_cards_from_graveyard" {
		for _, tgt := range targets {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			for _, p := range gs.Players {
				card, err := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(tgt.CardID)
				if err != nil || card == nil {
					continue
				}
				p.Exile.AddCard(card)
				break
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	// exile_target_card_from_graveyard primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-exile-target-card-from-graveyard-d5c2a6e92f, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:exile / "exile up to one target card from a graveyard"
	// family — Immersturm Predator class): unlike
	// exile_target_cards_from_graveyard above (N explicitly chosen
	// graveyard targets, none of them optional), this shape's target is
	// OPTIONAL ("up to one") — the triggered ability may resolve with zero
	// targets chosen. Only the first IsCard target is honored; it is
	// looked up across every player's graveyard (any player's, per the
	// Oracle text) and moved into that same owner's exile zone, mirroring
	// exile_target_cards_from_graveyard's per-target lookup. A vanished
	// target (Rule 608.2b) or no target at all is a no-op.
	if effect == "exile_target_card_from_graveyard" {
		for _, tgt := range targets {
			if !tgt.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			for _, p := range gs.Players {
				card, err := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(tgt.CardID)
				if err != nil || card == nil {
					continue
				}
				p.Exile.AddCard(card)
				return nil
			}
			return nil
		}
		return nil
	}
	// exile_target_cards_single_graveyard primitive — see
	// executeExileTargetCardsSingleGraveyardEffect above for the full
	// rationale (Famished Ghoul: "Exile up to two target cards from a
	// single graveyard", ticket
	// engine.auto-exile-graveyard-card-target-ca4c0bd5ac). Delegates
	// entirely so the same-graveyard enforcement is unit-testable on its
	// own.
	if effect == "exile_target_cards_single_graveyard" {
		return gs.executeExileTargetCardsSingleGraveyardEffect(targets)
	}
	// exile_target_cards_from_event_player_graveyard primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-exile-cards-from-specific-player-graveyard-d4263a7f1f,
	// Skullsnatcher class) — see
	// executeExileTargetCardsFromEventPlayerGraveyardEffect above for the
	// full rationale. Delegates entirely so the event-player scoping is
	// unit-testable on its own.
	if effect == "exile_target_cards_from_event_player_graveyard" {
		return gs.executeExileTargetCardsFromEventPlayerGraveyardEffect(stackObj, targets)
	}
	// exile_self_from_zone primitive (ticket exile-self-from-zone-as-cost,
	// TicketSpec exile_self_from_zone_as_cost, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:
	// exile / "exile ~ from your graveyard" family — Kozilek's Return
	// class): unlike exile_self_on_death above (always binds to the dying
	// EVENT SUBJECT via stackObj.EventCard) or the generic
	// target_ref=="self" rewrite further below (only ever finds a
	// BATTLEFIELD permanent, via the existing executeExileEffect
	// primitive), this shape is a self-referential ("this card"/"~")
	// exile from an explicit NAMED non-battlefield zone
	// (effectValue["zone"]) on a triggered ability whose source itself is
	// sitting in that zone. Only "graveyard" is supported — the zone this
	// family actually names. Moves the source directly from that zone's
	// CardCollection into its owner's exile zone, the same
	// RemoveCard/AddCard move exile_all_from_zone above uses, so a
	// dependent atom in the same sequence ("this card deals 5 damage to
	// each creature") resolves after the move. Deliberately does NOT fall
	// back to the battlefield: an activated ability naming a battlefield
	// target, or a creature self-exiling off the battlefield, must keep
	// going through executeExileEffect unchanged. No source, wrong zone,
	// or source not actually present in that zone (e.g. an already-
	// resolved/declined optional exile): no-op.
	if effect == "exile_self_from_zone" {
		if source == nil {
			return nil
		}
		zone := strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(getStringValue(effectValue, "zone", "graveyard")))
		if zone != "graveyard" {
			return nil
		}
		owner := gs.Players[source.Owner]
		card, err := owner.Graveyard.RemoveCard(source.ID)
		if err != nil || card == nil {
			return nil
		}
		owner.Exile.AddCard(card)
		return nil
	}
	// multi_zone_named_card_search primitive (ticket #3039, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:? / "search your graveyard, hand and/or library for a
	// card named X ... put it onto/into your Y" family — Agency Outfitter,
	// Ajani's Aid, Angrath's Fury, Arachnus Spinner class): unlike
	// search_library_by_name (library-only source, hand-only destination,
	// unconditional post-search shuffle), this family searches a
	// per-card-configured set of zones for one or more named cards and
	// shuffles the library only if library was among the zones actually
	// searched. EffectValue carries "search_zones" ([]interface{} of
	// "graveyard"/"hand"/"library"), "card_names" ([]interface{} of exact
	// card names), and "destination" ("hand" or "battlefield").
	if effect == "multi_zone_named_card_search" {
		gs.executeMultiZoneNamedCardSearch(controller, effectValue)
		return nil
	}
	// self-referent resolution (verb_unmapped:? round, 2026-08-08): "it
	// gets +N/+N until end of turn for each X" (Butch DeLoria, Muxus,
	// Goblin Grandee class) — "it" is the triggered ability's own source,
	// not a player-chosen target. target_ref=="self" binds the atom's
	// target directly to source so the existing pump_until_eot executor
	// (targeted-only, see registry.go) applies unchanged.
	if ref, _ := effectValue["target_ref"].(string); ref == "self" && source != nil {
		ev := make(map[string]interface{}, len(effectValue))
		for k, v := range effectValue {
			ev[k] = v
		}
		delete(ev, "target_ref")
		effectValue = ev
		targets = []Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: source.ID}}
	}
	// Per-ability per-turn resolution cap (effect_once_per_turn_limiter
	// round, 2026-08-10): "Do this only once each turn" (Amzu, Swarm's
	// Hunger / Ancient Cornucopia / Calix, Guided by Fate / Deep Gnome
	// Terramancer class). effectValue["once_per_turn"]==true marks an
	// ability whose effect may resolve at most once per turn no matter how
	// many times its trigger condition matches; the actual per-turn state
	// lives on GameState (OncePerTurnGate) since it must persist across
	// repeat StackObject pushes for the same source this turn.
	if once, _ := effectValue["once_per_turn"].(bool); once && source != nil {
		if !gs.OncePerTurnGate(source.ID + "|" + effect) {
			return nil
		}
	}
	// referent resolution (round 7): target_ref=="event_subject" binds the
	// atom to the triggering event's subject card — it becomes an ordinary
