	// target for the target-consuming executors. Vanished subject (Rule
	// 608.2b): the effect does nothing.
	if ref, _ := effectValue["target_ref"].(string); ref == "event_subject" {
		ev := make(map[string]interface{}, len(effectValue))
		for k, v := range effectValue {
			ev[k] = v
		}
		delete(ev, "target_ref")
		effectValue = ev
		if stackObj.EventCard != nil {
			targets = []Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: stackObj.EventCard.ID}}
		} else {
			return nil
		}
	} else if ref == "event_player" {
		// event-player round (2026-08-03): "that player" is the player the
		// triggering event involved (Fate Unraveler's "whenever an opponent
		// draws a card, ~ deals 1 damage to THAT PLAYER"). No stamped player
		// (Rule 608.2b analog): the effect does nothing.
		ev := make(map[string]interface{}, len(effectValue))
		for k, v := range effectValue {
			ev[k] = v
		}
		delete(ev, "target_ref")
		effectValue = ev
		if stackObj.HasEventPlayer && stackObj.EventPlayer >= 0 && stackObj.EventPlayer < len(gs.Players) {
			targets = []Target{{IsCard: false, PlayerIdx: stackObj.EventPlayer}}
		} else {
			return nil
		}
	} else if ref == "event_subject_controller" {
		// "that creature's/land's controller" (Zo-Zu / Ankh of Mishra class):
		// the controller of the event's subject card.
		ev := make(map[string]interface{}, len(effectValue))
		for k, v := range effectValue {
			ev[k] = v
		}
		delete(ev, "target_ref")
		effectValue = ev
		if stackObj.EventCard != nil && stackObj.EventCard.Controller >= 0 && stackObj.EventCard.Controller < len(gs.Players) {
			targets = []Target{{IsCard: false, PlayerIdx: stackObj.EventCard.Controller}}
		} else {
			return nil
		}
	}

	// scratch-binding round (ticket-2827, "return it to" blink sub-shape):
	// "write_scratch_slot" stashes this atom's resolved single card target
	// into a named sequence scratch slot, generalizing the manifest_dread
	// producer/target_scratch_ref consumer pattern (game/effect_sequence.go)
	// to ANY target-consuming atom — "exile TARGET creature, then return it
	// to the battlefield..." (Distinguished Conjurer class) needs a LATER
	// atom to reference the SAME card THIS exile just exiled; unlike
	// self/event_subject referents, a freshly player-chosen target has no
	// other binding mechanism. Generic and effect-agnostic (checked before
	// the effect switch, exactly like the target_ref rewrites just above),
	// so any future target-consuming verb gets this for free.
	if slot, _ := effectValue["write_scratch_slot"].(string); slot != "" && len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard {
		if gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
			gs.seqSlotsStr = map[string]string{}
		}
		gs.seqSlotsStr[slot] = targets[0].CardID
	}

	switch effect {
	case "earthbend":
		// "Earthbend N." (TLA/TLE keyword action): target land you control
		// becomes a 0/0 haste creature (still a land), gets N +1/+1
		// counters, and returns tapped on death/exile. The land is an
		// ordinary player-chosen target (Rule 601.2c) — resolved the same
		// way any other targeted triggered/activated atom's target arrives
		// here, via stackObj.Targets.
		if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard {
			return nil
		}
		land, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(targets[0].CardID)
		if err != nil || land == nil || !land.IsLand() || land.Controller != controller {
			return nil
		}
		gs.EarthbendLand(source, controller, land, getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0))
		return nil
	case "end_turn":
		// Rule 500.4's "end the turn" keyword action, activated-ability
		// flavor (Obeka-style "{T}: The player whose turn it is may end
		// the turn"). Controller/targets are irrelevant — resolveEndTheTurn
		// always ends the CURRENT active player's turn, matching real
		// cards' "end the turn" wording (there is no active-player choice
		// baked into the keyword action itself; the "may" is provided by
		// the caller pushing this with StackObject.IsOptionalMay set).
		return gs.resolveEndTheTurn()
	case "double_counters":
		// verb_unmapped:? round (ticket #2629, widened parser-spine round
		// 2026-08-09): "double the number of +1/+1 counters on it/that
		// creature" now resolves against whatever battlefield card the
		// atom's target points at — source via target_ref=="self" (lines
		// 39-53 above), or an external card via target_ref=="event_subject"
		// (Aragorn, Hornburg Hero class: "whenever a renowned creature you
		// control deals combat damage..., double the number of +1/+1
		// counters on it" — the renowned creature is frequently NOT the
		// ability's own source, which the original source-only gate
		// silently no-op'd for).
		kind, _ := effectValue["kind"].(string)
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			if kind == "" || kind == "each" {
				card.Counters.DoubleCounters("")
			} else {
				card.Counters.DoubleCounters(CounterType(kind))
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "temporary_characteristic_overwrite_eot":
		return gs.executeTemporaryCharacteristicOverwriteEOTEffect(source, controller, targets)
	case "suspect":
		// verb_unmapped:kw_action round (ticket #3206): Rule 702.164a "Suspect
		// it" grants the target creature Menace and "can't block" for as long
		// as it remains on the battlefield — suspicion ends the instant the
		// permanent changes zones, so no leaves-battlefield revocation is
		// needed (a new object re-entering later is never suspected). Reuses
		// the same Keywords-append idiom cardfns.GrantCantAttack uses for
		// Defender and the CannotBlock field cardfns.GrantCantBlock sets;
		// idempotent like double_counters above.
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID)
			if err != nil || card == nil {
				continue
			}
			if !card.HasKeyword(Menace) {
				card.Keywords = append(card.Keywords, Menace)
			}
			card.CannotBlock = true
		}
		return nil
	case "put_battlefield_property_filter_match":
		// ticket #3220 (verb_unmapped:put_battlefield): "put a creature
		// card with mana value equal to the number of charge counters on
		// this artifact from your hand onto the battlefield" (Aether Vial
		// class). The dynamic mana-value target is resolved from amount_count
		// (counters_on_source/charge) into effectValue["amount"] by
		// resolveDynamicAmountFull before this switch; the property-filter
		// match itself is the additive "property" check in
		// executeReturnFromGraveyard's auto-select loop, so this case simply
		// delegates to that executor with source="hand"/destination="battlefield".
		return gs.executeReturnFromGraveyard(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "return_from_graveyard_multi_type":
		// ticket engine.auto-return-from-graveyard-multi-type-e59a25a846
		// (Crop Sigil / Delirium class): "Return up to one target
		// creature card and up to one target land card from your
		// graveyard to your hand." Both targets travel in the same
		// targets slice; the helper type-checks and moves each
		// independently instead of the amount/property-filter
		// auto-select executeReturnFromGraveyard uses.
		return gs.executeReturnCreatureAndLandFromGraveyard(controller, targets)
	case "return_card_from_graveyard_subset_to_hand":
		// ticket engine.auto-return-card-from-graveyard-to-hand-01eaafe522
		// (Midnight Tilling class): the candidate set is restricted to an
		// explicit caller-supplied subset (effectValue["candidates"], the
		// cards a preceding mill/dig step just placed in the graveyard)
		// rather than the whole graveyard, and the pick is optional.
		return gs.executeReturnCardFromGraveyardSubsetToHand(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "exile_target_from_graveyard":
		// ticket engine.auto-exile-from-graveyard-3fefe932a6 (Scour the
		// Desert class): "Exile target creature card from your graveyard."
		// Distinct from the amount-based auto-select "exile_from_graveyard"
		// primitive above (no target) and from battlefield exile — this is
		// a player-chosen graveyard target, optionally type-filtered.
		return gs.executeExileFromGraveyardTargetEffect(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "draw":
		return gs.executeDrawEffect(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "gain_energy":
		return gs.executeGainEnergyEffect(controller, effectValue)
	case "proliferate":
		// Rule 701.28 keyword action, no-target generic-atom path (the
		// Atraxa/Merfolk Skydiver/Yawgmoth "proliferate."/"Proliferate."
		// shape — triggered or activated, no object). Existing per-card
		// handlers still call cardfns.Proliferate(gs, playerIdx) directly
		// for cards needing "whenever you proliferate" hooks
		// (cardfns.OnYouProliferate); this generic path does not fire those.
		// count-aware round (ticket #3206, "proliferate twice" — Agent Frank
		// Horrigan class): an optional "count" in effectValue repeats the
		// keyword action that many times, each an independent Rule 701.28
		// proliferation. Missing/non-positive count defaults to a single
		// proliferation, preserving every existing no-count caller exactly.
		count := 1
		if c, ok := effectValue["count"].(float64); ok && int(c) > 1 {
			count = int(c)
		}
		for i := 0; i < count; i++ {
			gs.Proliferate()
		}
		return nil
	case "damage", "deal_damage_target", "damage_target_player":
		// deal_damage_target is a Speed-1 spelling variant (task-2121,
		// [deal_damage_target] Chandra, Legacy of Fire class): the parser
		// names an explicit "deals N damage to target ..." line after the
		// target it names, distinct from the bare "damage" line already
		// registered — same executor, both already accept targets.
		//
		// damage_target_player is the player-half sibling of that same
		// variant family (ticket #4938, REPARSE-MAP fixed_numeric_damage /
		// capability 117, Annie Joins Up class): "deals N damage to target
		// player/opponent". It routes into the SAME executeDamageEffect,
		// whose explicit-player-target branch (Target.IsPlayer) reduces
		// that player's life by exactly N — no separate executor.
		//
		// fixed_numeric_damage referent round (ticket #4671, Ashmouth Hound
		// class: "Whenever this creature blocks or becomes blocked by a
		// creature, this creature deals 1 damage to that creature."): a
		// referential damage atom whose printed target is the ANTECEDENT
		// "that creature" carries target_ref=="event_subject". When no
		// player-chosen target survived conversion, bind that antecedent
		// directly from the stamped trigger-event subject
		// (stackObj.EventCard) before dispatching — exactly like the
		// exile_then_return local fallback above — so the atom resolves
		// against the blocked/blocking creature instead of silently
		// no-op'ing on an empty target list (Rule 608.2b analog).
		if len(targets) == 0 {
			if ref, _ := effectValue["target_ref"].(string); ref == "event_subject" && stackObj != nil && stackObj.EventCard != nil {
				targets = []Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: stackObj.EventCard.ID}}
			}
		}
		return gs.executeDamageEffect(controller, source, effectValue, targets)

	case "damage_equal_to_damage_already_dealt":
		// Whipkeeper class (ticket 8109732332): amount is read from the
		// TARGET's own Card.Damage (Rule 120.3 marked damage, cleared each
		// cleanup step), not a fixed number or the target's power/toughness
		// — distinguishing this from the adjacent "damage"/stat-based atoms.
		return gs.executeDamageEqualToDamageAlreadyDealt(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "damage_each_of_x_targets":
		// damage_each_of_x_targets primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-damage-each-of-x-targets-f42082ead2, Meteor Blast class:
		// "~ deals 4 damage to each of X targets."). X is bound to the value
		// paid for the spell's {X} cost (StackObject.EffectValue["x_value"],
		// CastXSpell) and the exact count is enforced upstream by the targets
		// the casting player chose — exactly like executeUntapDynamicXTargetLands's
		// "X target lands" (count enforced upstream; this executor just
		// applies to every target it's handed, no fewer and no more). Unlike
		// a Fireball-style split ("divide N damage as you choose among any
		// number of targets"), the printed amount is NOT divided: each of
		// the X targets independently takes the full amount, so this reuses
		// executeDamageEffect's existing per-target loop unchanged. A no-op
		// with zero targets (X==0 or none chosen) rather than falling back
		// to executeDamageEffect's target-less default recipient — "each of
		// X targets" never has an implicit recipient the way a bare
		// "damage" line's target-less branch does.
		if len(targets) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		return gs.executeDamageEffect(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "damage_additional_to_subset":
		// ticket engine.auto-damage-additional-to-subset-0177a098ca
		// (Kaervek's Hex class): "~ deals X damage to each [filter] and an
		// additional Y damage to each [subset]" — a single effect, not two
		// independent damage atoms, so subset members take X+Y in one hit.
		return gs.executeDamageAdditionalToSubsetEffect(controller, source, effectValue)
	case "damage_unless_hand_count":
		// damage_unless_hand_count primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-trigger-unless-hand-count-condition-f7d59939b7,
		// Skullcage class): "~ deals N damage to that player unless they
		// have exactly three or exactly four cards in hand." See
		// executeDamageUnlessHandCountEffect.
		return gs.executeDamageUnlessHandCountEffect(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "life_gain", "gain_life":
		// gain_life is a Speed-1 spelling variant (schema-v2 3c round 11).
		return gs.executeLifeGainEffect(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "double_life_total":
		// "Double target player's life total." (Beacon of Immortality class,
		// dynamic-amount round 2, 2026-08-08).
		return gs.executeDoubleLifeTotalEffect(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "life_loss", "lose_life", "opponent_loses_life":
		// lose_life / opponent_loses_life are Speed-1 spelling variants of
		// life_loss (schema-v2 3c round 8, 76 records) — same executor;
		// its no-target default (opponent loses) matches both shapes.
		return gs.executeLifeLossEffect(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "lose_unspent_mana":
		// lose_unspent_mana primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-lose-unspent-mana-9905dcb8a3, Mana Short class: "...
		// that player loses all unspent mana."). Distinct from any tapping
		// the same ability atom performs elsewhere — this only empties the
		// mana pool via ManaPool.Empty() (the same helper Rule 500.5's
		// end-of-phase drain uses, player.go EmptyAtPhaseEnd), so mana added
		// AFTER this effect resolves is unaffected.
		return gs.executeLoseUnspentManaEffect(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "pump_and_grant_keywords_until_eot":
		if len(targets) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		return gs.executePumpAndGrantKeywordsUntilEOT(source, effectValue, targets)
	case "pump":
		effectValue = gs.resolvePumpSubtypeCountAmount(effectValue, source)
		effectValue = gs.resolvePumpTargetOwnPower(effectValue, targets)
		return gs.executePumpEffect(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "pump_all_referent_controller_eot":
		return gs.executePumpAllReferentControllerEOT(source, effectValue)
	case "pump_until_eot":
		// "Target creature gets +N/+N until end of turn" as a sequence/mode
		// atom (modal round 2, 2026-08-02) — executePumpEffect's targeted
		// branch registers exactly this UntilEOT ContinuousEffect. NO-TARGET
		// GUARD: unlike "pump", a pump_until_eot atom is always the printed
		// targeted form (the parser has no self shape for it), so with no
		// target supplied this must stay a no-op — falling into
		// executePumpEffect's self-pump default would silently pump the
		// SOURCE instead of the printed target (the ~180 shipped activated/
		// triggered records whose per-atom target never survives conversion
		// keep their current inert behavior until target promotion exists
		// for them). UNTARGETED-ALL EXCEPTION (ticket
		// engine.auto-pump-until-eot-untargeted-all-36523aeef0, Tip the
		// Scales class: "all creatures get -X/-X until end of turn"): that
		// shape carries no target at all by design, so it's let through to
		// executePumpEffect's own effectValue["all"] mass-filter branch
		// instead of tripping the no-target guard above.
		if len(targets) == 0 {
			group, _ := effectValue["group_filter"].(string)
			all, _ := effectValue["all"].(bool)
			if group == "" && !all {
				return nil
			}
		}
		return gs.executePumpEffect(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "pump_all":
		// "Creatures you control get +N/+N until end of turn" — team pump
		// (schema-v2 3c round 6: 53 records were parser-emitted with zero
		// consuming code). Filter-scoped UntilEOT ContinuousEffect; the
		// converter stashes the record's Affects into EffectValue.
		//
		// attacking_creatures_affects_filter primitive (ticket #3105,
		// REPARSE-MAP static_mod_unmapped/until end of: "Attacking
		// creatures get +N/+N until end of turn" — Army of Allah/Auron's
		// Inspiration/Dinosaur Stampede class): the printed text carries no
		// "you control" qualifier, so the affected set is every attacking
		// creature regardless of controller — unlike the ordinary team-pump
		// default. Default AffectsController to "" (unrestricted, same
		// convention AffectsSubtype already uses below) whenever the atom
		// is attacking/blocking-scoped and no explicit affects_controller
		// was supplied, instead of pump_all's usual "you" default. An
		// explicit affects_controller (e.g. Kangee's printed "you control")
		// still overrides this.
		//
		// pump_all_dynamic_x_at_resolution primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-pump-until-eot-no-target-a7be48a829, Moonshaker
		// Cavalry/Craterhoof Behemoth class: "creatures you control gain
		// flying and get +X/+X until end of turn, where X is the number of
		// creatures you control"). cards/converter.go's "the number of
		// creatures you control" fold already emits the closed sentinel
		// string "creature_count_you_control" for EffectValue["power"]/
		// ["toughness"], but getIntValue below only understands a literal
		// int/float64 and silently reads 0 for a string, so the bonus never
		// applied — this atom names NO target at all, unlike the existing
		// targeted pump_until_eot path (resolvePumpAttachedCreatureNoTarget
		// above), so there was no target-side chokepoint to fold it either.
		// Unlike evaluateVariablePowerMod's PowerModSource/ToughnessModSource
		// (Crown of Gondor's live, continuously-recalculated bonus), the
		// count here is read ONCE, right now, and folded into a literal
		// effectValue entry before the ContinuousEffect below is built —
		// matching the Comprehensive Rules fixed-at-resolution reading of
		// "X" in an until-end-of-turn pump, rather than a value that would
		// keep drifting as creatures enter or leave for the rest of the turn.
		if p, ok := effectValue["power"].(string); ok && p == "creature_count_you_control" && gs.EffectManager != nil {
			effectValue["power"] = gs.EffectManager.countCardsByType(controller, "Creature")
		}
		if t, ok := effectValue["toughness"].(string); ok && t == "creature_count_you_control" && gs.EffectManager != nil {
			effectValue["toughness"] = gs.EffectManager.countCardsByType(controller, "Creature")
		}
		defaultController := "you"
		if attacking, _ := effectValue["affects_attacking"].(bool); attacking {
			defaultController = ""
		} else if blocking, _ := effectValue["affects_blocking"].(bool); blocking {
			defaultController = ""
		} else if other, _ := effectValue["affects_other"].(bool); other {
			// pump_non_targeted_until_eot primitive (ticket
			// engine.auto-pump-non-targeted-until-eot-ce8685b08b, Massacre Girl
			// class: "each creature other than ~ gets -1/-1 until end of turn").
			// Every committed "affects_other" carddb record pairs it with an
			// explicit affects_controller (e.g. "you") that still overrides this
			// default via getStringValue below — but a plain "other than SOURCE"
			// filter with no controller qualifier at all must hit every
			// creature on the battlefield, not just the caster's, the same
			// unrestricted-controller default already used above for
			// attacking/blocking-scoped atoms.
			defaultController = ""
		}
		eff := &ContinuousEffect{
			Source:            source,
			SourceID:          source.ID,
			PowerMod:          getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 0),
			ToughnessMod:      getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 0),
			AffectsFilter:     "creature",
			AffectsController: getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_controller", defaultController),
			AffectsSubtype:    getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_subtype", ""),
			UntilEOT:          true,
		}
		if b, _ := effectValue["affects_other"].(bool); b {
			eff.ExcludeSelf = true
		}
		// affects_attacking/affects_blocking/affects_keyword (task-2389,
		// Kangee class: "Attacking/Blocking creatures with KEYWORD get
		// +N/+N until end of turn") — ContinuousEffect and its generic
		// effectAffectsCard matcher already carry/read these fields (used
		// by the static-subject path, task 2369); pump_all's own
		// ContinuousEffect just never set them before.
		if b, _ := effectValue["affects_attacking"].(bool); b {
			eff.AffectsAttacking = true
		}
		if b, _ := effectValue["affects_blocking"].(bool); b {
			eff.AffectsBlocking = true
		}
		if kw := getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_keyword", ""); kw != "" {
			eff.AffectsKeyword = Keyword(kw)
		}
		gs.EffectManager.Register(eff)
		// Cavalier-of-Flame shape: the pump also grants keywords.
		for _, kw := range keywordListFromValue(effectValue) {
			gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
				Source: source, SourceID: source.ID, GrantKeyword: kw,
				AffectsFilter: "creature", AffectsController: eff.AffectsController,
				AffectsSubtype: eff.AffectsSubtype, ExcludeSelf: eff.ExcludeSelf,
				AffectsAttacking: eff.AffectsAttacking, AffectsBlocking: eff.AffectsBlocking,
				AffectsKeyword: eff.AffectsKeyword,
				UntilEOT:       true,
			})
		}
		return nil
	case "bolster":
		// Rule 701.32 keyword action "bolster N": choose a creature with
		// the least toughness among creatures you control and put N +1/+1
		// counters on it. Random tiebreak among tied-lowest-toughness
		// creatures (CR 701.32); no-op if you control no creatures. The
		// target is self-selected at resolution time — not a player-chosen
		// target — so this is a distinct executor from put_counters.
		// (task-2910, 2026-08-10)
		amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
		if amount < 1 {
			amount = 1
		}
		var candidates []*Card
		minToughness := int(^uint(0) >> 1) // max int
		for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
			if !c.IsCreature() || c.Controller != controller {
				continue
			}
			t := c.GetToughnessWithEffects(gs.EffectManager)
			if t < minToughness {
				minToughness = t
				candidates = []*Card{c}
			} else if t == minToughness {
				candidates = append(candidates, c)
			}
		}
		if len(candidates) == 0 {
			return nil // no-op: controller has no creatures
		}
		chosen := candidates[0]
		if len(candidates) > 1 {
			chosen = candidates[rand.Intn(len(candidates))]
		}
		gs.AddCounters(chosen, CounterPlusOnePlusOne, amount)
		gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
		return nil
	case "put_counter", "put_counters":
		// put_counters (plural) was parser-emitted but never consumed —
		// schema-v2 3c round 1 wires both spellings to the same executor.
		return gs.executePutCounterEffect(source, effectValue, targets)
	case "put_counter_and_draw_card":
		if err := gs.executePutCounterEffect(source, effectValue, targets); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if controller >= 0 && controller < len(gs.Players) {
			_ = gs.Players[controller].DrawCards(1)
		}
		return nil
	case "put_counters_independent_multi_target":
		// put_counters_independent_multi_target primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-put-counters-independent-multi-target-8814b1cc89,
		// River Heralds' Boon class): "Put a +1/+1 counter on target
		// creature and a +1/+1 counter on up to one target Merfolk." See
		// executePutCountersIndependentMultiTarget.
		return gs.executePutCountersIndependentMultiTarget(targets, effectValue)
	case "tap_and_put_counter":
		return gs.executeTapAndPutCounterEffect(targets, effectValue)
	case "remove_counter_damage":
		return gs.executeRemoveCounterDamageEffect(source, effectValue, targets)
	case "destroy_up_to_artifact_creature_land":
		// "Destroy up to one target artifact, up to one target creature,
		// and up to one target land." (Boom Box class) — three
		// independently optional targets in a single ability resolution.
		// Unlike the "all" mass-destroy branch below (whole battlefield,
		// filter-scoped) and the plain single-target branch below (exactly
		// one target), the caller supplies zero to three targets here —
		// one per declined-or-chosen slot — and every supplied target is
		// destroyed; a declined slot is simply absent from targets, not an
		// error. See executeDestroyUpToArtifactCreatureLand.
		return gs.executeDestroyUpToArtifactCreatureLand(targets)
	case "destroy_up_to_dynamic_permanent_count":
		// "destroy up to X target artifacts without mana abilities, where
		// X is the number of Vampires you control" (ticket
		// engine.auto-destroy-up-to-dynamic-permanent-count-8e715d81dc,
		// Midnight Arsonist class). X is a LIVE count, not a fixed
		// literal — already folded into effectValue["amount"] above via
		// the existing generic "battlefield"+"subtype" amount_count kind
		// (dynamic_amount.go), the same fold every other amount_count
		// primitive rides. See executeDestroyUpToDynamicPermanentCount.
		return gs.executeDestroyUpToDynamicPermanentCount(targets, effectValue)
	case "destroy_up_to_lands_by_source_counter":
		// destroy_up_to_lands_by_source_counter primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-destroy-up-to-dynamic-targets-0722caa971, Rumbling
		// Crescendo class: "{R}, Sacrifice this enchantment: Destroy up to
		// X target lands, where X is the number of verse counters on this
		// enchantment."). See executeDestroyUpToLandsBySourceCounter.
		return gs.executeDestroyUpToLandsBySourceCounter(source, targets, effectValue)
	case "destroy_multi_filter_targets":
		// "Destroy target artifact, target creature, target enchantment,
		// and target land." (Decimate class) — N SIMULTANEOUS REQUIRED
		// targets resolved in one shot, each validated against its OWN
		// positional filter (effect_value.filters[i] for targets[i]).
		// Unlike destroy_up_to_artifact_creature_land just above (every
		// slot optional) and the "all" mass-destroy branch below (one
		// shared filter across the whole battlefield), every slot here is
		// mandatory and independently typed. See
		// executeDestroyMultiFilterTargets.
		return gs.executeDestroyMultiFilterTargets(targets, effectValue)
	case "exile_linked_tokens_on_leaves_battlefield":
		// exile_linked_tokens_on_leaves_battlefield primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-exile-linked-tokens-on-leaves-battlefield-1bf674ba89,
		// Mysterio, Master of Illusion class: "Exile those tokens when
		// Mysterio leaves the battlefield."). The recipient set is
		// whatever executeCreateLinkedExileTokens recorded against
		// source.ID -- the exact tokens an earlier effect of this same
		// ability created -- not a fresh battlefield filter query, so
		// this is never conflated with the "all"-filter mass-exile shape.
		// See executeExileLinkedTokensOnLeave.
		return gs.executeExileLinkedTokensOnLeave(source)
	case "destroy":
		// destroy_up_to_one_target_creature_triggered (ticket
		// engine.auto-destroy-up-to-one-target-creature-triggered-fae51c6fdb,
		// Necron Deathmark class: "destroy up to one target creature"):
		// the counted-target executor below already implements the
		// generic "up to N" optional-targeting convention (0..maxTargets
		// targets), but was previously gated to maxTargets>1 only, so a
		// triggered ability's max_targets==1 ("up to one") atom fell
		// through to the mandatory single-target path instead. A plain
		// mandatory "destroy target creature" atom never sets max_targets
		// (getIntValue defaults to 0), so it is untouched by widening this
		// to >=1.
		if maxTargets := getIntValue(effectValue, "max_targets", 0); maxTargets >= 1 {
			return gs.executeDestroyCountedMultiTarget(targets, maxTargets, false)
		}
		// "Destroy all/each X[ you (don't) control]" as one step of a v2
		// effect sequence (task-1876 round 2, e.g. "Destroy all creatures.
		// Draw a card."). Filter-scoped, not target-scoped — mirrors the
		// whole-spell resolveMassDestroy path but reads filter/controller
		// off this atom's effect_value map instead of SpellEffect.
		//
		// triggered_destroy_single_target_referent round (ticket
		// engine.auto-triggered-destroy-single-target-referent-340d84d886,
		// Mire Blight class: "When enchanted creature is dealt damage,
		// destroy it."): the antecedent "it" is bound to the triggering
		// event's subject exactly like the "damage" case's
		// target_ref=="event_subject" fallback elsewhere in this switch —
		// the enchanted creature that was dealt damage (a ReceiverSide
		// trigger stamps stackObj.EventCard = event.Target) rather than a
		// chosen target. Only fires when no target was already supplied,
		// so a real "destroy target creature" atom (which always carries a
		// chosen target) is untouched, and it's independent of the "all"
		// mass-destroy branch just below.
		if len(targets) == 0 {
			if ref, _ := effectValue["target_ref"].(string); ref == "event_subject" && stackObj != nil && stackObj.EventCard != nil {
				targets = []Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: stackObj.EventCard.ID}}
			}
		}
		if all, _ := effectValue["all"].(bool); all {
			// "destroy all OTHER creatures" (task-2439, The Nipton Lottery)
			// -- mirrors bounce's exclude_self/excludeID just below.
			excludeSelf, _ := effectValue["exclude_self"].(bool)
			var excludeID string
			if excludeSelf && source != nil {
				excludeID = source.ID
			}
			// cant_be_regenerated (ticket #3974, REPARSE-MAP
			// destroy_mass_subtype_token_combo / "destroy all X tokens.
			// They can't be regenerated." — Drudge Spell class): thread
			// the flag through to destroyAllMatching so the sweep uses
			// DestroyTargetForced (Rule 701.16b) instead of the
			// shield-honoring DestroyTarget — mirrors the whole-spell
			// resolveMassDestroy path's SpellEffect.CantBeRegenerated.
			cantBeRegenerated, _ := effectValue["cant_be_regenerated"].(bool)
			tokensOnly, _ := effectValue["tokens_only"].(bool)
			filter := getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "")
			// mass_destroy_filter_mana_value_le_x (ticket
			// engine.auto-mass-destroy-filter-mana-value-le-x-d65b4b5043,
			// Meltdown class: "Destroy each artifact with mana value X or
			// less."). X is bound at resolution time (mana actually
			// paid/spent, folded into effectValue["mana_value_x"] by the same
			// dispatch chokepoint executeCopyTargetManaValueEqualsX documents
			// above), not printed literally in the filter string, so it can't
			// ride matchesPermanentFilter's massFilterCmpRe "_mv_lte_\d+"
			// suffix directly (that regex only recognizes a LITERAL printed
			// N). Splicing the resolved X into that exact suffix here reuses
			// matchesPermanentFilter's existing comparator support unchanged.
			// Absent mana_value_x (-1 default), filter is passed through
			// untouched, so a plain "destroy each artifact" (no threshold)
			// keeps its original flat all-artifacts shape.
			if manaValueX := getIntValue(effectValue, "mana_value_x", -1); manaValueX >= 0 {
				if filter == "" {
					filter = "permanent"
				}
				filter = filter + "_mv_lte_" + strconv.Itoa(manaValueX)
			}
			// destroy_filter_mv_eq_source_counters (ticket
			// engine.auto-destroy-filter-mv-eq-source-counters-090e801bc2,
			// Necroplasm class: "destroy each creature with mana value equal
			// to the number of +1/+1 counters on this creature"). Unlike
			// mana_value_x above (folded from paid/chosen X by
			// dynamic_amount.go), this threshold is a LIVE read of the
			// +1/+1 counters currently on the permanent generating the
			// effect (source) at resolution time, so it's read directly off
			// source.Counters instead of effectValue.
			if eq, _ := effectValue["mv_eq_source_counters"].(bool); eq && source != nil {
				if filter == "" {
					filter = "permanent"
				}
				filter = filter + "_mv_eq_" + strconv.Itoa(source.Counters.GetCount(CounterPlusOnePlusOne))
			}
			// destroy_by_referent_mana_value (ticket
			// engine.auto-destroy-by-referent-mana-value-102f6a6186, Sanguine
			// Praetor class: "{B}, Sacrifice a creature: Destroy each creature
			// with the same mana value as the sacrificed creature."). Unlike
			// mv_eq_source_counters above (a live read of source's own
			// counters), this threshold is the mana value of the object bound
			// by the ability's COST — the sacrificed creature — already
			// stamped onto effectValue as "sacrificed_mv" by
			// completeChoiceActivation (gamestate_choice.go) when a filtered
			// Sacrifice cost was paid, mirroring
			// resolveDamageSacrificedStatAmount's read of the same key.
			if eq, _ := effectValue["mv_eq_referent"].(bool); eq {
				if mv, ok := effectValue["sacrificed_mv"].(int); ok {
					if filter == "" {
						filter = "permanent"
					}
					filter = filter + "_mv_eq_" + strconv.Itoa(mv)
				}
			}
			// destroy_attached_to_referent (ticket
			// engine.auto-destroy-attached-to-referent-d32bfbcd7a, Turn to Slag
			// class: "~ deals 5 damage to target creature. Destroy all
			// Equipment attached to that creature."). "That creature" is
			// whatever card a PRECEDING atom in this SAME sequence most
			// recently acted on (gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"], armed by
			// effect_sequence.go's noteSeqReferent), mirroring
			// destroy_delayed_referent's referent read elsewhere in this
			// file. Absent an armed referent, this no-ops rather than
			// falling back to the unscoped mass-destroy sweep — a plain
			// "destroy all Equipment" (no attached_to_referent flag) is
			// completely untouched and keeps sweeping the whole battlefield
			// (negative example: unscoped mass destroy must not be narrowed).
			var attachedTo string
			if attachedToReferent, _ := effectValue["attached_to_referent"].(bool); attachedToReferent {
				if gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
					return nil
				}
				attachedTo = gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"]
				if attachedTo == "" {
					return nil
				}
			}
			return gs.destroyAllMatching(controller,
				filter,
				getStringValue(effectValue, "controller", ""), cantBeRegenerated, excludeID, tokensOnly, attachedTo)
		}
		// cant_be_regenerated on a single-target destroy (ticket #3976,
		// REPARSE-MAP destroy_single_target_regen_gap / "destroy target X
		// and it can't be regenerated." — Knight of the Mists class):
		// thread the flag through so the chosen target is destroyed via
		// DestroyTargetForced (Rule 701.16b) instead of the shield-honoring
		// DestroyTarget — mirrors the mass branch's flag just above.
		// "Destroy target non-X, non-Y, ... creature" (task
		// engine.auto-single-target-destroy-creature-excluding-subtype-b7eec0f39d,
		// Power Word Kill class): a single-target destroy whose legality is
		// gated on the target's SubTypes excluding a fixed set — see
		// executeDestroyExcludingSubtypesEffect above.
		if attached, _ := effectValue["target_attached"].(bool); attached {
			if source != nil && source.AttachedTo != "" {
				if card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(source.AttachedTo); err == nil {
					targets = []Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: card.ID}}
				}
			}
		}
		if excludeSubtypes := subtypesFromEffectValue(effectValue["exclude_subtypes"]); len(excludeSubtypes) > 0 {
			return gs.executeDestroyExcludingSubtypesEffect(targets, excludeSubtypes)
		}
		cantBeRegenerated, _ := effectValue["cant_be_regenerated"].(bool)
		gs.noteDestroyedArtifactsPerController(targets)
		return gs.executeDestroyEffect(targets, cantBeRegenerated)
	case "damage_each_player_artifacts_destroyed_this_way":
		// destroyed_this_way_per_player_count (ticket
		// engine.auto-destroyed-this-way-per-player-count-58304e2f98,
		// Builder's Bane class: "~ deals damage to each player equal to
		// the number of artifacts they controlled that were put into a
		// graveyard this way."). Unlike Angel's Trumpet's tapped_this_way
		// (a single flat seqTally value applied to one recipient), each
		// player is damaged by their OWN count, read back from the
		// per-controller keys the preceding "destroy" atom in the same
		// sequence wrote via noteDestroyedArtifactsPerController.
		return gs.executeDamageEachPlayerArtifactsDestroyedThisWay()
	case "bounce":
		if handled, err := gs.executeBounceFromGraveyardTarget(targets); handled || err != nil {
			return err
		}
		// "return ~ to its owner's hand" (task-1983 spine_L2, Reinforced
		// Ronin / Thopter Assembly class): a no-target bounce of the
		// ability's own source. Mirrors executePutCounterEffect's
		// target_self read — the parser emits value.target_self for a
		// bounce whose object is the source itself (parse_atom reports
		// {'filter':'self'}), and the engine bounces the source card.
		if effectValue["target_self"] != nil {
			if source == nil {
				return nil
			}
			return gs.executeBounceEffect([]Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: source.ID}})
		}
		// "Return all/each/every [other] X[ you (don't) control] to their
		// owners' hand(s)" as one step of a v2 effect sequence (task-2392
		// round 2, mirrors destroy/exile's identical "all" mass-atom branch
		// just above/below) — filter/controller-scoped across the WHOLE
		// battlefield, unlike the ability's-own-controller-only choice just
		// below. A leading "other" (effect_value.exclude_self) excludes the
		// ability's own source from the sweep.
		if all, _ := effectValue["all"].(bool); all {
			excludeSelf, _ := effectValue["exclude_self"].(bool)
			var excludeID string
			if excludeSelf && source != nil {
				excludeID = source.ID
			}
			return gs.bounceAllMatching(controller,
				getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", ""),
				getStringValue(effectValue, "controller", ""), excludeID)
		}
		// "return a/another/each other [filter] X you control to its owner's
		// hand" (task-2392): an untargeted filter-scoped choice, not MTG
		// targeting — mirrors the "sacrifice" case's identical filter/n/
		// exclude_self value shape just below. See executeBounceFiltered.
		if filter, ok := effectValue["filter"].(string); ok && filter != "" {
			return gs.executeBounceFiltered(controller, source, effectValue)
		}
		return gs.executeBounceEffect(targets)
	case "attach":
		// "attach ~ to that creature" (Rule 303.4h/301.5c reattach effect —
		// Brilliant Wings' "you may pay {1}. If you do, attach this Aura to
		// that creature," and the manifest-dread Equipment cycle's "attach
		// this Equipment to that creature"). Reuses gs.AttachAura (aura.go),
		// which already clears the attachment's entry from its PREVIOUS
		// target's Attachments list on reattach — the exact bug this effect
		// would otherwise hit. Works unchanged for Equipment too: AttachAura
		// only consults EnchantFilter, which is empty/unset for Equipment,
		// so auraTargetMatchesFor's "" case matches any target.
		if source == nil || len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard {
			return nil
		}
		return gs.AttachAura(source.Controller, source.ID, targets[0].CardID, "")
	case "exile":
		// "Exile all/each X[ you (don't) control]" as one step of a v2
		// effect sequence (task-1922, mirrors task-1876 round 2 for
		// destroy) — filter-scoped, not target-scoped.
		if all, _ := effectValue["all"].(bool); all {
			return gs.exileAllMatching(controller,
				getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", ""),
				getStringValue(effectValue, "controller", ""))
		}
		// "exile ~" on an activated/triggered ability's own source (task-2363,
		// Aethergeode Miner/Eternal Scourge/Nezahal/Obzedat/Stenn class) —
		// mirrors bounce's target_self bridge just above: no cast-time
		// SpellEffect involved, so the source is looked up directly at
		// resolution rather than riding the spell-only SelfExile flag
		// (cards/converter.go) the 'self' value key feeds instead.
		if effectValue["target_self"] != nil {
			if source == nil {
				return nil
			}
			return gs.executeExileEffect([]Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: source.ID}}, -1)
		}
		// mana_value filter (ticket
		// engine.auto-exile-permanent-with-mana-value-filter-29de385b25,
		// Isolate class): "Exile target permanent with mana value N." —
		// map_atom emits effect_value.mana_value alongside the ordinary
		// filter key; -1 (absent) keeps every other exile atom unrestricted.
		manaValue := getIntValue(effectValue, "mana_value", -1)
		// color filter (ticket
		// engine.auto-exile-color-filter-effectvalue-wiring-abf0d6f486,
		// Devout Decree class): "Exile target creature or planeswalker
		// that's black or red." — effect_value.colors restricts exile to
		// permanents of that color; empty/absent keeps every other exile
		// atom unrestricted.
		colorFilter := exileColorFilterFromEffectValue(effectValue)
		return gs.executeExileEffect(targets, manaValue, colorFilter...)
	case "exile_target_plus_named_match_tokens":
		// exile_target_plus_named_match_tokens primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-exile-target-plus-named-match-tokens-b9b031ee17, Legions
		// to Ashes class): "Exile target nonland permanent an opponent
		// controls and all tokens that player controls with the same name as
		// that permanent." One resolution, two groups: the chosen target
		// (ordinary targeting, gated by the ability's own "nonland permanent
		// an opponent controls" Target filter) PLUS every token under that
		// target's controller sharing its name.
		return gs.executeExileTargetPlusNamedMatchTokens(targets)
	case "exile_mv_le_source_counters_multizone":
		// exile_mv_le_source_counters_multizone primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-exile-mv-le-source-counters-multizone-e9a468b183,
		// Sphere of Annihilation class): "exile ~, all creatures and
		// planeswalkers with mana value less than or equal to the number
		// of void counters on it, and all creature and planeswalker cards
		// in graveyards with mana value less than or equal to the number
		// of void counters on it." Untargeted, source-scoped — reads its
		// threshold from a named counter on source, then sweeps three
		// zones (source itself, battlefield, every graveyard).
		return gs.executeExileMvLeSourceCountersMultizone(source, effectValue)
	case "look_top_n_put_matching_onto_battlefield_tapped_attacking_rest_bottom_random", "may_look_top_n_put_matching_onto_battlefield_tapped_attacking_rest_bottom_random":
		// look_top_n_put_matching_onto_battlefield_tapped_attacking_rest_bottom_random
		// primitive (ticket #4027, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:look_top / "look
		// at the top N cards of your library. You may put a [filter] card
		// from among them onto the battlefield tapped and attacking. Put the
		// rest on the bottom of your library in a random order" — Arthur,
		// Marigold Knight class): look at the top N of the controller's
		// library, put the player's chosen card from among them (validated
		// against effectValue["filter"] via matchesRevealFilter, the same
		// card-type/subtype vocabulary the reveal-N family uses) onto the
		// battlefield TAPPED AND ATTACKING, and put the remaining looked-at
		// cards on the bottom of the library in a random order. The chosen
		// card arrives as effectValue["chosen"] (a card ID); when absent
		// (declined "may" or a non-interactive run) nothing enters the
		// battlefield and ALL N looked-at cards go to the bottom shuffled —
		// a legal player choice, so no state is lost either way.
		//
		// "Tapped and attacking" is the one piece with no existing executor:
		// DeclareAttackers only runs in the declare-attackers step and
		// rejects tapped/summoning-sick creatures, and DeclareAttacker
		// (combat.go) enforces the same CanAttack gate. So the entry is
		// modeled directly: battlefield entry (ETB event + ability
		// registration, mirroring earthbend.go's return path), then the
		// tapped-and-attacking state the card text promises (Tapped,
		// Attacking, CombatState.Attackers, attacks event — the same fields
		// DeclareAttackerWithEffects sets for a normal declaration). The
		// card's printed haste (Arthur) is what legally makes the attack
		// possible; the engine does not re-litigate that legality here, it
		// just records the declared attack. "may_" variant: the optional
		// keyword is a player-choice concern handled at activation time
		// (declining simply never supplies "chosen"), so both effect names
		// share this executor.
		n := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 0)
		if n <= 0 {
			return nil
		}
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		lib := gs.Players[controller].Library
		top, err := lib.LookAtTop(n)
		if err != nil || len(top) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		byID := make(map[string]*Card, len(top))
		for _, c := range top {
			byID[c.ID] = c
		}
		var chosen *Card
		if id, _ := effectValue["chosen"].(string); id != "" {
			if c, ok := byID[id]; ok && matchesRevealFilter(c, getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "")) {
				chosen = c
			}
		}
		rest := make([]*Card, 0, len(top))
		for _, c := range top {
			if c != chosen {
				rest = append(rest, c)
			}
		}
		// Remove the top N looked-at cards from the library, then put only
		// the rest (excluding the chosen card, which goes to the battlefield
		// below) on the bottom in a random order.
		lib.Cards = lib.Cards[len(top):]
		rand.Shuffle(len(rest), func(i, j int) {
			rest[i], rest[j] = rest[j], rest[i]
		})
		for _, c := range rest {
			_ = lib.PutOnBottom(c)
		}
		if chosen != nil {
			chosen.Controller = controller
			chosen.Tapped = true
			chosen.Attacking = true
			gs.Battlefield.AddCard(chosen)
			gs.RegisterCardAbilities(chosen)
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				for _, a := range gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateETBEvent(chosen, controller)) {
					gs.PutTriggeredAbilityOnStack(a)
				}
				if gs.CombatState != nil {
					gs.CombatState.Attackers = append(gs.CombatState.Attackers, chosen)
				}
				gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateAttacksEvent(chosen, controller, 1-controller))
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "look_top_n_rearrange", "may_look_top_n_rearrange":
		// library_reorder_in_any_order primitive (ticket #4027, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:look_top / "look at the top N cards of your library,
		// then put them back in any order" family — Aven Fateshaper class):
		// reveal the top N of the controller's library, then let the
		// controller reorder exactly those N cards and put them back on top
		// in the chosen order (no cards move to any other zone). This engine
		// models every zone as fully visible already (see
		// look_top_library's doc just below), so "reveal" has no boardstate
		// effect of its own; the observable behavior is the reorder. The
		// player's chosen order arrives as effectValue["reorder"] ([]string
		// of card IDs, first element = new top card); when absent (a
		// non-interactive test/CLI run, or the "may" variant declined) the
		// top N are put back in their original order — a legal player
		// choice, so no state is lost either way. ReorderTopInPlace
		// (zone.go) validates the set is exactly the current top N and
		// rejects anything that would move a card to another zone or
		// reorder cards beyond the top N. "may_" variant: the optional
		// keyword is a player-choice concern handled at activation time
		// (declining simply never runs this effect), so both effect names
		// share this executor.
		n := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 0)
		if n <= 0 {
			return nil
		}
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		lib := gs.Players[controller].Library
		top, err := lib.LookAtTop(n)
		if err != nil || len(top) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		ids, _ := effectValue["reorder"].([]string)
		if len(ids) == 0 {
			return nil // no chosen order: put the top N back as-is
		}
		byID := make(map[string]*Card, len(top))
		for _, c := range top {
			byID[c.ID] = c
		}
		chosen := make([]*Card, 0, len(ids))
		for _, id := range ids {
			c, ok := byID[id]
			if !ok {
				return nil // invalid order: leave the library untouched
			}
			chosen = append(chosen, c)
		}
		return lib.ReorderTopInPlace(chosen)
	case "look_top_library":
		// look_top_library primitive (ticket #3029, REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:?
		// / "look at the top card(s) of library" family — Amareth/A-Case the
		// Joint/Black Cat/Blightwing Bandit class). This engine models every
		// zone as fully visible already (see GameState.HandRevealedThisTurn's
		// doc comment), so "look at" has no boardstate effect beyond
		// validating the peek doesn't disturb the library — Zone.LookAtTop
		// (already used unchanged by exile_top_may_play/exile_library_top)
		// deliberately does not remove cards. "who" scoping mirrors
		// exile_library_top's controller/each_opponent/each_player triple; a
		// player-target atom (e.g. "target opponent's library") is honored
		// via targets when present, ahead of the "who" fallback.
		n := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
		var playerIdxs []int
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard && t.PlayerIdx >= 0 && t.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
				playerIdxs = append(playerIdxs, t.PlayerIdx)
			}
		}
		if len(playerIdxs) == 0 {
			switch getStringValue(effectValue, "who", "you") {
			case "each_opponent":
				for i := range gs.Players {
					if i != controller {
						playerIdxs = append(playerIdxs, i)
					}
				}
			case "each_player":
				for i := range gs.Players {
					playerIdxs = append(playerIdxs, i)
				}
			default:
				playerIdxs = []int{controller}
			}
		}
		for _, pi := range playerIdxs {
			if pi < 0 || pi >= len(gs.Players) {
				continue
			}
			gs.Players[pi].Library.LookAtTop(n)
		}
		return nil
	case "goad":
		// Rule 701.38 keyword action "goad" (goad round 2026-08-03,
		// task-2002): mark target creatures goaded until their controller's
		// next turn. Sets Card.GoadedUntilTurn = TurnCount+1 directly — the
		// same field cardfns/lib_batch_c.go's Goad sets and DeclareAttackers
		// (gamestate.go) enforces as "must attack if able". Inlined rather
		// than calling cardfns.Goad to avoid a game->cardfns import cycle.
		// Mass shape ("goad all creatures your opponents control") is
		// filter-scoped like exile/destroy all and resolved from the
		// battlefield at resolution time.
		if all, _ := effectValue["all"].(bool); all {
			return gs.executeGoadAllEffect(controller,
				getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", ""),
				getStringValue(effectValue, "controller", ""))
		}
		for _, target := range targets {
			if !target.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			if card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID); err == nil {
				card.GoadedUntilTurn = gs.TurnCount + 1
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "lock_creature":
		// "{C}, {T}: Target creature can't attack or block this turn."
		// Grant Defender keyword (prevents attacks) and register a blocking restriction
		return gs.executeLockCreatureEffect(source, targets)
	case "cant_block_target":
		// "Target creature can't block this turn." (e.g. Voldaren Duelist)
		// Only the ability to block is removed; the creature can still attack.
		return gs.executeCantBlockTargetEffect(source, targets)
	case "skip_untap_next":
		// "~ doesn't untap during your next untap step." (? round
		// 2026-08-03) — rides the exert flag (Rule 701.39 machinery,
		// consumed+cleared by Zone.UntapAll).
		if source != nil {
			source.SkipNextUntap = true
		}
		return nil
	case "cast_from_zone":
		// cast_free round (2026-08-03, primitive spec from GLM park #2106),
		// round-1 slice: grant permission to cast the EVENT-REFERENCED card
		// from EXILE ("exile it. You may cast it [this turn / for as long as
		// it remains exiled] [without paying its mana cost]"). Rides the
		// existing ExilePlayGrant channel (cast_from_exile.go) — the read
		// side (exilePlayGrantFor + CastSpellFromExileWithTargets) is fully
		// wired incl. Free/AnyColor cost modes. Vanished referent = no-op.
		if stackObj.EventCard == nil {
			return nil
		}
		free, _ := effectValue["free"].(bool)
		anyColor, _ := effectValue["any_mana"].(bool)
		allowLand, _ := effectValue["allow_land"].(bool)
		altCostEnergyEqualMV, _ := effectValue["alt_cost_energy_equal_mv"].(bool)
		altCostLifeEqualMV, _ := effectValue["alt_cost_life_equal_mv"].(bool)
		untilEOT := true
		if d, _ := effectValue["duration"].(string); d == "while_exiled" {
			untilEOT = false
		}
		stackObj.EventCard.ExilePlay = &ExilePlayGrant{
			Controller:           controller,
			Free:                 free,
			AnyColor:             anyColor,
			AllowLand:            allowLand,
			AltCostEnergyEqualMV: altCostEnergyEqualMV,
			AltCostLifeEqualMV:   altCostLifeEqualMV,
			UntilEndOfTurn:       untilEOT,
			GrantedTurn:          gs.TurnCount,
		}
		return nil
	case "cast_free_from_hand":
		// cast_free round (2026-08-08, ticket #2506, Kellan the Kid class):
		// "you may cast a [filter] spell with equal or lesser mana value
		// [than the triggering spell] from your hand without paying its mana
		// cost." Unlike cast_from_zone (which grants permission to cast an
		// EXILE-bound card later), this casts immediately out of HAND — no
		// grant to record, no zone-permission plumbing. maxMV comes from the
		// event-referenced card (the spell that triggered this ability); a
		// nil EventCard is a no-op (nothing to compare against). Auto-picks
		// the first matching hand card — the engine has no player-choice hook
		// here, matching the CastFirstFromHandForFree convention the existing
		// bespoke Baral's/Kari Zev's Expertise handlers already use.
		if stackObj.EventCard == nil || controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		maxMV := stackObj.EventCard.ManaCost.CMC()
		permanentOnly, _ := effectValue["permanent_only"].(bool)
		hand := gs.Players[controller].Hand
		for _, c := range hand.GetAll() {
			if c.IsLand() || c.ManaCost.CMC() > maxMV {
				continue
			}
			if permanentOnly && !c.IsPermanent() {
				continue
			}
			if _, err := hand.RemoveCard(c.ID); err == nil {
				gs.Stack.PushSpell(c, controller)
			}
			break
		}
		return nil
	case "cast_spells_as_though_flash":
		// cast_free round (2026-08-04): the THIS-TURN sibling of the
		// always-continuous static AbilityToEffect case (converter.go
		// case "cast_spells_as_though_flash" -> GrantsAsThoughFlash).
		// Activated/triggered "you may cast [Type] spells this turn as
		// though they had flash" (Alchemist's Refuge, Emergence Zone,
		// Winding Canyons) — a temporary caster-permission grant. Registers
		// a UntilEOT ContinuousEffect with GrantsAsThoughFlash + the same
		// AffectsFilter/AffectsSubtype/AffectsController scoping pump_all
		// uses; the read side (EffectManager.CasterGrantedAsThoughFlash /
		// effectAppliesToCaster / GameState.CanCastAsThoughFlash) already
		// consults it at every cast-timing check. AffectsFilter=="" (no
		// filter) means all spells — effectAppliesToCaster's empty-filter
		// fall-through matches any spell. Register stamps SourceController
		// from source.Controller, so AffectsController="you" scopes to the
		// activator. Mirrors pump_all.
		if gs.EffectManager == nil {
			return nil
		}
		gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
			Source:              source,
			GrantsAsThoughFlash: true,
			AffectsFilter:       getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_filter", ""),
			AffectsSubtype:      getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_subtype", ""),
			AffectsController:   getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_controller", "you"),
			UntilEOT:            true,
		})
		return nil
	case "cant_be_blocked_target":
		// "Target creature can't be blocked this turn." (e.g. Passwall Adept)
		return gs.executeCantBeBlockedTargetEffect(source, targets)
	case "token_copy":
		// "Create a token that's a copy of X." (task-1994) — see
		// game/token_copy.go for the target_self/target_attached/explicit-
		// target/event_subject-referent dispatch.
		return gs.executeTokenCopyEffect(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "encore":
		// "Encore [cost]" (Rule 702.130b) — per-opponent hasty token copies
		// that must attack this turn, sacrificed at the next end step; see
		// game/encore.go. The exile-from-graveyard cost was already paid at
		// activation (Cost.Sacrifice == "self_from_graveyard").
		return gs.executeEncoreEffect(controller, source)
	case "reconfigure":
		// "Reconfigure [cost]" (Rule 702.151) — modal on live attach state:
		// unattached -> attach to target creature you control, attached ->
		// unattach; see game/reconfigure.go. The mana cost was already paid
		// at activation.
		return gs.executeReconfigureEffect(controller, source, targets)
	case "token", "create_token":
		// Resolve counter-based token count from the source card when no
		// explicit count is set. "create a token for each +1/+1 counter on
		// this creature" — count_var:"plus_one_plus_one" (new imports) or
		// detected from effect_text (legacy stored defs).
		if effectValue != nil {
			if _, hasCount := effectValue["count"]; !hasCount {
				// create_token_dynamic_count primitive (ticket #5067,
				// REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:create_token / "create a token
				// for each <runtime count>" family — Aatchik, Emerald
				// Radian class): N is resolved AT TRIGGER TIME from a named
				// runtime count source via the SAME closed count-spec
				// vocabulary EvalDynamicAmount already understands
				// (game/dynamic_amount.go) — graveyard card-type counts
				// (e.g. artifact and/or creature cards in your graveyard),
				// devotion, counters put on creatures this turn, mana
				// spent, or the X of the cost. effectValue["count_spec"]
				// carries the spec (parser-supplied, out of scope here).
				// The resolved value is folded onto "count" so
				// executeTokenEffect keeps reading a plain number, exactly
				// like the plus_one_plus_one branch below; an unresolvable
				// spec is left alone (no guess) rather than defaulting to
				// one token.
				if spec, ok := effectValue["count_spec"].(map[string]interface{}); ok && spec != nil {
					if n, ok := gs.EvalDynamicAmount(spec, controller, source); ok {
						effectValue["count"] = n
					}
				}
			}
			if _, hasCount := effectValue["count"]; !hasCount {
				useCounterCount := false
				if cv, ok := effectValue["count_var"].(string); ok && cv == "plus_one_plus_one" {
					useCounterCount = true
				} else {
					text := strings.ToLower(getStringValue(effectValue, "effect_text", ""))
					if strings.Contains(text, "for each") && strings.Contains(text, "+1/+1 counter") {
						useCounterCount = true
					}
				}
				if useCounterCount && source != nil {
					effectValue["count"] = source.Counters.PlusOnePlusOne
				}
			}
		}
		// token_create_target_controlled (task-2847): "target opponent
		// creates N tokens" (Clackbridge Troll/Forbidden Orchard/Generous
		// Plunderer/Hungry Lynx class) — executeTokenEffect always created
		// tokens under this ability's own controller, so a player target
		// (IsCard == false) had nowhere to redirect to. A card/creature
		// target, or no target at all, keeps the existing self-controlled
		// behaviour unchanged.
		tokenController := controller
		if len(targets) > 0 && !targets[0].IsCard {
			tokenController = targets[0].PlayerIdx
		}
		// put_counters_on_self_created_token (ticket bf85bad357): route
		// through the tracking wrapper so a following "put X +1/+1 counters
		// on it" atom in the same paragraph chain can resolve the pronoun.
		return gs.executeCreateTokenAndTrackLast(tokenController, effectValue)
	case "pump_self_eot":
		// "This creature gets +P/+T until end of turn." Used by parameterised
		// prowess variants (e.g. Slickshot Show-Off's +2/+0). Registers a
		// continuous effect targeting the source itself with UntilEOT so
		// the cleanup step automatically tears it down. An optional "keyword"
		// value also grants that keyword ("... and gains vigilance until end
		// of turn", e.g. Territorial Boar).
		if source != nil && gs.EffectManager != nil {
			gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
				Source:          source,
				SourceID:        source.ID,
				AffectsSpecific: source.ID,
				PowerMod:        getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 0),
				ToughnessMod:    getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 0),
				GrantKeyword:    Keyword(getStringValue(effectValue, "keyword", "")),
				UntilEOT:        true,
			})
		}
		return nil
	case "grant_keywords_referent_eot":
		// sequence_referent_keyword_grant primitive (ticket #3470,
		// REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:grant_eot / "It gains haste until end
		// of turn" family — Alexios Deimos of Kosmos, Admiral Brass
		// Unsinkable, Awaken the Sleeper, Baral and Kari Zev class): "it"
		// refers to whatever card a PRECEDING atom in this SAME sequence
		// most recently acted on (gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"], armed by
		// executeEffectSequenceEv's per-atom bookkeeping in
		// effect_sequence.go's noteSeqReferent), not this atom's own
		// targets. Deliberately left untargeted so an intervening rider
		// atom (e.g. "Untap that creature.") can sit between the
		// referent-establishing atom (gain_control, untap_target_permanent,
		// etc.) and this one without breaking the grant — the gap the
		// narrower gain_control-bundled shape (control_effects.go) can't
		// cover.
		kws := keywordListFromValue(effectValue)
		// referent_pump_and_keyword_until_eot primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-referent-pump-and-keyword-until-eot-b5949b1730,
		// Macabre Mockery class: "It gets +2/+0 and gains haste until end
		// of turn."): the SAME referent-reading atom above now also carries
		// "power"/"toughness" so a single untargeted atom can apply a
		// combined P/T buff AND keyword grant(s) to the cross-atom referent.
		_, hasPower := effectValue["power"]
		_, hasToughness := effectValue["toughness"]
		if len(kws) == 0 && !hasPower && !hasToughness {
			return nil
		}
		if gs.EffectManager == nil || source == nil || gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
			return nil
		}
		refID := gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"]
		if refID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		if _, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(refID); err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		if hasPower || hasToughness {
			gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
				Source:          source,
				SourceID:        source.ID,
				AffectsSpecific: refID,
				PowerMod:        getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 0),
				ToughnessMod:    getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 0),
				UntilEOT:        true,
			})
		}
		for _, kw := range kws {
			gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
				Source:          source,
				SourceID:        source.ID,
				AffectsSpecific: refID,
				GrantKeyword:    kw,
				UntilEOT:        true,
			})
		}
		return nil
	case "destroy_delayed_referent":
		// destroy_delayed_referent primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-destroy-delayed-referent-aee71e6496, Mogg Cannon
		// class: "Target creature you control gets +1/+0 and gains flying
		// until end of turn. Destroy that creature at the beginning of the
		// next end step."). "That creature" is whatever card a PRECEDING
		// atom in this SAME sequence most recently acted on
		// (gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"], armed by effect_sequence.go's
		// noteSeqReferent), not this atom's own targets — deliberately
		// untargeted, mirroring grant_keywords_referent_eot's referent read
		// just above. Unlike executeExileTemporaryReturnEffect (a fixed
		// next-end-step delayed RETURN of an already-exiled card), the
		// referent here stays on the battlefield the whole time, so this
		// schedules a delayed DESTROY instead: a self-contained,
		// self-guarding EventEndStep closure (a local "destroyed" flag
		// makes every firing after the first a no-op) so it only ever fires
		// once, matching Rule 603.7's one-shot delayed trigger. No-op if no
		// referent was armed or the referent can't be found on the
		// battlefield right now.
		if gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
			return nil
		}
		refID := gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"]
		if refID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		if card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(refID); err != nil || card == nil {
			return nil
		}
		destroyed := false
		gs.EventBus.Subscribe(EventEndStep, func(ev *Event) {
			if destroyed {
				return
			}
			destroyed = true
			_ = DestroyTarget(Target{IsCard: true, CardID: refID}, gs)
		})
		return nil
	case "exile_referent_at_next_end_step":
		// exile_referent_at_next_end_step primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-exile-referent-at-next-end-step-109a50b307, Stone
		// Idol Trap class: "Create a 6/12 colorless Construct artifact
		// creature token with trample. Exile it at the beginning of your
		// next end step."). "It" is whatever card a PRECEDING atom in this
		// SAME sequence most recently acted on (gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"],
		// armed by effect_sequence.go's noteSeqReferent — including the
		// token a create_token atom just put onto the battlefield), not
		// this atom's own targets — same untargeted referent-read shape as
		// destroy_delayed_referent just above. Unlike that case (a delayed
		// DESTROY straight to the graveyard), this is a permanent, one-way
		// EXILE with no return clause, so it goes through
		// gs.ExileFromBattlefield (the same helper channelExileTarget uses
		// for its own immediate exile) once the delayed trigger fires,
		// wrapped in the same self-guarding one-shot EventEndStep closure.
		// No-op if no referent was armed or the referent can't be found on
		// the battlefield right now.
		if gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
			return nil
		}
		refID := gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"]
		if refID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		if card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(refID); err != nil || card == nil {
			return nil
		}
		exiled := false
		gs.EventBus.Subscribe(EventEndStep, func(ev *Event) {
			if exiled {
				return
			}
			exiled = true
			_ = gs.ExileFromBattlefield(refID)
		})
		return nil
	case "grant_keywords_until_eot":
		// "X gains KEYWORD (and KEYWORD) until end of turn" — plural-keyword
		// variant (schema-v2 3c round 4: 123 records were parser-emitted with
		// zero consuming code). Recipients, in priority order: explicit
		// targets → affects.enchanted (the permanent this Aura is attached
		// to, stashed by the converter as affects_filter) → affects group
		// (creature/permanent you control) → the source itself (the common
		// "{cost}: ~ gains flying until end of turn" self-shape).
		kws := keywordListFromValue(effectValue)
		if len(kws) == 0 || gs.EffectManager == nil {
			return nil
		}
		grant := func(cardID string) {
			for _, kw := range kws {
				gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
					Source:          source,
					SourceID:        source.ID,
					AffectsSpecific: cardID,
					GrantKeyword:    kw,
					UntilEOT:        true,
				})
			}
		}
		if len(targets) > 0 {
			// pump_dynamic_creature_count_until_eot primitive (ticket
			// engine.auto-pump-dynamic-creature-count-until-eot-d32219cf1e,
			// Treeguard Duo class: "until end of turn, target creature you
			// control gains vigilance and gets +X/+X, where X is the number of
			// creatures you control."). power/toughness land here already
			// resolved (plain ints, or folded from pt_amount_count by the
			// resolveDynamicAmountFull chokepoint before the effect switch) —
			// this targeted branch only ever granted keywords before, so a
			// same-atom P/T boost was silently dropped for the single explicit
			// target shape. Mirrors grant_keywords_referent_eot's hasPower/
			// hasToughness handling above, just applied to the explicit target
			// instead of the sequence referent.
			_, hasPower := effectValue["power"]
			_, hasToughness := effectValue["toughness"]
			for _, t := range targets {
				if t.IsCard && t.CardID != "" {
					grant(t.CardID)
					if hasPower || hasToughness {
						gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
							Source:          source,
							SourceID:        source.ID,
							AffectsSpecific: t.CardID,
							PowerMod:        getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 0),
							ToughnessMod:    getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 0),
							UntilEOT:        true,
						})
					}
				}
			}
			return nil
		}
		affects := getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_filter", "")
		// grant_keywords_until_eot_controlled_creatures primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-grant-keywords-until-eot-controlled-creatures-
		// be9ff5177e): a TRIGGERED ability's mass "creatures you control
		// gain KEYWORD until end of turn" clause (Essence of Antiquity: "...
		// creatures you control gain hexproof until end of turn") reaches
		// this executor as a plain EffectValue["target_filter"] ==
		// "all_creatures_you_control" sequence-atom key — converter.go's
		// EffectAtomSpec loop never stashes the affects_filter/
		// affects_controller pair AbilityToActivated stashes for
		// single-effect ACTIVATED abilities, so the mass shape had no way
		// to reach the existing "creature"/"you" branch below. Alias it
		// onto that SAME branch (affects_controller already defaults to
		// "you") instead of a parallel per-card loop, reusing the identical
		// filter-scoped ContinuousEffect path — self-shape (no
		// affects/target_filter) and single-target shape (explicit
		// targets, handled above) are untouched.
		if affects == "" && getStringValue(effectValue, "target_filter", "") == "all_creatures_you_control" {
			affects = "creature"
		}
		switch affects {
		case "enchanted", "equipped", "equipped_creature_and_equipment":
			if source.AttachedTo != "" {
				grant(source.AttachedTo)
				if affects == "equipped_creature_and_equipment" {
					grant(source.ID)
				}
			}
		case "creature", "permanent":
			// affects_subtype/affects_controller/affects_other (task-2366):
			// forwarded by converter.go's isRecipientAffects glue from the
			// ability's top-level Affects field, same as pump_all's
			// group-keyword-grant shape below, but previously never read
			// here — every subtype-qualified group grant ("Knights you
			// control gain double strike until end of turn") silently
			// widened to "every creature you control" instead. Filter-scoped
			// ContinuousEffect (not a per-card grant() loop) so the existing
			// AffectsSubtype/AffectsController/ExcludeSelf matching in
			// effectAffectsCard — the same code pump_all's keyword grants
			// already rely on — does the recipient check instead of a
			// hand-rolled reimplementation.
			affSubtype := getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_subtype", "")
			affController := getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_controller", "you")
			excludeSelf, _ := effectValue["affects_other"].(bool)
			// affects_token (task-2366): "creature TOKENS you control gain
			// KEYWORD until end of turn" (Ainok Strike Leader, King Darien
			// XLVIII, Ravenous Robots) — same forwarding as affects_subtype
			// above, read straight off the atom's own value map.
			affToken, _ := effectValue["affects_token"].(bool)
			// affects_counter_type (task-2418): "creatures you control with
			// [a/no] counter(s) on {it,them}" (Bulwark Ox, Tyrant Guard,
			// Chocobo Knights, Synchronized Charge). effectAffectsCard
			// already gates generically on ContinuousEffect.AffectsCounterType
			// ("any" sentinel = presence of any counter kind, mirrors the
			// STATIC grant_ability path's identical field, task 2370) — this
			// case just never forwarded it.
			affCounterType := getStringValue(effectValue, "affects_counter_type", "")
			// grant_valued_keyword_until_eot (ticket engine.auto-grant-
			// valued-keyword-until-eot-1312f3366b, Plague Nurse class):
			// "Each other creature you control with toxic gains toxic 1
			// until end of turn" — a VALUED keyword grant to a filtered
			// group, unlike the plain boolean/presence keyword grants the
			// loop below handles. Dispatches to the dedicated executor
			// above instead, since the granted value has to land on
			// Card.ToxicN directly rather than through a lazily-evaluated
			// filter effect (see that executor's doc). Absent
			// keyword_value (every existing boolean keyword grant: flying,
			// haste, reach, deathtouch, indestructible, ...) this is a
			// no-op and control falls straight through to the untouched
			// loop below.
			if kwValue := getIntValue(effectValue, "keyword_value", 0); kwValue != 0 {
				requiresKw := Keyword(strings.Title(strings.ToLower(getStringValue(effectValue, "requires_keyword", ""))))
				for _, kw := range kws {
					gs.executeGrantValuedKeywordFilteredUntilEOT(controller, source, kw, kwValue, requiresKw)
				}
				return nil
			}
			for _, kw := range kws {
				gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
					Source:             source,
					SourceID:           source.ID,
					GrantKeyword:       kw,
					AffectsFilter:      affects,
					AffectsSubtype:     affSubtype,
					AffectsController:  affController,
					ExcludeSelf:        excludeSelf,
					AffectsToken:       affToken,
					AffectsCounterType: affCounterType,
					UntilEOT:           true,
				})
			}
		default:
			grant(source.ID)
		}
		return nil
	case "grant_keyword_eot":
		// "Any number of target permanents you control gain KEYWORD until end
		// of turn." Used by Slickshot Show-Off's cast-from-exile trigger.
		// When explicit targets are provided they are used; otherwise the
		// effect falls back to all permanents the controller controls (the
		// "any number" clause includes choosing all).
		kwStr := getStringValue(effectValue, "keyword", "")
		if kwStr == "" || gs.EffectManager == nil {
			return nil
		}
		kw := Keyword(kwStr)
		if len(targets) > 0 {
			for _, t := range targets {
				if !t.IsCard {
					continue
				}
				card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID)
				if err != nil {
					continue
				}
				gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
					Source:          source,
					AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
					GrantKeyword:    kw,
					UntilEOT:        true,
				})
			}
		} else {
			for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
				if card.Controller == controller {
					gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
						Source:          source,
						AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
						GrantKeyword:    kw,
						UntilEOT:        true,
					})
				}
			}
		}
		return nil

	case "grant_keyword_plus_dynamic_pt_until_eot":
		// grant_keyword_plus_dynamic_pt_until_eot primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-grant-keyword-plus-dynamic-pt-until-eot-bdb265d199,
		// Skittering Cicada class): grants a fixed keyword AND a symmetric
		// +X/+X in the SAME ContinuousEffect (built by
		// buildGrantKeywordPlusDynamicPTEffect), so both expire together at
		// end of turn and repeated triggers stack independently instead of
		// overwriting one another.
		if gs.EffectManager == nil {
			return nil
		}
		if ce := gs.buildGrantKeywordPlusDynamicPTEffect(controller, source, effectValue, stackObj.EventCard, targets); ce != nil {
			gs.EffectManager.Register(ce)
		}
		return nil
	case "animate_self":
		// "This permanent becomes a P/T creature until end of turn." Used by
		// manlands (Mishra's Foundry, Mutavault, Blinkmoth Nexus, etc.).
		// Sets AnimatedAsCreature on the source card and registers a Layer-7b
		// SetPower/SetToughness ContinuousEffect marked UntilEOT. The
		// CleanupEndOfTurnEffects path resets AnimatedAsCreature when the
		// effect expires.
		if source != nil && gs.EffectManager != nil {
			power := getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 0)
			toughness := getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 0)
			source.AnimatedAsCreature = true
			gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
				Source:             source,
				SourceID:           source.ID,
				AffectsSpecific:    source.ID,
				SetPower:           &power,
				SetToughness:       &toughness,
				UntilEOT:           true,
				GrantsCreatureType: true,
			})
		}
		return nil
	case "counter_unless_discard":
		// Reality Smasher: counter the targeting spell unless its
		// controller discards a card. Prompt the player to choose which
		// card to discard; if they have none, counter the spell.
		if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard {
			return nil
		}
		spellID := targets[0].SpellID
		if spellID == "" {
			spellID = targets[0].CardID
		}
		spellObj, err := gs.Stack.GetByID(spellID)
		if err != nil || spellObj == nil {
			return nil
		}
		spellOwner := spellObj.Controller
		owner := gs.Players[spellOwner]
		if owner.Hand.Count() > 0 {
			candidates := make([]string, 0, owner.Hand.Count())
			for _, c := range owner.Hand.GetAll() {
				candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
			}
			sourceName := ""
			if source != nil {
				sourceName = source.Name
			}
			gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
				PlayerIdx:      spellOwner,
				ChoiceType:     "hand_discard",
				Candidates:     candidates,
				Count:          1,
				SourceName:     sourceName,
				Optional:       true,
				CounterSpellID: spellID,
			}
			return nil
		}
		// No card to discard → counter the spell.
		return gs.CounterSpell(spellID)
	case "exile_until_leaves":
		// Static-Prison-style: exile a target permanent until source
		// leaves the battlefield. We exile it; on source's leave we
		// look up the StashedExileIDs slice on the source card and put
		// each exiled card back where it came from (battlefield → its
		// owner's battlefield).
		if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard {
			return nil
		}
		if victim, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(targets[0].CardID); err == nil {
			gs.Battlefield.RemoveCard(victim.ID)
			gs.UnregisterCardAbilities(victim)
			if victim.Owner >= 0 && victim.Owner < len(gs.Players) {
				gs.Players[victim.Owner].Exile.AddCard(victim)
			}
			// Track for later return when source leaves.
			if source != nil {
				source.StashedExileIDs = append(source.StashedExileIDs, victim.ID)
			}
			return nil
		}
		// task-2535 (verb_unmapped:exile): "exile a [nonland] card from [a
		// player's] hand until ~ leaves the battlefield" (Deep-Cavern Bat
		// class) — the targeted card isn't on the battlefield, so fall back
		// to searching every player's hand. Tracked on a separate list from
		// StashedExileIDs because the return destination differs (hand, not
		// battlefield) — see returnStashedExiledFromHandCards.
		for _, p := range gs.Players {
			victim, err := p.Hand.RemoveCard(targets[0].CardID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			p.Exile.AddCard(victim)
			if source != nil {
				source.StashedExileFromHandIDs = append(source.StashedExileFromHandIDs, victim.ID)
			}
			return nil
		}
		return nil
	case "exile_linked_to_source":
		// exile_linked_to_source primitive (ticket #3467, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:exile / "exile a card from your hand face down" +
		// static_unmapped "you may look at cards exiled with ~" +
		// verb_unmapped:bounce "return a card exiled with ~ to its owner's
		// hand" — Bane Alley Broker class). Reuses the SAME Card.ExiledBySource
		// name-tag already established for cast-linked exile
		// (cardfns.TagExiledBySource / SourceExilePlayGrants, task-1610 The
		// Temporal Anchor): any later "look at" or "return to hand" ability
		// can find every card this source exiled by scanning a zone for
		// ExiledBySource == source.Name, without a new per-source ID list.
		// Set directly here (not via cardfns.TagExiledBySource) since
		// cardfns imports game and the reverse import would cycle.
		if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard || source == nil {
			return nil
		}
		for _, p := range gs.Players {
			victim, err := p.Hand.RemoveCard(targets[0].CardID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			victim.ExiledBySource = source.Name
			p.Exile.AddCard(victim)
			return nil
		}
		return nil
