	case "exile_top_may_play":
		// "Exile the top N cards of your library. You may play that/those
		// card(s) this turn." (Count on Luck / Laelia, the Blade Reforged /
		// Moria Marauder class): a bounded, same-turn sibling of the Gonti/
		// Praetor's Grasp ExilePlay grant (game/cast_from_exile.go). Unlike
		// exile_top_cast_self (Stormchaser's Talent, cast-only), this sets
		// AllowLand so an exiled land card can be PLAYED too, matching the
		// "play" (not "cast") Oracle wording this shape always uses.
		n := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
		player := gs.Players[controller]
		cards, err := player.Library.LookAtTop(n)
		if err != nil || len(cards) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		ids := make([]string, len(cards))
		for i, c := range cards {
			ids[i] = c.ID
		}
		for _, id := range ids {
			c, err := player.Library.RemoveCard(id)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			player.Exile.AddCard(c)
			c.ExilePlay = &ExilePlayGrant{
				Controller:     controller,
				AllowLand:      true,
				UntilEndOfTurn: !getBoolValue(effectValue, "indefinite", false),
				GrantedTurn:    gs.TurnCount,
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "exile_library_top":
		// "Exile the top N cards of your library[ face down]." / "...of each
		// opponent's library." / "...of each player's library." (task-2363,
		// verb_unmapped:exile) — a bare zone move, NO play permission (see
		// registry_exile_library.go). Mirrors executeMillEffect's
		// controller-scope resolution, Exile instead of Graveyard.
		// chosen-player target round (ticket #3075, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:exile / "exile the top"): "Exile the top card of
		// target player's/opponent's library" (Circu, Dimir Lobotomist /
		// Court of Locthwain) names a single CHOSEN player rather than a
		// controller-scope keyword ("you"/"each opponent"/"each player").
		// A player target on the atom is honored ahead of the "who"
		// fallback, mirroring look_top_library's target-vs-who resolution
		// (the chosen referent selects whose library top is the exiled
		// card — the chosen_card_referent primitive for the exile verb).
		n := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
		var playerIdxs []int
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard && t.PlayerIdx >= 0 && t.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
				playerIdxs = append(playerIdxs, t.PlayerIdx)
			}
		}
		if len(playerIdxs) == 0 {
			switch getStringValue(effectValue, "who", "you") {
			case "each_opponent":
				for i := range gs.Players {
					if i != controller {
						playerIdxs = append(playerIdxs, i)
					}
				}
			case "each_player":
				for i := range gs.Players {
					playerIdxs = append(playerIdxs, i)
				}
			default:
				playerIdxs = []int{controller}
			}
		}
		for _, pi := range playerIdxs {
			if pi < 0 || pi >= len(gs.Players) {
				continue
			}
			player := gs.Players[pi]
			for i := 0; i < n; i++ {
				card, err := player.Library.DrawCard()
				if err != nil {
					break
				}
				player.Exile.AddCard(card)
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "control_opponent_next_turn":
		// Emrakul / Mindslaver / Worst Fears: queue Mindslaver-style
		// control on the named opponent's next turn. If a target was
		// chosen, use that PlayerIdx; otherwise default to the
		// caster's first opponent.
		var oppIdx int
		picked := false
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard && t.PlayerIdx >= 0 && t.PlayerIdx != controller {
				oppIdx = t.PlayerIdx
				picked = true
				break
			}
		}
		if !picked {
			for i := range gs.Players {
				if i != controller {
					oppIdx = i
					picked = true
					break
				}
			}
		}
		if picked && oppIdx >= 0 && oppIdx < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.Players[oppIdx].PendingControl = controller
		}
		return nil
	case "grant_extra_turn":
		// Emrakul, the Promised End: "After that turn, that player takes
		// an extra turn." Queue one extra turn on the same target
		// opponent — same target-selection logic as control_opponent_next_turn.
		var oppIdx int
		picked := false
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard && t.PlayerIdx >= 0 && t.PlayerIdx != controller {
				oppIdx = t.PlayerIdx
				picked = true
				break
			}
		}
		if !picked {
			for i := range gs.Players {
				if i != controller {
					oppIdx = i
					picked = true
					break
				}
			}
		}
		if picked && oppIdx >= 0 && oppIdx < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.Players[oppIdx].PendingExtraTurns++
		}
		return nil
	case "extra_turn":
		// "Take an extra turn after this one." — registered v2 executor
		// (ticket 2633, Time Walk / Beacon of Tomorrows class), the
		// triggered/activated flavor (Time Sieve, Timestream Navigator).
		// value.who selects the recipient: "you" (default) queues on the
		// ability's controller; "target_player" queues on the resolved
		// player target (falling back to the controller if none was
		// selected — never on an arbitrary opponent, unlike Emrakul's
		// grant_extra_turn above, whose card text names the opponent).
		// value.count queues that many turns. Consumed by NextTurn
		// (gamestate.go) one at a time per Rule 500.7.
		recipient := controller
		if who, ok := effectValue["who"].(string); ok && who == "target_player" {
			for _, t := range targets {
				if !t.IsCard && t.PlayerIdx >= 0 && t.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
					recipient = t.PlayerIdx
					break
				}
			}
		}
		count := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 1)
		if count < 1 {
			count = 1
		}
		if recipient >= 0 && recipient < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.Players[recipient].PendingExtraTurns += count
		}
		return nil
	case "transform_self":
		// Flip a double-faced permanent (Rule 711). Counters/damage/
		// tapped state/attachments persist; face-scoped state swaps.
		if source != nil {
			gs.TransformCard(source)
			// Westvale Abbey style: "Transform this land, then untap it."
			if effectValue != nil {
				if untap, ok := effectValue["untap_after"].(bool); ok && untap {
					source.Untap()
				}
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "exile_self_return_transformed":
		// Ajani-style: exile this permanent, then immediately return it to
		// the battlefield as its back face under its owner's control (Rule
		// 400.7, 603.6a). Unlike transform_self, this is a zone change —
		// the object is exiled (losing counters/attachments) and a new
		// game object enters, triggering ETBs.
		if source == nil || source.BackFace == nil {
			return nil
		}
		ownerIdx := source.Owner
		if ownerIdx < 0 || ownerIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		// Step 1: exile — remove from battlefield.
		gs.Battlefield.RemoveCard(source.ID)
		gs.UnregisterCardAbilities(source)
		gs.Players[ownerIdx].Exile.AddCard(source)
		// Step 2: flip to back face before re-entering.
		incomingIsPlaneswalker := source.BackFace.Type == TypePlaneswalker
		incomingStartingLoyalty := source.BackFace.StartingLoyalty
		pending := snapshotFace(source)
		applyFace(source, source.BackFace)
		source.BackFace = pending
		source.FaceUp = "back"
		if incomingIsPlaneswalker && incomingStartingLoyalty > 0 {
			source.Counters.Loyalty = incomingStartingLoyalty
		}
		// Step 3: return to battlefield under owner's control.
		gs.Players[ownerIdx].Exile.RemoveCard(source.ID) //nolint:errcheck
		source.Controller = ownerIdx
		source.SummoningSick = true
		gs.Battlefield.AddCard(source)
		gs.RegisterCardAbilities(source)
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			triggered := gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateETBEvent(source, ownerIdx))
			for _, ability := range triggered {
				gs.PutTriggeredAbilityOnStack(ability)
			}
		}
		gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
		return nil
	case "search":
		// Activated search ability (fetchland-style). Read the filter
		// + destination from EffectValue and open a PendingSearch so the
		// player can choose which matching card to take.
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		filter := getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "")
		destination := getStringValue(effectValue, "to", "hand")
		player := gs.Players[controller]
		candidates := []string{}
		if filter == "named_card" {
			names := getStringSliceValue(effectValue, "names")
			for _, c := range player.Library.GetAll() {
				for _, name := range names {
					if strings.EqualFold(c.Name, name) {
						candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
						break
					}
				}
			}
		} else if filter == "named_self" {
			// task-2425 (verb_unmapped:search): "search your library for a
			// card named ~" (Avarax) — self-ref '~' is normalized ahead of
			// map_atom ever seeing the text (same as the "~'s power" idiom),
			// so there's no literal name string to carry in the record;
			// resolve it against the ability's own source card instead.
			if source != nil {
				for _, c := range player.Library.GetAll() {
					if strings.EqualFold(c.Name, source.Name) {
						candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
					}
				}
			}
		} else {
			for _, c := range player.Library.GetAll() {
				if matchesActivatedSearchFilter(c, filter) {
					candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
				}
			}
		}
		if len(candidates) == 0 {
			player.Library.Shuffle()
			return nil
		}
		sourceName := ""
		if source != nil {
			sourceName = source.Name
		}
		// count > 1 (e.g. "up to two basic land cards") opens a multi-pick
		// search via Remaining; optional marks "up to" searches decline-able.
		//
		// task-2425 (verb_unmapped:search): "up to X <filter> cards, where X
		// is <count>" (Boundless Realms, Jaheira's Respite) carries its count
		// as value.amount_count instead of a literal "count" int — the SAME
		// closed count-spec vocabulary every other dynamic-amount verb uses.
		// ExecuteAbilityEffect's resolveDynamicAmountEv chokepoint (called
		// before this switch dispatches) has already folded that spec into a
		// concrete "amount" by the time this case runs, so "amount"'s
		// presence (not its value) is what marks this a dynamic-count search.
		// "up to X" legitimately resolves to 0 (e.g. zero lands controlled)
		// — that's an honest empty search, not the "count<1 -> clamp to 1"
		// default a MISSING count gets.
		count := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 1)
		dynamicCount := false
		if _, ok := effectValue["amount"]; ok {
			count = getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0)
			dynamicCount = true
		}
		if dynamicCount && count < 1 {
			player.Library.Shuffle()
			return nil
		}
		if count < 1 {
			count = 1
		}
		optional := false
		if v, ok := effectValue["optional"].(bool); ok {
			optional = v
		}
		remaining := 0
		if count > 1 {
			remaining = count
		}
		gs.PendingSearch = &PendingSearch{
			PlayerIdx:   controller,
			Filter:      filter,
			Destination: destination,
			Candidates:  candidates,
			Optional:    optional,
			SourceName:  sourceName,
			Remaining:   remaining,
		}
		return nil
	case "reveal_choose":
		// "Look at/Reveal the top N cards of your library. Put [one of them |
		// a <type> card from among them] into your hand / onto the battlefield
		// [tapped], [and] the rest [into your graveyard / on the bottom of
		// your library [in any order]]." (Firja, Nymris, Sea Gate Oracle,
		// Satyr Wayfinder, Cavalier of Thorns, Shigeki, ...). Triggered/
		// activated sibling of the SPELL-path reveal_choose SpellEffect
		// (gamestate.go -> startRevealChoice): the SAME PendingSearch reveal-
		// N-choose-one flow (gamestate_tutor.go startRevealChoiceFor), reading
		// count/filter/to/rest straight off the atom's effectValue instead of
		// a SpellEffect. Single pick only — multi-pick ("put two of them into
		// your hand") is rejected at the parse layer, never reaching here.
		// (batch-merge 2026-08-04: 1984's startRevealChoiceFor call and
		// 2372's startRevealChooseValue both landed here — running BOTH
		// opened two pending choices back-to-back and corrupted the
		// candidate set. startRevealChooseValue reads every field off the
		// value map, superset of the For variant — it is the one kept.)
		// Triggered/activated counterpart of the spell-only reveal_choose
		// path (gamestate.go applySpellEffectCore -> startRevealChoice):
		// "Look at/Reveal the top N cards of your library. Put [a/one/all
		// matching] of them into X and the rest into Y" (Firja Judge of
		// Valor, Sea Gate Oracle, Cavalier of Thorns, Satyr Wayfinder, ...).
		// Added by the put_zone re-parse family (task 2372) — the original
		// implementation only ever ran from a resolving spell's SpellEffect
		// struct, so this idiom was unreachable from triggered/activated
		// bodies. Reuses the exact same PendingSearch/RevealedIDs/RestZone
		// machinery via startRevealChooseValue (gamestate_tutor.go).
		sourceName := ""
		if source != nil {
			sourceName = source.Name
		}
		gs.startRevealChooseValue(controller, sourceName, effectValue)
		return nil
	case "reveal_top_n_split_filter":
		// "Reveal the top N cards of your library. Put all <filter> cards
		// from among them onto the battlefield tapped and the rest on the
		// bottom of your library in a random order." (Animist's Awakening,
		// ...). No player choice — every revealed card is routed by the
		// printed filter alone, so this completes synchronously without
		// opening a PendingSearch (startRevealTopNSplitFilter,
		// gamestate_tutor.go). The non-choose sibling of the "reveal_choose"
		// case above, which parks a PendingSearch for the player to pick.
		count := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 1)
		filter := getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "")
		gs.startRevealTopNSplitFilter(controller, count, filter)
		return nil
	case "search_lands_to_battlefield":
		// Primeval Titan etc.: "you may search your library for up to N
		// land cards, put them onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffle."
		// Opens a PendingSearch so the player chooses which lands to fetch.
		n := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 1)
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		if n <= 0 {
			return nil
		}
		player := gs.Players[controller]
		candidates := []string{}
		for _, c := range player.Library.GetAll() {
			if c.IsLand() {
				candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
			}
		}
		if len(candidates) == 0 {
			player.Library.Shuffle()
			return nil
		}
		sourceName := ""
		if source != nil {
			sourceName = source.Name
		}
		gs.PendingSearch = &PendingSearch{
			PlayerIdx:   controller,
			Filter:      "land",
			Destination: "battlefield_tapped",
			Candidates:  candidates,
			Optional:    true,
			SourceName:  sourceName,
			Remaining:   n,
		}
		return nil
	case "exile_top_cast":
		// Ragavan-style: exile the top card of the opponent's library and grant
		// the attacker's controller permission to cast it this turn (normal cost).
		if len(gs.Players) < 2 {
			return nil
		}
		oppIdx := 1 - controller
		if oppIdx < 0 || oppIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		opp := gs.Players[oppIdx]
		topCards, err := opp.Library.LookAtTop(1)
		if err != nil || len(topCards) == 0 {
			return nil // Opponent's library is empty
		}
		topCard := topCards[0]
		if _, err := opp.Library.RemoveCard(topCard.ID); err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		// Place in the attacker-controller's exile so they can cast it.
		gs.Players[controller].Exile.AddCard(topCard)
		gs.Players[controller].CastableExiledCards = append(
			gs.Players[controller].CastableExiledCards, topCard)
		return nil
	case "exile_top_cast_self":
		// Stormchaser's Talent level 3: exile the top card of the controller's
		// own library and grant may-cast permission for that card this turn.
		player := gs.Players[controller]
		topCards, err := player.Library.LookAtTop(1)
		if err != nil || len(topCards) == 0 {
			return nil // Library is empty; no-op
		}
		topCard := topCards[0]
		if _, err := player.Library.RemoveCard(topCard.ID); err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		player.Exile.AddCard(topCard)
		player.CastableExiledCards = append(player.CastableExiledCards, topCard)
		return nil
	case "exile_nonland_from_opponent_hand":
		// Thought-Knot Seer ETB: "Target opponent reveals their hand;
		// you choose a nonland card from it and exile that card."
		// Auto-picks the first nonland — UI prompt for the actual choice
		// can come later.
		oppIdx := -1
		for i := range gs.Players {
			if i != controller {
				oppIdx = i
				break
			}
		}
		if oppIdx < 0 || oppIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		opp := gs.Players[oppIdx]
		var pickID string
		for _, c := range opp.Hand.GetAll() {
			if !c.IsLand() {
				pickID = c.ID
				break
			}
		}
		if pickID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		c, err := opp.Hand.RemoveCard(pickID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		opp.Exile.AddCard(c)
		return nil
	case "exile_nonbattlefield_zone":
		// exile_nonbattlefield_zone primitive (ticket #3467, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:exile / "exile a nonland card from [a player's]
		// hand" family — Deep-Cavern Bat/Elite Spellbinder/Kellan Joins Up
		// class). Unlike exile_nonland_from_opponent_hand above (which
		// auto-picks the first nonland card), this opens a real
		// PendingChoice — same idiom as "hand_discard"
		// (gamestate_choice.go) — so the controller picks WHICH nonland
		// card to exile, optionally capped by mana value
		// (EffectValue["max_mv"], Kellan Joins Up's "mana value 3 or
		// less") and optionally sourced from the controller's OWN hand
		// (EffectValue["self"] == true) instead of the default opponent's
		// hand. EffectValue["until_leaves"] == true ties the choice back
		// to source via StashedExileFromHandIDs, reusing the SAME delayed
		// return already wired for the pre-targeted "exile_until_leaves"
		// hand branch (returnStashedExiledFromHandCards).
		handOwner := controller
		if selfHand, _ := effectValue["self"].(bool); !selfHand {
			handOwner = -1
			for i := range gs.Players {
				if i != controller {
					handOwner = i
					break
				}
			}
		}
		if handOwner < 0 || handOwner >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		maxMV := -1
		if v, ok := effectValue["max_mv"].(float64); ok {
			maxMV = int(v)
		}
		destGraveyard, _ := effectValue["dest_graveyard"].(bool)
		zoneOwner := gs.Players[handOwner]
		candidates := make([]string, 0, zoneOwner.Hand.Count())
		for _, c := range zoneOwner.Hand.GetAll() {
			if c.IsLand() {
				continue
			}
			if maxMV >= 0 && c.ManaCost.CMC() > maxMV {
				continue
			}
			candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
		}
		if len(candidates) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		sourceName := ""
		untilLeavesSourceID := ""
		if source != nil {
			sourceName = source.Name
			if untilLeaves, _ := effectValue["until_leaves"].(bool); untilLeaves {
				untilLeavesSourceID = source.ID
			}
		}
		gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
			PlayerIdx:                controller,
			ChoiceType:               "exile_nonland_from_hand",
			Candidates:               candidates,
			Count:                    1,
			SourceName:               sourceName,
			Optional:                 true,
			ExileFromHandZone:        handOwner,
			ExileUntilLeavesSourceID: untilLeavesSourceID,
			HandChoiceDestGraveyard:  destGraveyard,
		}
		return nil
	case "exile_referent_from_zone":
		// exile_referent_from_zone primitive (ticket #3722, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:exile / capability #7): "You choose a nonland card
		// from it or a card from their graveyard. Exile that card." —
		// Agonizing Remorse class. "That card" is whatever card a
		// PRECEDING atom in this SAME ability already identified
		// (gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"], armed by effect_sequence.go's
		// noteSeqReferent), sitting in a player's hand or graveyard — not
		// a fresh choice (exile_nonbattlefield_zone) and not a
		// battlefield permanent (the registered "exile" verb, Rule
		// 701.15a). The referent's origin zone isn't tracked separately,
		// so every player's hand is checked before every player's
		// graveyard. EffectValue["until_leaves"] == true optionally ties a
		// HAND-origin referent back to source via the SAME
		// StashedExileFromHandIDs/returnStashedExiledFromHandCards path
		// exile_nonbattlefield_zone already uses; no printed card in this
		// family needs a graveyard-origin "until leaves" return, so that
		// combination stays a permanent exile.
		if gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
			return nil
		}
		refID := gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"]
		if refID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		untilLeaves := getBoolValue(effectValue, "until_leaves", false)
		for i := range gs.Players {
			p := gs.Players[i]
			if c, err := p.Hand.RemoveCard(refID); err == nil {
				p.Exile.AddCard(c)
				if untilLeaves && source != nil {
					source.StashedExileFromHandIDs = append(source.StashedExileFromHandIDs, refID)
				}
				return nil
			}
		}
		for i := range gs.Players {
			p := gs.Players[i]
			if c, err := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(refID); err == nil {
				p.Exile.AddCard(c)
				return nil
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "reveal_hand_choose_discard":
		// reveal_hand_choose_discard primitive (ticket #4635, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:? / "Target player reveals their hand and you choose
		// a card [of that color] from it. That player discards that card." —
		// Addle class): unlike discard_nonland_from_opponent_hand below
		// (which auto-picks), this opens a real PendingChoice so the
		// ability's CONTROLLER picks which revealed card is discarded;
		// the pick is then discarded BY the hand's owner into their own
		// graveyard (ResolvePendingChoice "discard_chosen_by_controller").
		// EffectValue["color"] ("W"/"U"/"B"/"R"/"G") narrows the revealed
		// candidate set to cards of that color (Addle's "a card of that
		// color"); absent, every card in hand is a candidate.
		handOwner := -1
		if len(targets) > 0 && !targets[0].IsCard && targets[0].PlayerIdx >= 0 && targets[0].PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
			handOwner = targets[0].PlayerIdx
		} else {
			for i := range gs.Players {
				if i != controller {
					handOwner = i
					break
				}
			}
		}
		if handOwner < 0 || handOwner >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		colorFilter, _ := effectValue["color"].(string)
		owner := gs.Players[handOwner]
		candidates := make([]string, 0, owner.Hand.Count())
		for _, c := range owner.Hand.GetAll() {
			if colorFilter != "" && !c.HasColor(Color(colorFilter)) {
				continue
			}
			candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
		}
		if len(candidates) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		sourceName := ""
		if source != nil {
			sourceName = source.Name
		}
		gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
			PlayerIdx:           controller,
			ChoiceType:          "discard_chosen_by_controller",
			Candidates:          candidates,
			Count:               1,
			SourceName:          sourceName,
			DiscardFromHandZone: handOwner,
		}
		return nil
	case "discard_referent_cards":
		// discard_referent_cards primitive (ticket #3732, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:? / "that player discards that card", capability #8):
		// a SEQUENCE atom that consumes the card(s) established by an earlier
		// atom in the same sequence (gs.ReferentCardIDs) and the referent
		// player it names (gs.ReferentPlayer), and makes THAT player discard
		// THAT card into their OWN graveyard (Rule 701.8a) — the Addle class.
		// This is the "that player discards that card" clause, distinct from
		// the self/opponent/each_player "discard" effect and from whole-hand
		// discards.
		if gs.ReferentPlayer < 0 || gs.ReferentPlayer >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		for _, id := range gs.ReferentCardIDs {
			if err := gs.Discard(gs.ReferentPlayer, id); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		gs.ReferentCardIDs = nil
		return nil
	case "discard_nonland_from_opponent_hand":
		// Temporal Intervention: "Target opponent reveals their hand. You choose
		// a nonland card from it. That player discards that card."
		// Auto-picks the first nonland — UI prompt for the actual choice
		// can come later.
		oppIdx := -1
		for i := range gs.Players {
			if i != controller {
				oppIdx = i
				break
			}
		}
		if oppIdx < 0 || oppIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		opp := gs.Players[oppIdx]
		var pickID string
		for _, c := range opp.Hand.GetAll() {
			if !c.IsLand() {
				pickID = c.ID
				break
			}
		}
		if pickID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		c, err := opp.Hand.RemoveCard(pickID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		opp.Graveyard.AddCard(c)
		return nil
	case "exile_hand_each_opponent_may_play":
		// Lightstall Inquisitor ETB: "each opponent exiles a card from their hand
		// and may play that card for as long as it remains exiled. Each spell cast
		// this way costs {1} more to cast. Each land played this way enters tapped."
		// This is an MVP implementation: auto-picks the first card from each opponent's
		// hand and exiles it. The actual "may play" mechanic with cost increases and
		// tapped lands is handled by the game state's cast logic when needed.
		for i := range gs.Players {
			if i == controller {
				continue
			}
			opp := gs.Players[i]
			if len(opp.Hand.GetAll()) == 0 {
				continue
			}
			// Auto-pick the first card (UI can enhance this later)
			var pickID string
			if cards := opp.Hand.GetAll(); len(cards) > 0 {
				pickID = cards[0].ID
			}
			if pickID == "" {
				continue
			}
			c, err := opp.Hand.RemoveCard(pickID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			opp.Exile.AddCard(c)
		}
		return nil
	case "shuffle_graveyard_to_library_bottom":
		// Endurance: "you may shuffle any number of target players'
		// graveyards into their libraries." Targets[0] selects the player.
		idx := controller
		if len(targets) > 0 && !targets[0].IsCard {
			idx = targets[0].PlayerIdx
		}
		if idx >= 0 && idx < len(gs.Players) {
			p := gs.Players[idx]
			cards := p.Graveyard.GetAll()
			// Random ordering — for determinism we just iterate the slice
			// as-is; rules-wise the order doesn't matter for any current
			// trigger.
			for _, c := range cards {
				p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(c.ID)
				p.Library.AddCardToBottom(c)
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "each_player_shuffles_graveyard":
		// Emrakul LTB: each player shuffles their graveyard into their library.
		for _, p := range gs.Players {
			cards := p.Graveyard.GetAll()
			for _, c := range cards {
				p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(c.ID)
				p.Library.AddCardToBottom(c)
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "put_land_from_hand":
		// "You may put a land card from your hand onto the battlefield tapped."
		// Opens a PendingSearch so the player can choose which land (or decline).
		//
		// value["basic"]=true narrows it to BASIC lands only (Elvish Pioneer:
		// "you may put a basic land card from your hand onto the battlefield
		// tapped"). Parameterised rather than given its own effect name: a
		// separate put_basic_land_from_hand_tapped was authored once and, being
		// absent from this switch, silently did nothing — the card looked fine
		// and never worked. Narrowing Candidates is authoritative because
		// gamestate_tutor.go rejects any choice outside that list.
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		basicOnly := false
		if effectValue != nil {
			basicOnly, _ = effectValue["basic"].(bool)
		}
		player := gs.Players[controller]
		candidates := []string{}
		for _, c := range player.Hand.GetAll() {
			if !c.IsLand() {
				continue
			}
			if basicOnly && !c.IsBasic {
				continue
			}
			candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
		}
		if len(candidates) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		sourceName := ""
		if source != nil {
			sourceName = source.Name
		}
		filter := "land"
		if basicOnly {
			filter = "basic_land"
		}
		gs.PendingSearch = &PendingSearch{
			PlayerIdx:   controller,
			Filter:      filter,
			Destination: "battlefield_tapped",
			Candidates:  candidates,
			Optional:    true,
			SourceName:  sourceName,
			Source:      "hand",
		}
		return nil
	case "sacrifice_self":
		// Phlage-style "sacrifice it unless it escaped" — the source
		// permanent is moved to its owner's graveyard. Gated by the
		// IfCondition (handled before this dispatch runs).
		if source != nil {
			gs.Battlefield.RemoveCard(source.ID)
			gs.UnregisterCardAbilities(source)
			if source.Owner >= 0 && source.Owner < len(gs.Players) {
				source.MarkEnteredGraveyardFromBattlefield(gs.TurnCount)
				gs.Players[source.Owner].Graveyard.AddCard(source)
			}
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateDiesEvent(source, controller))
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "shuffle_self_into_library":
		// "Shuffle ~ into its owner's library." (engine round 2026-08-08,
		// fleet verdict on ticket 2633 — the Beacon cycle: "Draw four
		// cards. Shuffle ~ into its owner's library."). Three honest
		// contexts, checked by where the source card actually is:
		//
		//  1. Ability source still on the battlefield (a permanent's
		//     activated/triggered ability): shuffle the permanent itself
		//     from the battlefield into its owner's library, with the same
		//     leave-the-battlefield cleanup executeBounceEffectTo does.
		//  2. Resolving instant/sorcery mid-sequence-walk: the card is in
		//     NO zone (ResolveTopOfStack popped it and places it only
		//     after the walk), so set Card.ShuffleSelfIntoLibraryOnResolve
		//     — the graveyard-routing point in ResolveTopOfStack consumes
		//     and clears it. A sequence that PAUSED (optional atom) has
		//     already routed the spell to the graveyard, so a resumed atom
		//     instead recovers it from its owner's graveyard directly.
		//  3. Anything else (permanent card no longer on the battlefield,
		//     token, exiled card, ...): no clean "shuffle self" semantics
		//     — return without action rather than guess (no
		//     silently-wrong; Rule 608.2b flavor: the object is gone).
		if source == nil {
			return nil
		}
		owner := gs.GetPlayer(source.Owner)
		if gs.Battlefield.HasCard(source.ID) {
			if owner == nil {
				return nil
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.RemoveCard(source.ID)
			if err != nil {
				return nil
			}
			gs.EffectManager.RemoveBySource(card)
			gs.UnregisterCardAbilities(card)
			// Leaving the battlefield: reset battlefield-only state, same
			// fields executeBounceEffectTo resets.
			card.Tapped = false
			card.SummoningSick = false
			card.Damage = 0
			card.Attacking = false
			card.Blocking = false
			owner.Library.AddCard(card)
			owner.Library.Shuffle() //nolint:errcheck
			return nil
		}
		if source.IsInstant() || source.IsSorcery() {
			// Resumed-after-pause sequence: the spell already reached its
			// owner's graveyard — shuffle it from there.
			if owner != nil && owner.Graveyard.HasCard(source.ID) {
				card, err := owner.Graveyard.RemoveCard(source.ID)
				if err != nil {
					return nil
				}
				owner.Library.AddCard(card)
				owner.Library.Shuffle() //nolint:errcheck
				return nil
			}
			// Mid-resolution (the normal Beacon shape): mark for the
			// after-resolution placement site in ResolveTopOfStack.
			source.ShuffleSelfIntoLibraryOnResolve = true
			return nil
		}
		// Context 3: documented no-op.
		return nil
	case "level_up":
		// Class level activation. Mana cost was already paid by the
		// activated-ability framework; here we just advance the level
		// and fire the becomes-level-N trigger.
		target := getIntValue(effectValue, "target_level", 0)
		if source != nil {
			return gs.bumpClassLevel(source, controller, target)
		}
		return nil
	case "scry":
		// "Scry N" (schema-v2 3c round 6: 50 records + 2 cardfns emitters
		// resolved into the silent default). Keep-all auto-complete via the
		// existing keyword-action APIs — deterministic-order convention as
		// documented on cardfns.ScryN; fires EventScry via CompleteScry.
		n := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
		if result, err := gs.Scry(controller, n); err == nil && result != nil {
			ids := make([]string, 0, len(result.Cards))
			for _, c := range result.Cards {
				ids = append(ids, c.ID)
			}
			return gs.CompleteScry(controller, ids, nil)
		}
		return nil
	case "surveil":
		// "Surveil N" triggered/activated shape (schema-v2 3c round 6: 60 of
		// 69 records hit the silent default; only the spell path via
		// Card.SurveilN worked). Opens the same PendingSurveil flow the spell
		// path uses — ResolveSurveilChoice tolerates a nil Stashed object.
		n := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
		player := gs.GetPlayer(controller)
		if player == nil || n <= 0 {
			return nil
		}
		revealed := []string{}
		for i := 0; i < n && player.Library.Count() > 0; i++ {
			top := player.Library.Cards[0]
			player.Library.Cards = player.Library.Cards[1:]
			player.Exile.AddCard(top)
			revealed = append(revealed, top.ID)
		}
		if len(revealed) > 0 {
			gs.PendingSurveil = &PendingSurveil{
				PlayerIdx:   controller,
				RevealedIDs: revealed,
				SourceName:  source.Name,
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "fabricate":
		// "Fabricate N" (Rule 702.122) — controller picks +1/+1 counters or
		// Servo tokens via PendingOptionChoice; see game/fabricate.go.
		return gs.executeFabricate(source, controller, effectValue)
	case "grant_keyword_choice_eot":
		// grant_keyword_choice_until_eot primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-grant-keyword-choice-until-eot-e7d7628ca9,
		// REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:grant_eot / "This creature gains your
		// choice of flying, deathtouch, or lifelink until end of turn"
		// family — Assassin Initiate class): the ability's controller
		// chooses EXACTLY ONE keyword from the fixed list carried in
		// effectValue["keywords"], and only that chosen keyword is granted
		// to the source until end of turn. Deliberately distinct from
		// grant_keywords_until_eot, which grants EVERY listed keyword
		// together — that would misrepresent the modal choice. Recipient
		// follows the same priority as grant_keywords_until_eot (explicit
		// card target, else the source itself for the common "~ gains ..."
		// self-shape). The choice rides the SAME single-chooser flow
		// (PendingOptionChoice / ResolveOptionChoice) fabricate uses.
		kws := keywordListFromValue(effectValue)
		if len(kws) == 0 || gs.EffectManager == nil || source == nil {
			return nil
		}
		recipientID := source.ID
		if len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard && targets[0].CardID != "" {
			recipientID = targets[0].CardID
		}
		sourceName := source.Name
		options := make([]string, len(kws))
		for i, kw := range kws {
			options[i] = string(kw)
		}
		gs.StartOptionChoice(controller, options, sourceName,
			func(gs *GameState, chosen string) {
				if _, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(recipientID); err != nil {
					return
				}
				gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
					Source:          source,
					SourceID:        source.ID,
					AffectsSpecific: recipientID,
					GrantKeyword:    Keyword(chosen),
					UntilEOT:        true,
				})
			})
		return nil
	case "put_counter_or_permanent_keyword_choice":
		// put_counter_or_permanent_keyword_choice primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-put-counter-or-permanent-keyword-choice-5fb5795ba1,
		// REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:put_counters / Nature's Blessing class:
		// "Put a +1/+1 counter on target creature or that creature gains
		// banding, first strike, or trample."): a single targeted modal
		// choice between a +1/+1 counter on the target and exactly ONE
		// keyword from effectValue["keywords"], granted to that SAME target
		// PERMANENTLY (no UntilEOT — Rule 611.2c "lasts indefinitely"),
		// unlike grant_keyword_choice_eot just above, which grants its pick
		// only until end of turn. The two branches are flattened into ONE
		// StartOptionChoice list ("+1/+1 counter" plus every keyword)
		// instead of a nested choose-mode-then-choose-keyword flow, since
		// PendingOptionChoice only supports a single flat pick.
		kws := keywordListFromValue(effectValue)
		if len(kws) == 0 || gs.EffectManager == nil || source == nil {
			return nil
		}
		if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard || targets[0].CardID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		targetID := targets[0].CardID
		const putCounterModeOption = "+1/+1 counter"
		modeOptions := make([]string, 0, len(kws)+1)
		modeOptions = append(modeOptions, putCounterModeOption)
		for _, kw := range kws {
			modeOptions = append(modeOptions, string(kw))
		}
		gs.StartOptionChoice(controller, modeOptions, source.Name,
			func(gs *GameState, chosen string) {
				target, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(targetID)
				if err != nil || target == nil {
					return
				}
				if chosen == putCounterModeOption {
					gs.AddCounters(target, CounterPlusOnePlusOne, 1)
					gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
					return
				}
				gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
					Source:          source,
					SourceID:        source.ID,
					AffectsSpecific: targetID,
					GrantKeyword:    Keyword(chosen),
				})
			})
		return nil
	case "mill":
		return gs.executeMillEffect(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "mill_gain_life_per_creature":
		return gs.executeMillGainLifePerCreature(controller, effectValue)
	case "discard":
		return gs.executeDiscardEffect(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "target_player_draw_discard":
		return gs.executeTargetPlayerDrawDiscard(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "edict_sacrifice":
		// "[target/each] opponent sacrifices N [filter] permanents of their
		// choice" — the Diabolic Edict / Innocent Blood family (single
		// opponent; each-player / that-player variants stay unmapped). Mirrors
		// annihilator's choice-vs-auto-sacrifice shape, filter-scoped via
		// matchesPermanentFilter. See executeEdictSacrifice. `targets` also
		// threads through (task-2456) for the no-"target"-scope-word shape:
		// a real "target player" or an event_player referent.
		return gs.executeEdictSacrifice(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "annihilator_sacrifice":
		// Rule 702.86a: defending player sacrifices N permanents of their choice.
		return gs.executeAnnihilatorSacrifice(controller, effectValue)
	case "sacrifice":
		// "[you] sacrifice a/another/N [filter] permanent(s)" — the
		// ability's own controller chooses among their matching permanents
		// (Eldrazi Monument, Korvold, Wilhelt the Rotcleaver, ...). See
		// executeSacrificeFiltered.
		if unlessFilter, ok := effectValue["unless_filter"].(string); ok && unlessFilter != "" {
			// "sacrifice it unless you sacrifice a [filter] permanent" —
			// conditional self-sacrifice escape valve (Scythe Tiger class,
			// ticket engine.auto-sacrifice-unless-sacrifice-d9e71faebd). See
			// executeSacrificeUnlessSacrifice.
			return gs.executeSacrificeUnlessSacrifice(controller, source, effectValue)
		}
		return gs.executeSacrificeFiltered(controller, source, effectValue)
<<<<<<< HEAD

	case "sacrifice_self_unless_sacrifice_filtered":
		// "sacrifice this [source] unless you sacrifice a [filter]
		// permanent" (Endless Wurm class). Distinct from plain "sacrifice"
		// above: the controller isn't picking among precomputed candidates,
		// they're choosing between two DIFFERENT sacrifices — the fixed
		// source, or an alternate permanent matching FilterType — with the
		// source surviving only in the latter case. Deferred to
		// ResolveSacrificeSelfUnlessFilteredChoice.
		return gs.executeSacrificeSelfUnlessFiltered(controller, source, effectValue)

	case "sacrifice_any_number":
		// "sacrifice any number of [filter] permanents" (Scapeshift: "Sacrifice
		// any number of lands.") — unlike the fixed-N "sacrifice" case above,
		// the controller freely picks the sacrificed amount from zero up to
		// every matching permanent. Reuses the "sacrifice_keep_rest" resolver
		// (ResolvePendingChoice/resolveSacrificeKeepRestChoice): asking the
		// controller to choose up to Count candidates to KEEP already accepts
		// anywhere from 0 to Count kept, so setting Count to the full
		// candidate count makes "keep K of N" express "sacrifice 0..N",
		// without touching the shared resolver.
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		filter, _ := effectValue["filter"].(string)
		excludeSelf, _ := effectValue["exclude_self"].(bool)
		var anyNumCandidates []string
		for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.Cards {
			if c.Controller != controller || !matchesPermanentFilter(c, filter) {
				continue
			}
			if excludeSelf && source != nil && c.ID == source.ID {
				continue
			}
			anyNumCandidates = append(anyNumCandidates, c.ID)
		}
		if len(anyNumCandidates) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		anyNumSourceName := ""
		if source != nil {
			anyNumSourceName = source.Name
		}
		gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
			PlayerIdx:  controller,
			ChoiceType: "sacrifice_keep_rest",
			Candidates: anyNumCandidates,
			Count:      len(anyNumCandidates),
			SourceName: anyNumSourceName,
		}
		return nil
	case "sacrifice_source":
		// "sacrifice this artifact/creature/permanent" — always the
		// ability's own source, never a choice among other matching
		// permanents (Monkey Cage class: "sacrifice this artifact and
		// create X ... tokens"). Distinct from "sacrifice" above, which
		// lets the controller choose among filtered candidates elsewhere
		// on the battlefield.
		if source == nil {
			return nil
		}
		if _, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(source.ID); err != nil {
			// Source already left the battlefield (e.g. sacrificed or
			// destroyed by an earlier effect in the same resolution) —
			// nothing left to sacrifice.
			return nil
		}
		gs.sacrificeCard(source, controller)
		return nil

	case "damage_count_of_color":
		// damage_count_of_color primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-damage-count-of-color-fed721efbb, Searing Rays class:
		// "Choose a color. ~ deals damage to each player equal to the number
		// of creatures of that color that player controls."). No interactive

		// via chooseColorForProtectionByPlayer's idiom (most-represented

	case "conditional_counter_by_shared_color_with_control":

	case "copy":
		// "Copy target instant or sorcery spell[ you control]." (Rule 706.10)
		// — triggered/activated ability tier. See game/copy_effect.go.
		// task-2512: an explicit "amount" (Myojin of Cryptic Dreams' "...three
		// times") repeats the copy per target; unset defaults to 1.
		amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
		return gs.executeCopyEffect(controller, targets, amount)
	case "counter_unless_pay":
		manaCost := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0)
		sourceName := ""
		if source != nil {
			sourceName = source.Name
		}
		for _, target := range targets {
			if target.SpellID == "" {
				continue
			}
			spellObj, err := gs.Stack.GetByID(target.SpellID)
			if err != nil || spellObj == nil {
				continue
			}
			spellOwner := spellObj.Controller
			if !gs.DebugMode && !gs.Players[spellOwner].ManaPool.CanPay(ManaCost{Generic: manaCost}) {
				gs.CounterSpell(target.SpellID) //nolint:errcheck
				continue
			}
			gs.PendingUnlessPay = &PendingUnlessPay{
				PlayerIdx:  spellOwner,
				ManaCost:   manaCost,
				SpellID:    target.SpellID,
				SourceName: sourceName,
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "tap", "tap_target":
		// tap_target is a Speed-1 spelling variant (schema-v2 3c round 11).
		// target_attached (task-3188): "tap enchanted permanent" — mirrors
		// executePutCounterEffect's/executeTokenCopyEffect's AttachedTo
		// back-reference convention (task-1979).
		return gs.executeTapEffect(source, effectValue, targets)
	case "fight", "creature_fights_target":
		// "~ fights target creature" (Rule 701.12, schema-v2 3c round 11:
		// 15 records were inert — the earlier grep hit only EventFight).
		// The source fights the first card target via the existing gs.Fight.
		// creature_fights_target (ticket #4664, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:fight / capability #99) is a spelling alias of the
		// same Rule 701.12 keyword action — both combatants deal damage equal
		// to their power to each other simultaneously — so it routes through
		// this identical case rather than a duplicate executor. Distinct from
		// creature_deals_power_damage (#49, one-way, no retaliation).
		if source != nil && len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard && targets[0].CardID != "" {
			return gs.Fight(source.ID, targets[0].CardID)
		}
		return nil
	case "fight_referent":
		// fight_referent primitive (ticket #3961, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:fight / capability #61): "it fights up to one target
		// creature defending player controls" — the fight's SUBJECT is not
		// this ability's own source but the card a PRECEDING atom in this
		// SAME sequence established (gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"], armed by
		// executeEffectSequenceEv's per-atom bookkeeping in
		// effect_sequence.go's noteSeqReferent). Mirrors
		// grant_keywords_referent_eot's referent read; unlike "fight" above
		// (which hardcodes the source as one combatant) this one takes the
		// referent as combatant one and the atom's OWN first card target as
		// combatant two. No referent bound (or vanished from the
		// battlefield) means the fight can't happen — no-op, never falling
		// back to the source, so the existing source-fight path and
		// two-independent-target fights stay untouched.
		if gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
			return nil
		}
		refID := gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"]
		if refID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		if _, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(refID); err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		for _, t := range targets {
			if t.IsCard && t.CardID != "" && t.CardID != refID {
				if _, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID); err != nil {
					return nil
				}
				return gs.Fight(refID, t.CardID)
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "tap_with_stun_counter":
		// auto_tap_with_stun_counter_1c66e799e6 primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-tap-with-stun-counter-1c66e799e6, Frostfist Strider
		// class): "Tap target creature an opponent controls and put a stun
		// counter on it." A single sentence, two effects on the SAME
		// referent — tapping plus a one-shot stun counter (Rule 701.45a/
		// 613.3b: a stun counter causes the next untap to be skipped and
		// the counter removed instead) — so this executes both against the
		// same target rather than requiring two separate effect atoms.
		// Distinct case name from "tap"/"tap_all" so plain tap stays a bare
		// tap with no counter side effect. effectValue["amount"] (put_stun_
		// counters, Tranquilize class) lets this same combo primitive put
		// N stun counters instead of the fixed single counter.
		return gs.executeTapWithStunCounterEffect(targets, effectValue)
	case "tap_all":
		return gs.executeTapAllEffect(controller, source, effectValue)
	case "untap":
		return gs.executeUntapEffect(targets)
	case "tap_or_untap_target_choice":
		// choose_tap_or_untap_target primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-choose-tap-or-untap-target-79d61adb7e, Mind Over Matter
		// class): "You may tap or untap target artifact, creature, or land."
		// Distinct case name from "tap"/"untap" so those unconditional
		// single-outcome effects stay untouched; the controller's choice
		// between the two mutually exclusive outcomes is captured in
		// EffectValue["mode"], the same key executeTapUntapSharedCreatureTypeReferent
		// above reads for its own tap/untap battlefield sweep.
		return gs.executeTapOrUntapChoiceTarget(targets, effectValue)
	case "tap_or_untap":
		// auto_tap_or_untap_choice_target_21af274c97 primitive (Sewer-
		// veillance Cam class): "you may tap or untap target creature."
		// Same mutually-exclusive choice semantics as the
		// "tap_or_untap_target_choice" case above (EffectValue["mode"]
		// selects tap vs untap) — a distinct case name because this is the
		// effect key upstream tap-or-untap emitters (e.g. the ETB "may tap
		// or untap target creature" pattern) already use, rather than
		// splitting into two separate always-both-execute effects.
		return gs.executeTapOrUntapChoiceTarget(targets, effectValue)
	case "tap_choose_untapped_creatures_you_control":
		// auto_tap_choose_untapped_creatures_you_control_34de4bacb5
		// primitive (Tidal Terror class): "tap two other untapped
		// creatures you control" as a non-targeted, resolution-time
		// choice — see executeTapChooseUntappedCreaturesYouControl's doc
		// for why this is distinct from the Target-based tap cases above.
		return gs.executeTapChooseUntappedCreaturesYouControl(controller, source, effectValue)
	case "gain_control":
		// "Gain control of target creature[...][ until end of turn]." plus
		// optional untap/keyword riders bundled into the same atom (task-
		// 1998) — see executeGainControlEffect's doc for why the riders
		// can't ride their own sequence atoms.
		// ticket engine.auto-target-filter-power-leq-source-counters-8912357856
		// (Legacy's Allure class): "target creature with power less than or
		// equal to the number of <counter_type> counters on this
		// enchantment" — a DYNAMIC per-source threshold, unlike the static
		// numeric massFilterCmpRe buckets matchesPermanentFilter already
		// supports (gamestate.go's "_power_lte_N" suffix bakes N in at parse
		// time; this instead reads source.Counters live). Re-checked here
		// against LIVE state immediately before the steal, mirroring
		// executeDestroyPowerToughnessUnequalTargetEffect's target-loop skip
		// convention — an illegal target at resolution is silently skipped
		// (Rule 608.2b), not stolen. Static power thresholds and the
		// controller/ownership riders (owned_by_you, target_player_controls,
		// opponent_controls) above are untouched by this branch.
		if ct, ok := effectValue["power_leq_source_counters"].(string); ok && ct != "" && source != nil {
			filtered := make([]Target, 0, len(targets))
			for _, t := range targets {
				if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
					continue
				}
				card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID)
				if err != nil || card == nil || !card.IsCreature() {
					continue
				}
				if card.GetPowerWithEffects(gs.EffectManager) <= source.Counters.GetCount(CounterType(ct)) {
					filtered = append(filtered, t)
				}
			}
			targets = filtered
		}
		return gs.executeGainControlEffect(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "gain_control_referent_eot":
		// auto_gain_control_until_eot_1270729a25 primitive: "gain control
		// of it until end of turn" as a STANDALONE untargeted sequence atom
		// whose subject "it" refers to whatever card a PRECEDING atom in
		// this same sequence most recently acted on (gs.seqSlotsStr[
		// "referent"], armed by executeEffectSequenceEv's per-atom
		// bookkeeping in effect_sequence.go's noteSeqReferent). Needed by
		// the Goatnapper class — "untap target Goat and gain control of it
		// until end of turn" — where the single sentence carries TWO
		// targets, so the reparse emits the steal as its own atom that can
		// not carry the ability's single target. The steal is until end of
		// turn: the pre-steal controller is snapshotted before the transfer
		// and a one-shot EventCleanup subscription reverts control (the
		// same snapshot/steal/EventCleanup-revert skeleton as the
		// until_eot branch of executeGainControlEffect in
		// control_effects.go). Deliberately a control-change EOT effect,
		// not a keyword grant: no ContinuousEffect is registered.
		if gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
			return nil
		}
		refID := gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"]
		if refID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(refID)
		if err != nil || card == nil {
			return nil
		}
		owner := card.Controller
		if err := gs.GainControl(controller, card.ID); err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			reverted := false
			gs.EventBus.Subscribe(EventCleanup, func(e *Event) {
				if reverted {
					return
				}
				reverted = true
				if gs.Battlefield.HasCard(refID) {
					_ = gs.GainControl(owner, refID)
				}
			})
		}
		return nil
	case "gain_control_until_eot":
		// auto_gain_control_until_eot_3342d6fc4b primitive (Insurrection
		// class): mass filtered control-steal that always reverts control
		// to each original controller at the next cleanup — see
		// executeGainControlFilteredUntilEotEffect's doc.
		return gs.executeGainControlFilteredUntilEotEffect(controller, effectValue)
	case "untap_referent":
		// auto_untap_referent_target_b538048b69 primitive (Bloody Betrayal
		// class: "Gain control of target creature until end of turn. Untap
		// that creature. ..."). The untap is a STANDALONE untargeted
		// sequence atom whose subject "that creature" refers to whatever
		// card a PRECEDING atom in this same sequence most recently acted
		// on (gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"], armed by executeEffectSequenceEv's
		// per-atom bookkeeping in effect_sequence.go's noteSeqReferent).
		// Deliberately left untargeted so it can sit between the
		// referent-establishing atom (gain_control, etc.) and a later
		// referent-consuming atom (grant_keywords_referent_eot) without
		// breaking either. Mirrors gain_control_referent_eot's referent
		// read above; the untap itself mirrors executeUntapEffect's
		// Card.Untap + EventUntapped pair. No-op if no referent is armed
		// or the referent can no longer be found on the battlefield.
		if gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
			return nil
		}
		refID := gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"]
		if refID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(refID)
		if err != nil || card == nil {
			return nil
		}
		card.Untap()
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventUntapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
		}
		return nil
	case "untap_all":
		return gs.executeUntapAllEffect(controller, source, effectValue)
	case "control_enchanted":
		// "You control enchanted permanent" - Aura gives controller
		return gs.executeControlEnchantedEffect(source, controller)
	case "enchanted_doesnt_untap":
		// "Enchanted permanent doesn't untap during its controller's untap step"
		return gs.executeEnchantedDoesntUntapEffect(source)
	case "untap_self":
		// Untap the source card (used by Endbringer's untap trigger)
		if source != nil {
			source.Untap()
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventUntapped).WithSource(source).WithPlayer(source.Controller))
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "tap_self":
		// Tap the source permanent in place, no target (task-2383, mirrors
		// untap_self). e.g. Tiger-Seal "At the beginning of your upkeep, tap
		// this creature."
		if source != nil {
			source.Tap()
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventTapped).WithSource(source).WithPlayer(source.Controller))
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesTapped).WithSource(source).WithPlayer(source.Controller))
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "regenerate":
		// "{cost}: Regenerate this creature/~." (Rule 701.16/614.9), self-only
		// shape (patternManaRegenerateSelf / patternSacrificeTypeRegenerateSelf,
		// cards/patterns_complex.go — both emit Value{"target_self": true}).
		// Was parse-but-inert: GameState.Regenerate (replacement.go) already
		// implemented the shield mechanic and was unit-tested by poking
		// Card.RegenerationShields directly, but nothing in this switch ever
		// called it, so activating a printed "Regenerate ~" ability (Lotleth
		// Troll, Goblin Turncoat, ...) did nothing (found + fixed 2026-08-09,
		// docs/phase-rs-cross-reference-plan.md Track A round 1).
		if source != nil {
			return gs.Regenerate(source.Controller, source.ID)
		}
		return nil
	case "manifest_dread":
		// "Manifest dread." (Rule 701.62a): look at the top two cards of your
		// library, put one onto the battlefield face down as a 2/2 creature
		// (ManifestCardOntoBattlefield), put the other into your graveyard.
		// Grandfathered v1 string with NO prior executor at all (found while
		// auditing docs/phase-rs-cross-reference-plan.md's grandfather list —
		// biggest real gap found, 17 corpus cards). Peels the top two cards
		// into Exile the same way startSurveil does (gamestate_tutor.go) so
		// they're addressable while the player picks; ChoiceType
		// "manifest_dread" resolves in ResolvePendingChoice
		// (gamestate_choice.go). Only one card in library -> manifest it
		// directly, no choice needed (nothing left to discard). Zero cards ->
		// no-op.
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		player := gs.Players[controller]
		revealed := []string{}
		for i := 0; i < 2 && player.Library.Count() > 0; i++ {
			top := player.Library.Cards[0]
			player.Library.Cards = player.Library.Cards[1:]
			player.Exile.AddCard(top)
			revealed = append(revealed, top.ID)
		}
		switch len(revealed) {
		case 0:
			return nil
		case 1:
			c, err := player.Exile.RemoveCard(revealed[0])
			if err == nil {
				gs.ManifestCardOntoBattlefield(controller, c)
			}
			return nil
		default:
			name := "Manifest Dread"
			if source != nil {
				name = source.Name
			}
			gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
				PlayerIdx:  controller,
				ChoiceType: "manifest_dread",
				Candidates: revealed,
				Count:      1,
				SourceName: name,
			}
			return nil
		}
	case "amass":
		// Amass [Type] N (Rule 701.42, operator round ticket-2911): "Put N
		// +1/+1 counters on an Army you control. It's also a [Type]. If you
		// don't control an Army, create a 0/0 black [Type] Army creature
		// token first." Rides both the triggered/activated ability tier
		// (dispatched here) and the v2 spell-sequence walker
		// (effect_sequence.go's executeSequenceAtomEv routes every sequence
		// atom through ExecuteAbilityEffect), so "Amass Type N." works as a
		// standalone spell atom exactly like it does inside a trigger body.
		return gs.executeAmassEffect(controller, effectValue)
	case "remove_counter":
		// "{cost}: Remove [a/N] [TYPE] counter(s) from ~." (Dark Depths-class,
		// Rule 122.3). Self-only, matching the only clean corpus shape.
		// Grandfathered v1 string with NO executor at all before this round
		// (remove_counter_damage, a distinct compound, already had one) —
		// found auditing docs/phase-rs-cross-reference-plan.md's grandfather
		// list.
		if source == nil {
			return nil
		}
		counterType := getStringValue(effectValue, "counter_type", "+1/+1")
		amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
		if amount < 1 {
			amount = 1
		}
		source.Counters.RemoveCounter(CounterType(counterType), amount)
		return nil
	case "prevent_combat_damage_this_turn":
		// "Prevent all combat damage that would be dealt this turn." (Fog-
		// class, Rule 615.1/615.6). Universal (every creature and player,
		// not just this ability's controller), combat-only, unlimited,
		// until-end-of-turn shield. Grandfathered v1 string with NO
		// executor at all before this round —
		// CreateUnlimitedPreventionShield / CreateDamagePreventionShieldWithContext /
		// CreateSourceOnlyDamagePreventionShield (game/replacement.go)
		// already existed, fully wired on the CONSUMPTION side
		// (PreventDamageWithContext, called from combat.go/targeting.go),
		// but nothing ever CREATED a shield via any of them (found
		// auditing docs/phase-rs-cross-reference-plan.md's demand table —
		// same parse-but-inert shape as regenerate's missing grant side).
		// Built directly (AnyTarget has no existing named constructor)
		// rather than reusing CreateSourceOnlyDamagePreventionShield("",
		// ...), which sets Unlimited+AnyTarget correctly but has no
		// CombatOnly parameter.
		gs.ReplacementManager.Register(&ReplacementEffect{
			Type:       ReplacementDamagePrevention,
			AnyTarget:  true,
			CombatOnly: true,
			Unlimited:  true,
			UntilEOT:   true,
		})
		return nil
	case "prevent_damage_this_turn_scoped":
		// Source-scoped/qualified prevention shields (ticket-3226,
		// verb_unmapped:prevent) that prevent_combat_damage_this_turn's
		// bare-universal shape doesn't cover: "prevent all combat damage
		// that would be dealt this turn by non-Spider creatures"
		// (Arachnogenesis — effectValue["exclude_subtype"]) and "prevent
		// all combat damage that would be dealt by it this turn"/"...this
		// creature would deal this turn" (Ignoble Soldier, Zealot il-Vec —
		// effectValue["self_source"], reusing the existing SourceOnlyID
		// field to restrict the shield to this ability's own source). Same
		// universal any-target/combat-only/unlimited/until-EOT shield as
		// prevent_combat_damage_this_turn otherwise, just narrowed by
		// whichever scope key is present.
		shield := &ReplacementEffect{
			Type:       ReplacementDamagePrevention,
			AnyTarget:  true,
			CombatOnly: true,
			Unlimited:  true,
			UntilEOT:   true,
		}
		if selfSource, _ := effectValue["self_source"].(bool); selfSource && source != nil {
			shield.SourceOnlyID = source.ID
		}
		if excl := getStringValue(effectValue, "exclude_subtype", ""); excl != "" {
			shield.SourceExcludeSubType = excl
		}
		gs.ReplacementManager.Register(shield)
		return nil
	case "exile_until_next_end_step":
		// "Exile [X]. Return that card to the battlefield under its
		// owner's control at the beginning of the next end step[, tapped]."
		// (Flickerwisp-class, Rule 603.7 delayed trigger). No generic
		// delayed-trigger primitive existed at all before this round —
		// Flickerwisp-class cards were only ever solved per-card via
		// hand-authored cardfns handlers (cardfns.Flickerwisp). Mirrors the
		// already-proven idiom cardfns.ExileUntilNextEndStep and
		// channelExileTarget both use (collect id+owner before the zone
		// mutation, drain on the first EventEndStep seen, "returned" bool
		// closure guard instead of a formal unsubscribe) — reimplemented
		// here rather than shared, since cardfns imports game and game
		// cannot import cardfns back (same reasoning channelExileTarget's
		// own doc comment gives).
		//
		// Targets the SAME card the ability's own earlier "exile" atom
		// targeted (game/effect_sequence.go's executeEffectSequenceEv
		// bypasses routeSeqTargets specifically for this effect name, since
		// this atom never carries its own TargetType marker — found via
		// TestShape_Activated_ExileUntilNextEndStep, see that fix's own
		// comment) — "the next end step" here means ANY player's, not
		// scoped to this ability's controller; a "your next end step"/
		// "your next upkeep" variant would need an e.Player check and has
		// no emitter yet.
		//
		// The card is looked up in EXILE, not the battlefield: this atom
		// always runs AFTER the sequence's own "exile" atom already moved
		// it there (that's the whole point of "return THAT CARD" — a
		// second try looking on the battlefield would always miss, since
		// the card just left it).
		//
		// Self-exile shape (Nezahal, Primal Tide: "Exile Nezahal." has no
		// chosen target at all — target_self reads source directly, so
		// targets[] is empty) falls back to source.ID: the Card pointer
		// stays valid across the zone move atom 1 already performed, so
		// source.ID still correctly identifies it. Without this fallback
		// the len(targets)==0 guard alone would silently no-op for every
		// self-exile card — found before this landed, not a live bug.
		cardID := ""
		if len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard {
			cardID = targets[0].CardID
		} else if source != nil {
			cardID = source.ID
		}
		if cardID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		var target *Card
		for _, p := range gs.Players {
			if c, err := p.Exile.GetCard(cardID); err == nil {
				target = c
				break
			}
		}
		if target == nil {
			return nil
		}
		id, owner := target.ID, target.Owner
		tapped := effectValue["tapped"] != nil
		returned := false
		gs.EventBus.Subscribe(EventEndStep, func(e *Event) {
			if returned {
				return
			}
			returned = true
			if _, err := gs.Players[owner].Exile.GetCard(id); err != nil {
				return
			}
			_ = gs.ReturnFromExile(owner, id, "battlefield")
			if c, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(id); err == nil && c != nil {
				c.Controller = owner
				if tapped {
					c.Tapped = true
				}
			}
		})
		return nil
	case "sacrifice_until_next_end_step":
		// "Sacrifice it at the beginning of the next end step." (Rule
		// 603.7 delayed trigger — Arms Race / Attack-in-the-Box /
		// Balduvian Dead / Balduvian Atrocity class, ticket #3193). The
		// inner action of this delayed trigger is sacrifice, for which
		// no generic executor existed: ability_effects.go had only the
		// immediate sacrifice_self / edict_sacrifice and the exile-only
		// exile_until_next_end_step above, and eotSacrificeIDs is a
		// Blitz/Encore-internal queue, not a registered effect. Mirrors
		// exile_until_next_end_step's proven idiom (collect the target
		// id, EventBus.Subscribe(EventEndStep, ...) with a self-guarding
		// "done" bool closure instead of a formal unsubscribe) — but
		// the card stays on the BATTLEFIELD until the end step (it is
		// not pre-moved to exile), and the closure sacrifices it via
		// GameState.Sacrifice if it is still there. "The next end step"
		// means ANY player's, not scoped to this ability's controller
		// (a "your next end step" variant would need an e.Player check
		// and has no emitter yet). game/effect_sequence.go bypasses
		// routeSeqTargets for this effect name specifically, the same
		// as exile_until_next_end_step, since "Sacrifice it" never
		// carries its own TargetType marker.
		//
		// Target resolution matches exile_until_next_end_step: a chosen
		// target wins, else source.ID for the self / "it = this
		// creature" shape (Attack-in-the-Box), else no-op. Vanished
		// target (Rule 608.2b): the closure's battlefield lookup returns
		// err and the sacrifice is silently skipped.
		cardID := ""
		if len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard {
			cardID = targets[0].CardID
		} else if source != nil {
			cardID = source.ID
		}
		if cardID == "" {
			return nil
		}
		done := false
		gs.EventBus.Subscribe(EventEndStep, func(e *Event) {
			if done {
				return
			}
			done = true
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(cardID)
			if err != nil {
				// Already left the battlefield some other way — nothing to do.
				return
			}
			_ = gs.Sacrifice(card.Controller, cardID)
		})
		return nil
	case "grant_color_until_eot":
		return gs.executeGrantColorUntilEOTEffect(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "grant_protection_from_chosen_color_until_eot":
		return gs.executeGrantProtectionFromChosenColorUntilEOTEffect(targets)
	case "exile_artifact_gy_create_construct":
		return gs.executeExileArtifactGYCreateConstruct(controller, targets)
	case "exile_graveyard":
		return gs.executeExileGraveyardEffect(targets)
	case "exile_target_players_graveyards":
		return gs.executeExileTargetPlayersGraveyardsEffect(targets)
	case "exile_each_opponents_graveyard":
		return gs.executeExileEachOpponentsGraveyardEffect(controller)
	case "pump_etb_creatures_this_turn":
		return gs.executePumpETBCreaturesThisTurnEffect(controller, source)
	case "pump_counter_lifelink_eot_attacking":
		return gs.executePumpCounterLifelinkEOTAttacking(controller, source, targets)
	case "pump_counters_flying_counter_angel_type_attacking":
		return gs.executePumpCountersFlyingCounterAngelTypeAttacking(controller, source, targets)
	case "copy_tokens_entered_this_turn":
		return gs.executeCopyTokensEnteredThisTurn(controller)
	case "self_copy_on_cast":
		if token, _ := effectValue["token"].(bool); token {
			return gs.executeSelfCopyOnCastTokenEffect(source, controller, effectValue)
		}
		return gs.executeSelfCopyOnCastEffect(source, controller, gs.resolveSelfCopyOnCastAmount(effectValue))
	case "cast_trigger_choose_target":
		return gs.executeCastTriggerChooseTarget(source)
	case "create_token_copy_of_trigger":
		return gs.executeCreateTokenCopyOfTrigger(controller, targets)
	case "return_graveyard_to_hand":
		return gs.executeReturnGraveyardToHand(controller, effectValue, targets)
	case "sacrifice_self_return_exiled":
		// Promise of Tomorrow class: sacrifice this source and return every
		// card previously exiled with it to the battlefield under its
		// controller's control.
		return gs.executeSacrificeSelfReturnExiled(source, controller)

	case "return_from_graveyard":
		// Zombify / Eternal Witness family (schema-v2 3c round 7: 103
		// records were parser-emitted with ZERO consuming code — the largest
		// parse-but-inert class found by the migration).
		return gs.executeReturnFromGraveyard(controller, source, effectValue, targets)
	case "add_mana":
		return gs.executeTriggeredAddManaEffect(controller, effectValue)
	case "investigate":
		// "Investigate." — create a Clue artifact token ("{2}, Sacrifice
		// this token: Draw a card.").
		return gs.executeTokenEffect(controller, map[string]interface{}{
			"token_type": "clue",
			"count":      getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 1),
		})
	case "become_monarch":
		// "You become the monarch." (Rule 718.2) — thin wrapper over the
		// existing BecomeMonarch primitive (game/monarch.go), already used
		// by the monarch end-step-draw/combat-damage global triggers; this
		// wires the effect-atom side (task 1919).
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return errors.New("invalid controller for become_monarch")
		}
		BecomeMonarch(gs, controller)
		return nil
	case "win_game":
		// "You win the game." — thin wrapper over WinTheGame
		// (game/advanced_abilities.go), which already gates on
		// Player.CantWin.
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return errors.New("invalid controller for win_game")
		}
		gs.WinTheGame(controller, "win_game effect")
		return nil
	case "ring_tempts":
		// "The Ring tempts you." (LOTR) — thin wrapper over RingTemptsPlayer
		// (game/ring_tempts.go), which already increments
		// Player.RingTemptedCount and fires EventRingTempts.
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return errors.New("invalid controller for ring_tempts")
		}
		gs.RingTemptsPlayer(controller)
		return nil
	case "add_experience_counter":
		// "You get an experience counter." Mirrors cardfns.AddExperience
		// (Player.ExperienceCounters, only ever accumulates) without the
		// cardfns import — game/ability_effects.go can't import cardfns
		// (cardfns already imports game), and this executor is at the same
		// rigor level as the existing gain_energy case, which also writes
		// the player counter directly with no prevention gate.
		amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
		if amount <= 0 {
			return nil
		}
		if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
			return errors.New("invalid controller for add_experience_counter")
		}
		gs.Players[controller].ExperienceCounters += amount
		return nil
	case "draw_and_exile_each_opponent":
		// Nicol Bolas, Dragon-God +1: "You draw a card. Each opponent
		// exiles a card from their hand or a permanent they control."
		// MVP: draw for controller, auto-exile first card from each opponent's hand
		if err := gs.executeDrawEffect(controller, map[string]interface{}{"draw": 1}, nil); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for i := range gs.Players {
			if i == controller {
				continue
			}
			opp := gs.Players[i]
			if len(opp.Hand.GetAll()) == 0 {
				continue
			}
			// Auto-pick first card from hand (can enhance with UI choice later)
			if c, ok := opp.Hand.GetAll()[0], true; ok {
				opp.Hand.RemoveCard(c.ID) //nolint:errcheck
				opp.Exile.AddCard(c)
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "loss_each_opponent_no_legendary":
		// Nicol Bolas, Dragon-God -8: "Each opponent who doesn't control
		// a legendary creature or planeswalker loses the game."
		for i := range gs.Players {
			if i == controller {
				continue
			}
			opp := gs.Players[i]
			hasLegendary := false
			for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
				if c.Controller != i {
					continue
				}
				if !c.IsPermanent() {
					continue
				}
				// Check for legendary creature
				if c.IsCreature() && c.HasSubType("Legendary") {
					hasLegendary = true
					break
				}
				// Check for legendary planeswalker
				if c.IsPlaneswalker() && c.HasSubType("Legendary") {
					hasLegendary = true
					break
				}
			}
			if !hasLegendary {
				opp.Life = 0
			}
		}
		return nil
	case "attach_equipment":
		// attach_equipment_effect primitive (ticket #3030, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:? / "attach it to <target>" self-referential
		// Equipment-attach family): "When this Equipment enters, attach it
		// to [up to one] target creature you control" (Assassin Gauntlet,
		// Baseball Bat class). The proven attach helper
		// (cardfns.AttachEquipmentTo, lib_v2.go) isn't reachable from this
		// v2 declarative dispatcher — cardfns already imports game, so
		// game importing cardfns back would cycle — so this reuses the
		// SAME targeted-pump plumbing the "pump"/"pump_until_eot" cases
		// above already wire (executePumpEffect) for the static equip
		// bonus, plus setting AttachedTo directly. MVP scope: a fresh
		// ETB/triggered attach where source has no prior attachment to
		// detach first (both blocked examples are "when this Equipment
		// enters" triggers).
		if source == nil || !source.IsEquipment() || len(targets) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		attachTarget, aerr := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(targets[0].CardID)
		if aerr != nil || !attachTarget.IsCreature() {
			return nil
		}
		source.AttachedTo = attachTarget.ID
		return gs.executePumpEffect(controller, source, map[string]interface{}{
			"power":     source.EquipPower,
			"toughness": source.EquipToughness,
		}, targets)
	default:
		// Unknown effect - log but don't fail
		return nil
	}
}

// ============================================================================
// INDIVIDUAL EFFECT IMPLEMENTATIONS
// ============================================================================

// ExecuteAbilityEffectDirect is the direct-call entry point into the same
// verb switch ExecuteAbilityEffect dispatches through (ticket #4671,
// REPARSE-MAP fixed_numeric_damage / capability 96, Annie Joins Up class).
// Sequence atoms reach the switch by wrapping themselves in a synthetic
// StackObject (game/effect_sequence.go executeAtom); this wrapper exposes
// that same construction to callers that hold an already-parsed
// (controller, source, effectValue, targets) tuple — e.g. reparse-emitted
// single-atom abilities — without going through the stack at all.
func (gs *GameState) ExecuteAbilityEffectDirect(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	obj := &StackObject{
		Card:        source,
		Controller:  controller,
		IsAbility:   true,
		Text:        getStringValue(effectValue, "effect", ""),
		EffectValue: effectValue,
		Targets:     targets,
	}
	return gs.ExecuteAbilityEffect(obj)
}

// executeTriggeredAddManaEffect adds mana from a triggered ability (e.g. ETB
// "add {W}{U}{B}{R}{G}"). Reads a "colors" slice or single "color" string from
// effectValue and adds amount (default 1) of each color to the controller's pool.
func (gs *GameState) executeTriggeredAddManaEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	player := gs.Players[controller]
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	if amount <= 0 {
		amount = 1
	}
	// ticket-2827 ("spend this mana" family, Abstract Paintmage/Geosurge/
	// Metamorphosis class): "restriction":"filter" is the SAME generic
	// RestrictedMana shape resolveAddManaEffect (the activated-ability mana
	// executor, activated_abilities.go) already supports — this dispatcher
	// is the one triggered abilities AND spell "effects" sequences share
	// (ExecuteAbilityEffect's "add_mana" case / executeSequenceAtomEv), so
	// without this branch a triggered/spell add_mana with a printed spend
	// restriction would silently add UNRESTRICTED mana instead — spendable
	// on anything, not just the printed X spells. Only "filter" is handled
	// here (not "eldrazi"/"artifact"/"chosen_creature_type" — no card in
	// the corpus needs those off a triggered/spell add_mana yet); add as
	// needed, following the exact same pattern as resolveAddManaEffect.
	restriction := getStringValue(effectValue, "restriction", "")
	addOne := func(color Color) {
		if restriction == "filter" {
			spellFilter := parseManaSpendFilterValue(effectValue["spell_filter"])
			abilityFilter := parseManaSpendFilterValue(effectValue["ability_filter"])
			player.ManaPool.AddRestrictedMana(color, spellFilter, abilityFilter)
			return
		}
		player.ManaPool.Add(color, 1)
	}
	// Multi-color: "colors": ["W","U","B","R","G"]
	if colors, ok := effectValue["colors"].([]interface{}); ok {
		for _, c := range colors {
			if s, ok := c.(string); ok {
				if color, ok := parseManaColor(s); ok {
					for i := 0; i < amount; i++ {
						addOne(color)
					}
				}
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	// Single color: "color": "G", or "color": "any" — the five-color choice
	// ("Add one mana of any color") that Branch of Vitu-Ghazi (ticket
	// #2134, [add_mana] reparse round) needs. No player-choice UI exists
	// at this call site, so MVP auto-picks White (same "auto-pick first
	// option" convention as draw_and_exile_each_opponent above).
	if colorStr, ok := effectValue["color"].(string); ok {
		if colorStr == "any" {
			if color, ok := parseManaColor("W"); ok {
				for i := 0; i < amount; i++ {
					addOne(color)
				}
			}
			return nil
		}
		if color, ok := parseManaColor(colorStr); ok {
			for i := 0; i < amount; i++ {
				addOne(color)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeGainEnergyEffect — "you get N {E}" (Rule 106.11). Adds energy
// counters to the controller of the source ability/spell.
func (gs *GameState) executeGainEnergyEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	if amount <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return errors.New("invalid controller for gain_energy")
	}
	gs.GainEnergy(controller, amount)
	return nil
}

// executePumpETBCreaturesThisTurnEffect handles Guide of Souls' may-pay rider:
// "each other creature that entered the battlefield under your control this
// turn gets a +1/+1 counter." Iterates the controller's CreaturesEnteredThisTurn
// list and adds a counter to every creature still on the battlefield except
// the ability's source (Guide of Souls itself).
func (gs *GameState) executePumpETBCreaturesThisTurnEffect(controller int, source *Card) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	for _, cardID := range gs.Players[controller].CreaturesEnteredThisTurn {
		if source != nil && cardID == source.ID {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(cardID)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		gs.AddCounters(card, CounterPlusOnePlusOne, 1)
	}
	return nil
}

// executePumpCounterLifelinkEOTAttacking handles Guide of Souls' attacks trigger:
// "put a +1/+1 counter on target attacking creature and it gains lifelink until
// end of turn." When explicit targets are provided they are used; otherwise the
// effect auto-targets all attacking creatures the controller controls.
func (gs *GameState) executePumpCounterLifelinkEOTAttacking(controller int, source *Card, targets []Target) error {
	var candidates []*Card
	if len(targets) > 0 {
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID)
			if err != nil || !card.IsCreature() {
				continue
			}
			candidates = append(candidates, card)
		}
	} else {
		for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetCreatures(controller) {
			if card.Attacking {
				candidates = append(candidates, card)
			}
		}
	}
	for _, card := range candidates {
		gs.AddCounters(card, CounterPlusOnePlusOne, 1)
		if gs.EffectManager != nil {
			gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
				Source:          source,
				AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
				GrantKeyword:    Lifelink,
				UntilEOT:        true,
			})
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executePumpCountersFlyingCounterAngelTypeAttacking handles Guide of Souls'
// newer attacks trigger: "put two +1/+1 counters and a flying counter on target
// attacking creature. It becomes an Angel in addition to its other types."
// Adds the counters permanently and appends "Angel" to the creature's SubTypes
// (idempotent: won't duplicate if the subtype is already present).
func (gs *GameState) executePumpCountersFlyingCounterAngelTypeAttacking(controller int, source *Card, targets []Target) error {
	var candidates []*Card
	if len(targets) > 0 {
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard {
				continue
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID)
			if err != nil || !card.IsCreature() {
				continue
			}
