			candidates = append(candidates, card)
		}
	} else {
		for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetCreatures(controller) {
			if card.Attacking {
				candidates = append(candidates, card)
			}
		}
	}
	for _, card := range candidates {
		gs.AddCounters(card, CounterPlusOnePlusOne, 2)
		gs.AddCounters(card, CounterFlying, 1)
		// "becomes an Angel in addition to its other types" — add permanently
		hasAngel := false
		for _, st := range card.SubTypes {
			if strings.EqualFold(st, "Angel") {
				hasAngel = true
				break
			}
		}
		if !hasAngel {
			card.SubTypes = append(card.SubTypes, "Angel")
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDrawEffect - "Draw N cards"
func (gs *GameState) executeDrawEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	if amount <= 0 {
		amount = 1
	}

	// draw_target_player primitive (ticket #3241, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:p_draw / "target player"/"that player" family): a
	// player-type target — either interactively chosen for "target player
	// draws a card" (Lore Weaver, Xira Arien; ActivatedAbility.Target is
	// already wired into stackObj.Targets) or bound via a target_ref
	// rewrite like event_player for "that player" (Archivist of Gondor's
	// monarch end-step trigger; see ExecuteAbilityEffect above) — overrides
	// the controller/opponent default below.
	for _, t := range targets {
		if !t.IsCard && t.PlayerIdx >= 0 && t.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
			return gs.DrawCardsWithEvent(t.PlayerIdx, amount)
		}
	}

	targetPlayer := controller
	if targetType, ok := effectValue["target"].(string); ok && targetType == "opponent" {
		targetPlayer = 1 - controller
	}
	return gs.DrawCardsWithEvent(targetPlayer, amount)
}

// executeDamageAdditionalToSubsetEffect implements the
// damage_additional_to_subset primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-damage-additional-to-subset-0177a098ca, Kaervek's Hex class):
// "~ deals X damage to each [filter] and an additional Y damage to each
// [subset]." Every subset member also matches the base filter per the
// Oracle text (every green creature Kaervek's Hex hits is also nonblack),
// so subset members take X+Y in ONE hit rather than two separate damage
// events. Mirrors executeDamageEffect's "all" mass-damage loop (same
// matchesPermanentFilter bucket vocabulary) but resolves the per-creature
// amount before dealing it instead of using one fixed amount for every
// match.
func (gs *GameState) executeDamageAdditionalToSubsetEffect(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0)
	if amount <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	additional := getIntValue(effectValue, "additional", 0)
	filter, _ := effectValue["filter"].(string)
	subsetFilter, _ := effectValue["subset_filter"].(string)
	for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if !matchesPermanentFilter(card, filter) {
			continue
		}
		dealAmount := amount
		if subsetFilter != "" && matchesPermanentFilter(card, subsetFilter) {
			dealAmount += additional
		}
		recipient := card
		if gs.EffectManager != nil {
			recipient = gs.EffectManager.RedirectDamageTarget(card)
		}
		recipient.DealDamage(dealAmount)
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateDamageEvent(source, recipient, dealAmount, controller))
		}
	}
	gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
	return nil
}

// executeDamageUnlessHandCountEffect implements the
// trigger_unless_hand_count_condition primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-trigger-unless-hand-count-condition-f7d59939b7, Skullcage
// class): "~ deals N damage to that player unless they have exactly three
// or exactly four cards in hand." Unlike
// executeDestroyTargetUnlessLifeAtLeast's resolution-time state check, this
// compares each target player's LIVE hand count (Player.Hand.Count())
// against every entry in EffectValue["hand_counts"] (ints from DSL
// construction or float64s from a JSON round-trip, mirroring getIntValue's
// own type-switch) — an exact match on ANY listed count suppresses the
// damage for that player only (Rule 601.2c partial-legality: other targets
// in the same batch resolve independently). A target with no matching hand
// count takes the damage exactly like the plain "damage" case.
func (gs *GameState) executeDamageUnlessHandCountEffect(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	var handCounts []int
	if raw, ok := effectValue["hand_counts"].([]interface{}); ok {
		for _, v := range raw {
			switch n := v.(type) {
			case int:
				handCounts = append(handCounts, n)
			case float64:
				handCounts = append(handCounts, int(n))
			}
		}
	}
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.IsCard || target.PlayerIdx < 0 || target.PlayerIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
			continue
		}
		player := gs.Players[target.PlayerIdx]
		suppressed := false
		handSize := player.Hand.Count()
		for _, n := range handCounts {
			if handSize == n {
				suppressed = true
				break
			}
		}
		if suppressed {
			continue
		}
		player.TakeDamage(amount)
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateLifeLossEvent(target.PlayerIdx, amount))
		}
	}
	gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
	return nil
}

// executeDamageEffect - "Deal N damage to target"
// countCreaturesWithKeywordControlledBy counts creatures the given
// controller controls that have the named keyword, evaluated fresh at call
// time rather than snapshotted — a damage amount reading "equal to the
// number of creatures you control with [keyword]" (Vent Sentinel class)
// must reflect the battlefield as it stands when the effect resolves.
func (gs *GameState) countCreaturesWithKeywordControlledBy(controller int, keyword Keyword) int {
	count := 0
	for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if card.Controller != controller || !card.IsCreature() {
			continue
		}
		if card.HasKeywordWithEffects(keyword, gs.EffectManager) {
			count++
		}
	}
	return count
}

func (gs *GameState) executeDamageEffect(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	// damage_amount_count_keyword (Vent Sentinel class): "~ deals damage to
	// target player or planeswalker equal to the number of creatures you
	// control with defender." amount_keyword_count carries the keyword NAME;
	// the count is recomputed here, at resolution, rather than at cast/
	// activation time — a fixed "amount" (no amount_keyword_count key) and
	// comparison-based amounts (source_power/source_toughness in
	// dynamic_amount.go) are untouched, since this only fires when the key
	// is present.
	if kw, ok := effectValue["amount_keyword_count"].(string); ok && kw != "" {
		amount = gs.countCreaturesWithKeywordControlledBy(controller, Keyword(kw))
	}
	// Damage-replacement modifiers (Furnace of Rath / Torbran). This is
	// DAMAGE — pure life-loss effects (executeLifeLossEffect) are NOT
	// modified, matching the rules distinction.
	amount = ModifyDamageAmount(gs.Battlefield, source, amount)
	// [VOCAB] #59: turn-scoped, controller-filtered EOTDamageMultipliers
	// (Isengard Unleashed class) layer on top here too, not just in combat —
	// this funnel is non-combat spell/ability damage, and the effect reads
	// "if a source you control would deal damage THIS TURN", not "combat
	// damage", so it must apply here as well.
	amount = gs.ApplyEOTDamageMultiplier(source, amount)

	// linked_amount tally (dynamic-amount round, 2026-08-08): total damage
	// this atom actually applies, for a later "you gain that much life"
	// consumer. Deferred so every return branch below reports; no-op
	// outside a sequence walk.
	dealt := 0
	defer func() { gs.noteSeqTally("damage_dealt", dealt) }()

	// Mass damage ("~ deals N damage to each creature[ and each player]" —
	// Pestilence/Volcanic Fallout class, task-1982): filter-scoped, not
	// target-scoped — mirrors destroy/exile's value.all + value.filter[/
	// value.controller] mass-effect shape (destroyAllMatching/
	// exileAllMatching, gamestate.go), reusing the SAME matchesPermanentFilter
	// bucket vocabulary. each_player rides alongside rather than as a second
	// sequence atom because both halves share one amount/damage-source
	// (Pestilence's "and each player" is the SAME N, not an independent
	// effect the walker would apply its own modifiers to twice).
	if all, _ := effectValue["all"].(bool); all {
		filter, _ := effectValue["filter"].(string)
		scope, _ := effectValue["controller"].(string)
		for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
			if scope == "you" && card.Controller != controller {
				continue
			}
			if scope == "opponent" && card.Controller == controller {
				continue
			}
			if !matchesPermanentFilter(card, filter) {
				continue
			}
			recipient := card
			if gs.EffectManager != nil {
				recipient = gs.EffectManager.RedirectDamageTarget(card)
			}
			recipient.DealDamage(amount)
			dealt += amount
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateDamageEvent(source, recipient, amount, controller))
			}
		}
		if eachPlayer, _ := effectValue["each_player"].(bool); eachPlayer {
			for idx := range gs.Players {
				gs.Players[idx].TakeDamage(amount)
				dealt += amount
				if gs.EventBus != nil {
					gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateLifeLossEvent(idx, amount))
				}
			}
		}
		gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
		return nil
	}

	// If no targets specified, check if effect targets controller or opponent
	if len(targets) == 0 {
		// Sweep shapes (damage round 2026-08-03): "deals N damage to each
		// creature [with/without flying]" / "to each player" / "to each
		// creature and each player". value.each selects the scope; the
		// optional keyword filters narrow the creature half.
		if each, ok := effectValue["each"].(string); ok && each != "" {
			if each == "creature" || each == "creature_and_player" {
				kw, _ := effectValue["each_keyword"].(string)
				kwWithout, _ := effectValue["each_without_keyword"].(string)
				// task-2428 (verb_unmapped:damage): three more optional
				// qualifiers on the same bare sweep — "each OTHER creature[...]"
				// (excludes the source itself, Raging Swordtooth/Harbinger of
				// the Hunt class), "each non-<SUBTYPE> creature" (Fiery
				// Cannonade), "each UNTAPPED creature" (Calamity of Cinders).
				// All AND together with the existing keyword filters in the
				// same loop, same as kw/kwWithout already do.
				other, _ := effectValue["each_other"].(bool)
				withoutSubtype, _ := effectValue["each_without_subtype"].(string)
				untapped, _ := effectValue["each_untapped"].(bool)
				// snapshot first — DealDamage mutates battlefield state and
				// CheckStateBasedActions below removes the dead
				for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
					if !c.IsCreature() {
						continue
					}
					if kw != "" && !c.HasKeywordWithEffects(Keyword(kw), gs.EffectManager) {
						continue
					}
					if kwWithout != "" && c.HasKeywordWithEffects(Keyword(kwWithout), gs.EffectManager) {
						continue
					}
					if other && source != nil && c.ID == source.ID {
						continue
					}
					if withoutSubtype != "" && c.HasSubType(withoutSubtype) {
						continue
					}
					if untapped && c.Tapped {
						continue
					}
					recipient := c
					if gs.EffectManager != nil {
						recipient = gs.EffectManager.RedirectDamageTarget(c)
					}
					recipient.DealDamage(amount)
					dealt += amount
					if gs.EventBus != nil {
						gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateDamageEvent(source, recipient, amount, controller))
					}
				}
			}
			if each == "player" || each == "creature_and_player" {
				for pi := range gs.Players {
					gs.Players[pi].TakeDamage(amount)
					dealt += amount
					if gs.EventBus != nil {
						gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateLifeLossEvent(pi, amount))
					}
				}
			}
			gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
			return nil
		}
		targetPlayer := controller
		if targetType, ok := effectValue["target"].(string); ok {
			// "opponent" hits the other player; "you" (and the historical
			// bare default) is the controller.
			if targetType == "opponent" {
				targetPlayer = 1 - controller
			}
			// "~ deals N damage to each player." (Flame Rift/Spear Spewer) —
			// mirrors executeLifeLossEffect's identical each_player case.
			if targetType == "each_player" || targetType == "each_opponent" {
				// task-2544 (verb_unmapped:damage): "~ deals damage to each
				// player equal to the number of lands they control" (Acidic
				// Soil) — the amount is PER-PLAYER (each player's own count),
				// not one value shared across the sweep, so it can't ride the
				// amount_count chokepoint in ExecuteAbilityEffect (that
				// resolves once, relative to the ability's controller, before
				// this executor ever runs). each_player_amount_count carries
				// the SAME closed count-spec vocabulary (dynamic_amount.go),
				// evaluated once per player with that player as the
				// "you"-relative controller, so {"count":"battlefield",
				// "filter":"land","controller":"you"} counts THAT player's
				// own lands on each iteration.
				perPlayerSpec, hasPerPlayer := effectValue["each_player_amount_count"].(map[string]interface{})
				for idx := range gs.Players {
					if targetType == "each_opponent" && idx == controller {
						continue
					}
					dmg := amount
					if hasPerPlayer {
						n, ok := gs.EvalDynamicAmount(perPlayerSpec, idx, source)
						if !ok {
							n = 0
						}
						dmg = ModifyDamageAmount(gs.Battlefield, source, n)
						dmg = gs.ApplyEOTDamageMultiplier(source, dmg)
					}
					dealt += gs.dealNoncombatPlayerDamage(source, idx, dmg)
				}
				return nil
			}
		}
		dealt += gs.dealNoncombatPlayerDamage(source, targetPlayer, amount)
		return nil
	}

	// Apply damage to each target
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.IsCard {
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
			if err != nil {
				continue // Target no longer exists
			}
			// Card-level damage redirect ([VOCAB] #54): no-op absent a
			// registered redirect effect on card.
			recipient := card
			if gs.EffectManager != nil {
				recipient = gs.EffectManager.RedirectDamageTarget(card)
			}
			recipient.DealDamage(amount)
			dealt += amount

			// Fire damage event
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateDamageEvent(source, recipient, amount, controller))
			}
		} else if target.PlayerIdx >= 0 && target.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
			dmg := amount
			// The same recipient-relative count used by each-player sweeps also
			// applies to a real player target ("target player" / "that player").
			// Evaluate with the damaged player as "you", not the ability's
			// controller, so each target's own permanents determine the amount.
			if perPlayerSpec, ok := effectValue["each_player_amount_count"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {
				n, valid := gs.EvalDynamicAmount(perPlayerSpec, target.PlayerIdx, source)
				if !valid {
					n = 0
				}
				dmg = ModifyDamageAmount(gs.Battlefield, source, n)
				dmg = gs.ApplyEOTDamageMultiplier(source, dmg)
			}
			dealt += gs.dealNoncombatPlayerDamage(source, target.PlayerIdx, dmg)
		}
	}

	// Check state-based actions after dealing damage
	gs.CheckStateBasedActions()

	return nil
}

// executeLifeGainEffect - "Gain N life" / "Target player gains N life"
func (gs *GameState) executeLifeGainEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard && target.PlayerIdx >= 0 && target.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.GainLife(target.PlayerIdx, amount)
			return nil
		}
	}
	// Non-targeted you/opponent-agnostic agent (player_referent_target_resolution
	// round, 2026-08-11, ticket #3235): mirrors executeLifeLossEffect's
	// effectValue["target"] handling — "gains" previously always defaulted to
	// the controller, so "opponent"/"each player gains" cardfns (Aria of
	// Flame/Aven Shrine class) had no executor path.
	if targetType, ok := effectValue["target"].(string); ok {
		switch targetType {
		case "opponent":
			gs.GainLife(1-controller, amount)
			return nil
		case "each_player":
			for idx := range gs.Players {
				gs.GainLife(idx, amount)
			}
			return nil
		}
	}
	gs.GainLife(controller, amount)
	return nil
}

// executeDoubleLifeTotalEffect - "Double [a player]'s life total." (Beacon
// of Immortality class, dynamic-amount round 2, 2026-08-08). Rule 119.4:
// setting a life total to a HIGHER number means the player GAINS that much
// life, to a LOWER number means they LOSE that much — so doubling never
// writes Player.Life directly. It routes the delta through the SAME
// primitives executeLifeGainEffect/executeLifeLossEffect use (GainLife for
// the positive case — which also applies LifeGainModifier replacements and
// fires EventLifeGain triggers, both rules-correct for doubling; LoseLife +
// CreateLifeLossEvent for the negative case, where 2x of a negative total is
// LOWER: doubling -4 to -8 is a loss of 4). Doubling 0 changes nothing —
// no event, no trigger (Rule 119.10 analog; GainLife(_,0) already no-ops).
// value.whose: "you" (default) | "opponent" | "target_player" (first player
// target; absent/vanished target -> honest no-op, Rule 608.2b).
func (gs *GameState) executeDoubleLifeTotalEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	idx := controller
	switch getStringValue(effectValue, "whose", "you") {
	case "opponent":
		idx = 1 - controller
	case "target_player":
		idx = -1
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard && t.PlayerIdx >= 0 && t.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
				idx = t.PlayerIdx
				break
			}
		}
		if idx < 0 {
			return nil
		}
	}
	cur := gs.Players[idx].Life
	switch {
	case cur > 0:
		gs.GainLife(idx, cur)
	case cur < 0:
		gs.Players[idx].LoseLife(-cur)
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateLifeLossEvent(idx, -cur))
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeLifeLossEffect - "Target player loses N life"
func (gs *GameState) executeLifeLossEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	// Tally total life actually lost for linked_amount readers ("you gain
	// life equal to the life lost this way", Blood Tribute class — the
	// missing producer named by the 2583 harvest verdict). Deferred so every
	// branch (each_player / default-opponent / explicit targets) is covered.
	lost := 0
	defer func() { gs.noteSeqTally("life_lost", lost) }()

	// If no targets, opponent loses life
	if len(targets) == 0 {
		targetPlayer := 1 - controller
		if targetType, ok := effectValue["target"].(string); ok {
			if targetType == "you" {
				targetPlayer = controller
			}
			// "Each player loses N life." (e.g. Orzhov Guildmage)
			if targetType == "each_player" {
				for idx := range gs.Players {
					gs.Players[idx].LoseLife(amount)
					lost += amount
					if gs.EventBus != nil {
						gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateLifeLossEvent(idx, amount))
					}
				}
				return nil
			}
		}
		gs.Players[targetPlayer].LoseLife(amount)
		lost += amount

		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateLifeLossEvent(targetPlayer, amount))
		}
		return nil
	}

	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard && target.PlayerIdx >= 0 && target.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.Players[target.PlayerIdx].LoseLife(amount)
			lost += amount

			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateLifeLossEvent(target.PlayerIdx, amount))
			}
		}
	}

	return nil
}

// executeLoseUnspentManaEffect - "Target player loses all unspent mana"
// (Mana Short class). No-target default mirrors executeLifeLossEffect:
// opponent loses (or value.target="you"/"each_player" overrides).
func (gs *GameState) executeLoseUnspentManaEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	if len(targets) == 0 {
		if targetType, ok := effectValue["target"].(string); ok && targetType == "each_player" {
			for idx := range gs.Players {
				gs.Players[idx].ManaPool.Empty()
			}
			return nil
		}
		targetPlayer := 1 - controller
		if targetType, _ := effectValue["target"].(string); targetType == "you" {
			targetPlayer = controller
		}
		if targetPlayer >= 0 && targetPlayer < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.Players[targetPlayer].ManaPool.Empty()
		}
		return nil
	}

	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard && target.PlayerIdx >= 0 && target.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.Players[target.PlayerIdx].ManaPool.Empty()
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executePumpEffect - "Target creature gets +N/+M until end of turn"
// executePumpAllReferentControllerEOT implements the
// pump_all_referent_controller_spell primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-pump-all-referent-controller-spell-e7617de98a, Public
// Execution class): "Each other creature that player controls gets -2/-0
// until end of turn," where "that player" is the controller of the
// permanent a PRIOR atom in this same spell sequence acted on
// (gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"], the same cross-atom referent slot
// grant_keywords_referent_eot/gain_control_referent_eot consume) — not a
// statically-known you/opponent controller. Deliberately carries no target
// of its own: the mass recipient is derived entirely from the referent's
// controller, read while the referent is still on the battlefield (a
// preceding "destroy target creature" atom marks it for a state-based
// removal that hasn't pulled it off the battlefield yet when this atom
// runs later in the same resolution).
func (gs *GameState) executePumpAllReferentControllerEOT(source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	if gs.EffectManager == nil || source == nil || gs.seqSlotsStr == nil {
		return nil
	}
	refID := gs.seqSlotsStr["referent"]
	if refID == "" {
		return nil
	}
	refCard, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(refID)
	if err != nil || refCard == nil {
		return nil
	}
	referentController := refCard.Controller
	power := getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 0)
	toughness := getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 0)
	for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if card == nil || card.ID == refID {
			continue
		}
		if card.Controller != referentController {
			continue
		}
		if !matchesPermanentFilter(card, "creature") {
			continue
		}
		gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
			Source:          source,
			AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
			PowerMod:        power,
			ToughnessMod:    toughness,
			UntilEOT:        true,
		})
	}
	return nil
}

// pump_until_eot_with_keyword_grant primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-pump-until-eot-with-keyword-grant-ac968245d2, Moodmark Painter
// class: "target creature gains menace and gets +X/+0 until end of turn,
// where X is the number of creature cards in your graveyard."). A single
// pump/pump_until_eot atom can now ALSO carry effectValue["keywords"]/
// ["keyword"] (the same keywordListFromValue vocabulary grant_keywords_
// until_eot reads below) so the keyword grant and the P/T bonus land on the
// SAME target in the SAME resolution, instead of needing a second effect
// atom. Empty for every plain pump/pump_until_eot record — the negative
// example — so this is purely additive; the standalone
// grant_keywords_until_eot primitive is untouched for a record carrying only
// a keyword grant.
func (gs *GameState) executePumpEffect(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	power := getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 0)
	toughness := getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 0)
	keywords := keywordListFromValue(effectValue)

	// Non-targeted, referent-filtered group pump (ticket
	// engine.auto-pump-until-eot-nontargeted-filter-abf43bdc5c, Plague Wight
	// class): "Whenever this creature becomes blocked, each creature
	// blocking it gets -1/-1 until end of turn." Unlike the "all" mass
	// filter below (which scopes by controller/type/name across the whole
	// battlefield), this scopes to a referent set derived from the SOURCE's
	// current combat relationship — the creatures declared as blockers of
	// source, gs.CombatState.Blockers[source.ID] — and requires no target
	// selection at all. Checked before the no-target self-pump fallback
	// just below so a group_filter atom is never misread as pumping the
	// source itself.
	if groupFilter, _ := effectValue["group_filter"].(string); groupFilter != "" {
		switch groupFilter {
		case "blocking_source":
			if source != nil && gs.CombatState != nil {
				for _, blocker := range gs.CombatState.Blockers[source.ID] {
					if blocker == nil {
						continue
					}
					effect := &ContinuousEffect{
						Source:          source,
						AffectsSpecific: blocker.ID,
						PowerMod:        power,
						ToughnessMod:    toughness,
						UntilEOT:        true,
					}
					gs.EffectManager.Register(effect)
				}
			}
		}
		return nil
	}

	// Non-targeted mass "all" filter form (ticket
	// engine.auto-pump-until-eot-nontargeted-all-filter-027b809f04, Pack
	// Mastiff class): "Each creature you control named X gets +N/+M until
	// end of turn." Mirrors executeTapAllEffect's controller-scope +
	// matchesPermanentFilter mass shape instead of no-op'ing a no-target
	// atom. An optional effectValue["name"] additionally restricts the
	// mass filter to permanents sharing that exact printed name
	// (case-insensitive, Rule 201.4b), since the type-bucket filter alone
	// can't express a same-name restriction.
	if all, _ := effectValue["all"].(bool); all {
		scope := getStringValue(effectValue, "controller", "you")
		filter := getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "creature")
		name := getStringValue(effectValue, "name", "")
		// excludeSource skips the ability's own source card from the mass
		// match (ticket engine.auto-pump-until-eot-filtered-no-target-a592573664,
		// Wild Beastmaster class: "each OTHER creature you control gets
		// +X/+X until end of turn"). matchesPermanentFilter has no notion
		// of the source permanent, so "other" is expressed as a separate
		// exclusion flag instead of a new filter-vocabulary entry.
		excludeSource, _ := effectValue["exclude_source"].(bool)
		for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
			if card == nil {
				continue
			}
			if excludeSource && source != nil && card.ID == source.ID {
				continue
			}
			switch scope {
			case "you":
				if card.Controller != controller {
					continue
				}
			case "opponent":
				if card.Controller == controller {
					continue
				}
			}
			if !matchesPermanentFilter(card, filter) {
				continue
			}
			if name != "" && !strings.EqualFold(card.Name, name) {
				continue
			}
			effect := &ContinuousEffect{
				Source:          source,
				AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
				PowerMod:        power,
				ToughnessMod:    toughness,
				UntilEOT:        true,
			}
			gs.EffectManager.Register(effect)
		}
		return nil
	}

	// If no targets, might be a self-pump
	if len(targets) == 0 && source != nil && source.IsCreature() {
		// Create temporary effect (until end of turn)
		effect := &ContinuousEffect{
			Source:       source,
			PowerMod:     power,
			ToughnessMod: toughness,
			UntilEOT:     true,
		}
		gs.EffectManager.Register(effect)
		return nil
	}

	// Apply to targets
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.IsCard {
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			if card.IsCreature() {
				effect := &ContinuousEffect{
					Source:          source,
					AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
					PowerMod:        power,
					ToughnessMod:    toughness,
					UntilEOT:        true,
				}
				gs.EffectManager.Register(effect)
				for _, kw := range keywords {
					gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
						Source:          source,
						AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
						GrantKeyword:    kw,
						UntilEOT:        true,
					})
				}
			}
		}
	}

	return nil
}

// executeConditionalPumpOnCreatureType implements the
// conditional_pump_on_creature_type primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-conditional-pump-on-creature-type-87200f1a92, Kaseto, Orochi
// Archmage class): "... If that creature is a Snake, it gets +2/+2 until
// end of turn." Unlike executePumpEffect's unconditional targeted branch,
// this checks effectValue["creature_type"] against the target's printed
// subtypes (Card.HasSubType) before registering the UntilEOT
// ContinuousEffect — if the referent doesn't have the named creature type,
// the pump simply never applies (Rule 608.2b analog: the condition just
// isn't met, nothing else about the target is affected). No-op if no
// creature_type is configured or the target isn't a creature on the
// battlefield.
func (gs *GameState) executeConditionalPumpOnCreatureType(source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	creatureType := getStringValue(effectValue, "creature_type", "")
	if creatureType == "" {
		return nil
	}
	power := getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 0)
	toughness := getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 0)

	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
		if err != nil || card == nil || !card.IsCreature() {
			continue
		}
		if !card.HasSubType(creatureType) {
			continue
		}
		effect := &ContinuousEffect{
			Source:          source,
			AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
			PowerMod:        power,
			ToughnessMod:    toughness,
			UntilEOT:        true,
		}
		gs.EffectManager.Register(effect)
	}

	return nil
}

// executePumpAndGrantKeywordsUntilEOT applies both the P/T modification and
// keyword grant to each targeted creature in one until-end-of-turn effect.
func (gs *GameState) executePumpAndGrantKeywordsUntilEOT(source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	if gs.EffectManager == nil {
		return nil
	}

	power := getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 0)
	toughness := getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 0)
	keywords := keywordListFromValue(effectValue)

	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard || target.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
		if err != nil || card == nil || !card.IsCreature() {
			continue
		}

		for index, keyword := range keywords {
			effect := &ContinuousEffect{
				Source:          source,
				AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
				GrantKeyword:    keyword,
				UntilEOT:        true,
			}
			if index == 0 {
				effect.PowerMod = power
				effect.ToughnessMod = toughness
			}
			gs.EffectManager.Register(effect)
		}

		if len(keywords) == 0 {
			gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
				Source:          source,
				AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
				PowerMod:        power,
				ToughnessMod:    toughness,
				UntilEOT:        true,
			})
		}
	}

	return nil
}

// executePutCounterEffect - "Put a +1/+1 counter on this creature"
// keywordListFromValue reads value.keywords (list) or value.keyword (single)
// and canonicalizes parser-lowered names ("first strike" -> "First Strike").
func keywordListFromValue(effectValue map[string]interface{}) []Keyword {
	var out []Keyword
	add := func(s string) {
		s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
		if s == "" {
			return
		}
		out = append(out, Keyword(strings.Title(strings.ToLower(s))))
	}
	if raw, ok := effectValue["keywords"].([]interface{}); ok {
		for _, k := range raw {
			if s, ok := k.(string); ok {
				add(s)
			}
		}
	} else if s, ok := effectValue["keyword"].(string); ok {
		add(s)
	}
	return out
}

// executeAmassEffect implements Amass (Rule 701.42, ticket-2911): puts N
// +1/+1 counters on an Army the controller controls, creating a 0/0 black
// [Type] Army creature token first if they don't control one. Generic over
// creature type via effectValue["creature_type"] — unlike cardfns.Amass
// (backend/cardfns/lib_amass.go), which is hardcoded to the Zombie variant
// for its two pre-existing per-card callers (that helper predates this
// engine-level primitive and stays as-is; this is the new, generic path
// reparse.py's emitter targets), this one executor covers every printed
// Amass variant in the corpus (Orc, Zombie, Sliver, ...). Also grants the
// type to an ALREADY-controlled Army that lacks it (Rule 701.42d — "It's
// also a [Type]" applies even when reusing an Army amassed under a
// different type by an earlier activation), matching cardfns/ForayOfOrcs.go's
// hand-authored equivalent. Routes counter placement through gs.AddCounters
// so counter-placement replacement effects (Hardened Scales, etc.) apply
// the same way they would to any other +1/+1 counter placement.
func (gs *GameState) executeAmassEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	n := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0)
	if n <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	creatureType := getStringValue(effectValue, "creature_type", "")
	if creatureType == "" {
		return nil
	}

	var army *Card
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetCreatures(controller) {
		if c.HasSubType("Army") {
			army = c
			break
		}
	}
	if army == nil {
		army = gs.CreateToken(controller, "Army", 0, 0, []string{creatureType, "Army"}, []Color{Black}, nil)
	} else if !army.HasSubType(creatureType) {
		army.SubTypes = append(army.SubTypes, creatureType)
	}
	gs.AddCounters(army, CounterPlusOnePlusOne, n)
	return nil
}

// executeCreateTokenAndTrackLast wraps executeTokenEffect and records the
// resulting token as gs.LastCreatedToken (put_counters_on_self_created_token,
// ticket bf85bad357) so an immediately following "put X +1/+1 counters on
// it" atom in the same paragraph chain can resolve the pronoun without
// player-facing targeting. Diffed by ID against a battlefield snapshot taken
// before creation since executeTokenEffect only reports success/failure, not
// the created *Card.
func (gs *GameState) executeCreateTokenAndTrackLast(tokenController int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	before := make(map[string]bool)
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		before[c.ID] = true
	}
	err := gs.executeTokenEffect(tokenController, effectValue)
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if !before[c.ID] && c.Controller == tokenController {
			gs.LastCreatedToken = c
		}
	}
	return err
}

// applyCountersToCard adds amount counters of counterType to card, mirroring
// the Counters struct's known counter kinds (game/counters.go) and falling
// back to the Custom map for anything else.
func applyCountersToCard(card *Card, counterType string, amount int) {
	switch strings.ToLower(counterType) {
	case "+1/+1":
		card.Counters.PlusOnePlusOne += amount
	case "+0/+1":
		card.Counters.PlusZeroPlusOne += amount
	case "-1/-1":
		card.Counters.MinusOneMinusOne += amount
	case "-0/-1":
		card.Counters.MinusZeroMinusOne += amount
	case "loyalty":
		card.Counters.Loyalty += amount
	case "charge":
		card.Counters.Charge += amount
	default:
		if card.Counters.Custom == nil {
			card.Counters.Custom = make(map[string]int)
		}
		card.Counters.Custom[counterType] += amount
	}
}

func (gs *GameState) executePutCounterEffect(source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	counterType := getStringValue(effectValue, "counter_type", "+1/+1")
	targetSelf := effectValue["target_self"] != nil
	targetTrigger := effectValue["target_trigger_source"] != nil
	targetAttached := effectValue["target_attached"] != nil
	// put_counters_dynamic_amount primitive (ticket #3730, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:put_counters / "put that many +1/+1 counters on ~"
	// family — Ageless Entity class): the record carries
	// value.amount_count = a count spec (registered EvalDynamicAmount
	// vocabulary, e.g. linked_amount from a preceding atom's
	// damage_dealt/life_lost tally or x_value) instead of a literal
	// amount. Resolve it into a plain amount HERE, before any branch
	// reads it, so the target_self AND the each-mass branches below both
	// pick it up unchanged. Purely additive: absent for every
	// literal-amount record (which keeps reading value.amount as before)
	// and for remove_counter* effects (different executor, untouched).
	// Unresolvable spec -> honest no-op, never a guessed counter count.
	if spec, ok := effectValue["amount_count"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {
		n, resolved := gs.EvalDynamicAmountFull(spec, source.Controller, source, nil, targets)
		if !resolved || n <= 0 {
			return nil
		}
		effectValue = map[string]interface{}(effectValue)
		effectValue["amount"] = n
	}
	// put_counters_dynamic_x_amount primitive (ticket #3731, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:put_counters / "Put X +1/+1 counters on ~, where X is
	// <count>" — Astarion's Thirst class): the record carries
	// value.x_amount_count = a spec naming WHERE X comes from (the SAME
	// closed vocabulary lib_dynamic_x_amount.go's resolveXAmount folds at
	// ExecuteAbilityEffect's before-the-switch chokepoint, which has
	// already written a plain "amount" when resolvable). The chokepoint
	// fold is a no-op for an UNRESOLVABLE spec (no announced {X} cost),
	// leaving "amount" unset — and getIntValue below would then fall back
	// to the default 1, placing a GUESSED counter instead of honoring the
	// same no-guess contract the amount_count branch just above keeps.
	// So: an x_amount_count spec present but with no resolved "amount"
	// (and no literal "amount" the parser emitted alongside) is an
	// honest no-op. Purely additive: absent for every literal-amount and
	// amount_count record (which keep their existing paths unchanged)
	// and for remove_counter* effects (different executor, untouched).
	if _, hasXSpec := effectValue["x_amount_count"].(map[string]interface{}); hasXSpec {
		if _, hasAmount := effectValue["amount"]; !hasAmount {
			return nil
		}
	}
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	// An omitted static amount defaults to one; an explicitly resolved zero
	// must remain zero (the generic dynamic fold has already removed its spec).
	if amount <= 0 {
		amount = 0
		if effectValue["monstrosity"] == nil {
			return nil
		}
		// Monstrosity 0 still makes the creature monstrous.
	}
	// put_counters_on_self_created_token (ticket bf85bad357): "Put X +1/+1
	// counters on it" where "it" is the token this same spell/ability just
	// created (Wild Hypothesis class). Resolved via gs.LastCreatedToken (set
	// by executeCreateTokenAndTrackLast immediately above in the same
	// paragraph chain) rather than any player-facing target selection. Only
	// this exact "it" marker takes this branch — an explicit target phrase
	// ("target creature") never sets effectValue["target"] to "it", so it
	// keeps falling through unchanged to the existing targets-based handling
	// below, and this branch never touches "amount" itself, so a fixed
	// literal amount is passed through exactly as resolved above.
	if getStringValue(effectValue, "target", "") == "it" {
		if gs.LastCreatedToken == nil {
			return nil
		}
		applyCountersToCard(gs.LastCreatedToken, counterType, amount)
		gs.LastCreatedToken = nil
		return nil
	}
	adapt := effectValue["adapt"] != nil
	monstrosity := effectValue["monstrosity"] != nil

	// Each-shape (schema-v2 3c round 1): "Put a +1/+1 counter on each <X> you
	// control" — parser emits {target:"each", filter:<subtype|creature>,
	// controller:"you"}. Applies to every matching battlefield creature the
	// source's controller controls; no single target involved.
	//
	// controller/other (task-1979): widened from "you"-only to also accept
	// "opponent" ("each creature your opponents control"/"you don't
	// control" — Archfiend of Ifnir, Toxrill, the Corrosive) and an 'other'
	// self-exclusion ("each other Vampire you control" — Vampire Socialite
	// must not counter itself), mirroring executeUntapAllEffect's already-
	// proven controller-scope switch.
	if getStringValue(effectValue, "target", "") == "each" {
		filter := strings.ToLower(getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "creature"))
		scope := getStringValue(effectValue, "controller", "you")
		excludeSelf := effectValue["other"] != nil
		for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
			switch scope {
			case "you":
				if c.Controller != source.Controller {
					continue
				}
			case "opponent":
				if c.Controller == source.Controller {
					continue
				}
			}
			if excludeSelf && c.ID == source.ID {
				continue
			}
			if !c.IsCreature() {
				continue
			}
			if filter != "creature" && filter != "creatures" {
				match := false
				for _, st := range c.SubTypes {
					if strings.ToLower(st) == filter || strings.ToLower(st) == filter+"s" ||
						strings.ToLower(st)+"s" == filter {
						match = true
						break
					}
				}
				if !match {
					continue
				}
			}
			gs.AddCounters(c, CounterType(counterType), amount)
		}
		gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
		return nil
	}

	// Determine which card gets the counter
	var target *Card
	if tid := getStringValue(effectValue, "target_id", ""); tid != "" {
		// "put N counters on THAT CREATURE" where "that creature" is a prior
		// atom's result (target_scratch_ref, resolved to a concrete card ID
		// by game/effect_sequence.go's executeSequenceAtomEv pre-dispatch
		// rewrite — scratch-binding round, 2026-08-09,
		// docs/phase-rs-cross-reference-plan.md). Checked first: an explicit
		// resolved ID always wins over the target_self/filter/each
		// conventions below, which only apply when no scratch binding exists.
		if c, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tid); err == nil {
			target = c
		}
	} else if targetTrigger && len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard {
		// "put a +1/+1 counter on it" — the creature that triggered the ability
		// (e.g. the Vampire that dealt combat damage for Stensia Masquerade).
		if c, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(targets[0].CardID); err == nil {
			target = c
		}
	} else if targetAttached {
		// "put a counter on enchanted/equipped creature" (task-1979): no
		// target choice at all, always whatever this Aura/Equipment is
		// attached to — mirrors game/condition.go's Of=="enchanted"/
		// "equipped" state-condition lookup (same AttachedTo field, same
		// convention).
		if source != nil && source.AttachedTo != "" {
			if c, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(source.AttachedTo); err == nil {
				target = c
			}
		}
	} else if targetSelf {
		target = source
	} else if len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard {
		// Explicit target (modal round 2, 2026-08-02: "Put a charge counter
		// on target artifact" as a picked mode) — the walker threads the
		// validated mode target through StackObject.Targets. Previously this
		// shape errored below; no shipped record relied on that.
		if c, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(targets[0].CardID); err == nil {
			target = c
		}
	}

	if target == nil {
		return errors.New("put_counter effect has no valid target")
	}

	// put_counters_equal_power primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-put-counters-dynamic-amount-power-c327670baf, Railway
	// Brawler class: "Whenever another creature you control enters, put X
	// +1/+1 counters on it, where X is its power."). value.amount == "power"
	// (a string, not the usual int/float64 getIntValue reads) names the
	// counter count as the RESOLVED target's own power rather than a
	// literal amount — checked here, after target is picked by whichever
	// branch above resolved it (target_trigger_source for Railway Brawler),
	// so it applies to that same creature regardless of which target-
	// selection path found it. X==0 is an honest no-op, never a guessed
	// counter count; static literal-amount records (e.g. "put 1 +1/+1
	// counter") never carry this string value and are unaffected.
	if getStringValue(effectValue, "amount", "") == "power" {
		amount = target.GetPowerWithEffects(gs.EffectManager)
		if amount <= 0 {
			return nil
		}
	}

	// Adapt (Rule 702.130): only put counters if the creature has none.
	if adapt && target.Counters.PlusOnePlusOne > 0 {
		return nil
	}

	// Monstrosity (Rule 700.10/702.91): "If this creature isn't monstrous,
	// put N +1/+1 counters on it and it becomes monstrous." Activating the
	// ability again after it's already monstrous is legal (nothing stops
	// paying the cost) but resolves as a no-op — no counters, no re-publish.
	if monstrosity && target.IsMonstrous {
		return nil
	}

	// Add the counters (via gs.AddCounters so counter-placement replacement
	// effects like Hardened Scales apply).
	gs.AddCounters(target, CounterType(counterType), amount)

	if monstrosity {
		target.IsMonstrous = true
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesMonstrous).WithSource(target))
		}
	}

	// Trigger state-based actions in case the counter put the creature over lethality
	gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
	return nil
}

// executePutCountersIndependentMultiTarget implements the
// put_counters_independent_multi_target primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-put-counters-independent-multi-target-8814b1cc89, River
// Heralds' Boon class): "Put a +1/+1 counter on target creature and a
// +1/+1 counter on up to one target Merfolk." TWO independently resolved
// targets in one non-modal spell resolution — targets[0] is the MANDATORY
// slot (filters[0]), targets[1] is the OPTIONAL "up to one" slot
// (filters[1]): an absent/declined second target is simply skipped rather
// than an error, mirroring executeDestroyUpToArtifactCreatureLand's
// optional-slot tolerance, while validating each positional target
// against its OWN filter (not one shared filter) mirrors
// executeDestroyMultiFilterTargets. Distinct from the "each" mass-shape
// (a counter on EVERY qualifying permanent) and the modal round-2 shape
// (player picks one of several modes) handled by executePutCounterEffect
// above — this always resolves BOTH slots of one fixed, non-modal effect.
func (gs *GameState) executePutCountersIndependentMultiTarget(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	rawFilters, _ := effectValue["filters"].([]interface{})
	if len(rawFilters) == 0 || len(targets) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	counterType := getStringValue(effectValue, "counter_type", "+1/+1")
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	if amount < 1 {
		amount = 1
	}
	for i, t := range targets {
		if i >= len(rawFilters) || !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		filter, _ := rawFilters[i].(string)
		filter = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(filter))
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID)
		if err != nil || !matchesPermanentFilter(card, filter) {
			continue
		}
		gs.AddCounters(card, CounterType(counterType), amount)
	}
	gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
	return nil
}

// executeRemoveCounterDamageEffect - "Remove a +1/+1 counter from this creature: deal N damage to any target"
func (gs *GameState) executeRemoveCounterDamageEffect(source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	if source == nil {
		return errors.New("no source card for remove_counter_damage effect")
	}

	counterType := getStringValue(effectValue, "counter_type", "+1/+1")
	damageAmount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0)

	// Check if source has the counter to remove
	counterCount := 0
	switch counterType {
	case "+1/+1":
		counterCount = source.Counters.PlusOnePlusOne
	case "+0/+1":
		counterCount = source.Counters.PlusZeroPlusOne
	case "-0/-1":
		counterCount = source.Counters.MinusZeroMinusOne
	case "-1/-1":
		counterCount = source.Counters.MinusOneMinusOne
	default:
		if source.Counters.Custom != nil {
			counterCount = source.Counters.Custom[counterType]
		}
	}

	if counterCount == 0 {
		return fmt.Errorf("%s has no %s counters to remove", source.Name, counterType)
	}

	// Remove the counter
	switch counterType {
	case "+1/+1":
		source.Counters.PlusOnePlusOne--
	case "+0/+1":
		source.Counters.PlusZeroPlusOne--
	case "-0/-1":
		source.Counters.MinusZeroMinusOne--
	case "-1/-1":
		source.Counters.MinusOneMinusOne--
	default:
		if source.Counters.Custom != nil {
			source.Counters.Custom[counterType]--
		}
	}

	// Deal damage to the target
	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard {
			// Damage to player
			if target.PlayerIdx >= 0 && target.PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
				gs.Players[target.PlayerIdx].TakeDamage(damageAmount)
				if gs.EventBus != nil {
					gs.EventBus.Publish(NewEvent(EventDamageDealt).
						WithSource(source).
						WithPlayer(target.PlayerIdx).
						WithValue("amount", damageAmount))
				}
			}
		} else {
			// Damage to creature
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
			if err == nil && card != nil {
				card.Damage += damageAmount
				if gs.EventBus != nil {
					gs.EventBus.Publish(NewEvent(EventDamageDealt).
						WithSource(source).
						WithTarget(card).
						WithValue("amount", damageAmount))
				}
			}
		}
	}

	// Check state-based actions (creature may have lethal damage)
	gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyEffect - "Destroy target permanent"
// cantBeRegenerated (ticket #3976, REPARSE-MAP
// destroy_single_target_regen_gap / "destroy target X and it can't be
// regenerated." — Knight of the Mists class) routes each target through
// DestroyTargetForced (Rule 701.16b — regeneration shields cannot replace
// the destruction; indestructible is still honored) instead of the
// shield-honoring DestroyTarget.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyEffect(targets []Target, cantBeRegenerated bool) error {
	destroy := DestroyTarget
	if cantBeRegenerated {
		destroy = DestroyTargetForced
	}
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.IsCard {
			destroy(target, gs)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyCountedMultiTarget(targets []Target, maxTargets int, cantBeRegenerated bool) error {
	if maxTargets <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	destroy := DestroyTarget
	if cantBeRegenerated {
		destroy = DestroyTargetForced
	}
	destroyed := 0
	for _, target := range targets {
		if destroyed >= maxTargets {
			break
		}
		if !target.IsCard {
			continue
		}
		destroy(target, gs)
		destroyed++
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyAtEndOfCombat implements the at_end_of_combat rider on the
// destroy primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-destroy-at-end-of-combat-timing-935c317094, Silent Assassin
// class): "{3}{B}: Destroy target blocking creature at end of combat."
// Targets are captured now and re-checked for battlefield presence at
// resolution time (DestroyTarget's own Battlefield lookup) via a one-shot
// EventEndOfCombat subscription — mirrors executeGainControlEffect's
// until_eot revert-on-EventCleanup idiom (control_effects.go), just keyed
// to the end of combat step instead of cleanup. A nil EventBus falls back
// to immediate destruction rather than silently dropping the effect.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyAtEndOfCombat(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	destroy := DestroyTarget
	if cantBeRegenerated, _ := effectValue["cant_be_regenerated"].(bool); cantBeRegenerated {
		destroy = DestroyTargetForced
	}
	if gs.EventBus == nil {
		for _, target := range targets {
			if target.IsCard {
				destroy(target, gs)
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	captured := make([]Target, len(targets))
	copy(captured, targets)
	fired := false
	gs.EventBus.Subscribe(EventEndOfCombat, func(e *Event) {
		if fired {
			return
		}
		fired = true
		for _, target := range captured {
			if target.IsCard {
				destroy(target, gs)
			}
		}
	})
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyTargetBlockingSource implements the
// destroy_target_blocking_source primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-destroy-target-blocking-source-8c68a1acdf, Knight of Dusk
// class): "Destroy target creature blocking this creature." Unlike
// executeDestroyEffect's unconditional "destroy target permanent", each
// target is only destroyed if it is CURRENTLY blocking the ability's own
// source (Card.Blocking + Card.BlockingCardID) — a target that stopped
// blocking before this resolves (Rule 608.2b) is left alone, matching the
// no-target no-op convention used throughout this file.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyTargetBlockingSource(source *Card, targets []Target) error {
	if source == nil {
		return nil
	}
	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
		if err != nil || card == nil {
			continue
		}
		if !card.Blocking || card.BlockingCardID != source.ID {
			continue
		}
		DestroyTarget(target, gs)
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyAllAttachedToTarget implements the
// destroy_all_attached_to_target primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-destroy-all-attached-to-target-175ae9b2f8, Street Sweeper
// class): "destroy all <subtype> attached to target <type>." The caller
// supplies exactly one chosen target (the named-type permanent, e.g. a
// land); this executor scans the battlefield for every permanent whose
// AttachedTo points at that target AND whose SubTypes include the named
// subtype (effectValue["subtype"], e.g. "Aura"), and destroys each of them
// via the shield-honoring DestroyTarget. The destroyed set is scoped to the
// chosen target's attachments — permanents of the same subtype attached to
// OTHER permanents are left alone, and permanents attached to the chosen
// target but lacking the named subtype are left alone. Mirrors
// executeBounceAttachedAndItsAuras's AttachedTo-scan idiom and the
// no-target no-op convention used throughout this file: an empty/missing
// target, a target no longer on the battlefield, or an empty subtype is a
// no-op. The attachment IDs are captured before any destroy so removing one
// attachment can't affect the scan; destroy errors are swallowed
// (best-effort, mirroring executeDestroyTargetAndSameNameOthers).
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyAllAttachedToTarget(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	subtype := getStringValue(effectValue, "subtype", "")
	if subtype == "" {
		return nil
	}
	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard || target.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		if _, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID); err != nil {
			continue
		}
		var attachedIDs []string
		for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
			if c == nil || c.AttachedTo != target.CardID {
				continue
			}
			if !cardHasAnySubtype(c, []string{subtype}) {
				continue
			}
			attachedIDs = append(attachedIDs, c.ID)
		}
		for _, id := range attachedIDs {
			_ = DestroyTarget(Target{IsCard: true, CardID: id}, gs)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeBounceEffect - "Return target to its owner's hand"
func (gs *GameState) executeBounceEffect(targets []Target) error {
	return gs.executeBounceEffectTo(targets, "")
}

// executeBounceEffectTo is executeBounceEffect with a landing zone: ""
// (default) or "hand" returns to the owner's hand exactly as before;
// "library_top"/"library_bottom" (Submerge, Condemn class — task 2423,
// verb_unmapped:put_zone) instead put the permanent onto the owner's
// library via the same Zone.PutOnTop/PutOnBottom primitives the
// reveal_choose/return_from_graveyard destinations already use.
func (gs *GameState) executeBounceEffectTo(targets []Target, destination string) error {
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.IsCard {
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.RemoveCard(target.CardID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}

			// Remove any effects this card was generating
			gs.EffectManager.RemoveBySource(card)
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.EventBus.UnregisterAbilitiesFromCard(card.ID)
			}

			if card.Owner < 0 || card.Owner >= len(gs.Players) {
				continue
			}
			// Reset card state regardless of destination — it's leaving the
			// battlefield either way.
			card.Tapped = false
			card.SummoningSick = false
			card.Damage = 0
			card.Attacking = false
			card.Blocking = false
			owner := gs.Players[card.Owner]
			switch destination {
			case "library_top":
				owner.Library.PutOnTop(card) //nolint:errcheck
			case "library_bottom":
				owner.Library.PutOnBottom(card) //nolint:errcheck
			default:
				owner.Hand.AddCard(card)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// exileMatchesCreatureOrPlaneswalkerColor implements the target_filter half
// of the exile_target_creature_or_planeswalker_color_filter primitive
// (ticket engine.auto-exile-target-creature-or-planeswalker-color-filt-7aa86c3508,
// Devout Decree class): "Exile target creature or planeswalker that's
// black or red." A compound type-or-type (creature OR planeswalker) plus
// color-or-color (matches ANY of colors) filter — both halves must hold,
// generic on the colors passed in rather than a card-name special case.
func exileMatchesCreatureOrPlaneswalkerColor(c *Card, colors []Color) bool {
	isCreatureOrPW := c.IsCreature()
	if !isCreatureOrPW {
		for _, t := range c.Types {
			if t == "Planeswalker" {
				isCreatureOrPW = true
				break
			}
		}
	}
	if !isCreatureOrPW {
		return false
	}
	for _, cc := range c.Colors {
		for _, want := range colors {
			if cc == want {
				return true
			}
		}
	}
	return false
}

// exileColorFilterFromEffectValue reads a Python-emitted color restriction
// (effect_value.colors, e.g. Devout Decree's "black or red") off an exile
// atom's value map and converts it into the []Color colorFilter
// executeExileEffect expects. Absent/empty/non-string entries yield nil,
// which keeps executeExileEffect's existing unrestricted behavior for every
// other exile atom (Swords to Plowshares, Isolate's mana_value class, ...).
func exileColorFilterFromEffectValue(effectValue map[string]interface{}) []Color {
	raw, ok := effectValue["colors"]
	if !ok {
		return nil
	}
	var words []string
	switch v := raw.(type) {
	case []string:
		words = v
	case []interface{}:
		for _, item := range v {
			if s, ok := item.(string); ok {
				words = append(words, s)
			}
		}
	case string:
		words = []string{v}
	}
	var colors []Color
	for _, w := range words {
		if c, ok := altCostColorFromWord(w); ok {
			colors = append(colors, c)
		}
	}
	return colors
}

// executeExileEffect - "Exile target permanent". manaValue (ticket
// engine.auto-exile-permanent-with-mana-value-filter-29de385b25, Isolate
// class: "Exile target permanent with mana value 1.") optionally restricts
// exiled targets to permanents whose mana value equals manaValue; -1 means
// unrestricted (every pre-existing call site). colorFilter (ticket
// engine.auto-exile-target-creature-or-planeswalker-color-filt-7aa86c3508,
// Devout Decree class: "Exile target creature or planeswalker that's black
// or red.") is a second, independent variadic filter — when non-empty,
// only permanents matching exileMatchesCreatureOrPlaneswalkerColor are
// exiled; omitted (every pre-existing call site) leaves the original
// unrestricted behavior untouched, so plain "target creature or
// planeswalker" with no color restriction stays unaffected by this filter.
func (gs *GameState) executeExileEffect(targets []Target, manaValue int, colorFilter ...Color) error {
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.IsCard {
			if manaValue >= 0 {
				if c, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID); err == nil && c != nil && c.ManaCost.CMC() != manaValue {
					continue
				}
			}
			if len(colorFilter) > 0 {
				c, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
				if err != nil || c == nil || !exileMatchesCreatureOrPlaneswalkerColor(c, colorFilter) {
					continue
				}
			}
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.RemoveCard(target.CardID)
			if err != nil {
				// task-2363 (verb_unmapped:exile): "whenever [a creature]
				// dies, exile it" (target_ref=="event_subject" off a dies
				// trigger, e.g. Hofri Ghostforge/Nightmare Shepherd class) —
				// the referenced card has already left the battlefield for
				// the controller's graveyard by the time this ability
				// resolves, so the battlefield lookup above always misses.
				// Fall back to searching every player's graveyard by ID;
				// a hit there is exiled exactly like a battlefield card
				// (this is a no-op for every pre-existing exile call site,
				// which always targets a live battlefield permanent and
				// never reaches this branch).
				found := false
				for _, p := range gs.Players {
					if gc, gerr := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(target.CardID); gerr == nil {
						card = gc
						found = true
						break
					}
				}
				if !found {
					continue
				}
			} else {
				// Remove any effects this card was generating
				gs.EffectManager.RemoveBySource(card)
				gs.UnregisterCardAbilities(card)
			}

			// Exile the card
			if card.Owner >= 0 && card.Owner < len(gs.Players) {
				gs.Players[card.Owner].Exile.AddCard(card)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeExileTargetPlusNamedMatchTokens implements the
// exile_target_plus_named_match_tokens primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-exile-target-plus-named-match-tokens-b9b031ee17, Legions to
// Ashes class): "Exile target nonland permanent an opponent controls and
// all tokens that player controls with the same name as that permanent."
// Reads the chosen target's Name/Controller off the battlefield BEFORE
// exiling it via executeExileEffect (which removes it), then scans the
// remaining battlefield for tokens under that same controller sharing that
// name and exiles those too through the same helper — both groups leave
// the battlefield in this one resolution rather than as two separate
// effects.
func (gs *GameState) executeExileTargetPlusNamedMatchTokens(targets []Target) error {
	if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard {
		return nil
	}
	chosen, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(targets[0].CardID)
	if err != nil || chosen == nil {
		return nil
	}
	name := chosen.Name
	controller := chosen.Controller

	if err := gs.executeExileEffect(targets[:1], -1); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	var matches []Target
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if c != nil && c.IsToken && c.Controller == controller && c.Name == name {
			matches = append(matches, Target{IsCard: true, CardID: c.ID})
		}
	}
	if len(matches) > 0 {
		return gs.executeExileEffect(matches, -1)
	}
	return nil
}

// executeExileMvLeSourceCountersMultizone implements the
// exile_mv_le_source_counters_multizone primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-exile-mv-le-source-counters-multizone-e9a468b183, Sphere of
// Annihilation class): "exile ~, all creatures and planeswalkers with mana
// value less than or equal to the number of void counters on it, and all
// creature and planeswalker cards in graveyards with mana value less than
// or equal to the number of void counters on it." The threshold is read
// from source's OWN counter count (effectValue["counter_type"], e.g.
// "void") at resolution, then applied across THREE zones in one sweep: the
// source itself (unconditionally), every battlefield creature/planeswalker
// at or under that mana value, and every graveyard's creature/planeswalker
// cards at or under that mana value — the paired cross-zone MV-vs-counter
// comparison that distinguishes this from a plain battlefield-only or
// graveyard-only mass exile.
func (gs *GameState) executeExileMvLeSourceCountersMultizone(source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	if source == nil {
		return nil
	}
	counterName := getStringValue(effectValue, "counter_type", "")
	threshold := source.Counters.GetCount(CounterType(counterName))

	if err := gs.executeExileEffect([]Target{{IsCard: true, CardID: source.ID}}, -1); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	var battlefieldMatches []Target
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if c == nil || c.ID == source.ID {
			continue
		}
		if !c.IsCreature() && !c.IsPlaneswalker() {
			continue
		}
		if c.ManaCost.CMC() <= threshold {
			battlefieldMatches = append(battlefieldMatches, Target{IsCard: true, CardID: c.ID})
		}
	}
	if err := gs.executeExileEffect(battlefieldMatches, -1); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	for _, p := range gs.Players {
		if p == nil {
			continue
		}
		var graveyardMatches []Target
		for _, c := range p.Graveyard.GetAll() {
			if c == nil {
				continue
			}
			if !c.IsCreature() && !c.IsPlaneswalker() {
				continue
			}
			if c.ManaCost.CMC() <= threshold {
				graveyardMatches = append(graveyardMatches, Target{IsCard: true, CardID: c.ID})
			}
		}
		if err := gs.executeExileEffect(graveyardMatches, -1); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (gs *GameState) executeExileTargetGraveyardCardAndNamedMatches(targets []Target) error {
	if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard {
		return nil
	}

	var owner *Player
	var chosen *Card
	for _, p := range gs.Players {
		if p == nil {
			continue
		}
		card, err := p.Graveyard.GetCard(targets[0].CardID)
		if err == nil && card != nil {
			owner = p
			chosen = card
			break
		}
	}
	if owner == nil || chosen == nil || string(chosen.Type) == "Land" {
		return nil
	}

	card, err := owner.Graveyard.RemoveCard(chosen.ID)
	if err != nil || card == nil {
		return nil
	}
	owner.Exile.AddCard(card)

	name := chosen.Name
	for _, p := range gs.Players {
		if p == nil {
			continue
		}
		for _, c := range append([]*Card(nil), p.Graveyard.GetAll()...) {
			if c == nil || c.Name != name {
				continue
			}
			removed, err := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(c.ID)
			if err == nil && removed != nil {
				p.Exile.AddCard(removed)
			}
		}
	}

	var battlefieldMatches []Target
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if c != nil && c.Name == name {
			battlefieldMatches = append(battlefieldMatches, Target{IsCard: true, CardID: c.ID})
		}
	}
	if len(battlefieldMatches) > 0 {
		return gs.executeExileEffect(battlefieldMatches, -1)
	}
	return nil
}

// executeExileWithCounterEffect - exile_with_counter primitive helper. Same
// battlefield-then-graveyard lookup as executeExileEffect (so a target that
// already died to the SAME trigger, or a graveyard card chosen directly by a
// target ability, both resolve), but keeps the *Card pointer that lands in
// the exile zone so counterType/amount can be stamped on the exiled card
// itself rather than a stale copy.
func (gs *GameState) executeExileWithCounterEffect(targets []Target, counterType CounterType, amount int) error {
	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.RemoveCard(target.CardID)
		if err != nil {
			found := false
			for _, p := range gs.Players {
				if gc, gerr := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(target.CardID); gerr == nil {
					card = gc
					found = true
					break
				}
			}
			if !found {
				continue
			}
		} else {
			gs.EffectManager.RemoveBySource(card)
			gs.UnregisterCardAbilities(card)
		}

		if card.Owner >= 0 && card.Owner < len(gs.Players) {
			gs.Players[card.Owner].Exile.AddCard(card)
		}
		if counterType != "" && amount > 0 {
			gs.AddCounters(card, counterType, amount)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeGoadAllEffect - "goad all/each creatures [you / your opponents / you
// don't] control" (goad round 2026-08-03, task-2002): filter-scoped mass goad
// mirroring exileAllMatching — resolve the affected creatures from the
// battlefield at resolution time using the controller scope, then mark each
// goaded via Card.GoadedUntilTurn = TurnCount+1 (the same field cardfns.Goad
// sets and DeclareAttackers enforces). No cast-time target choice, so this
// shape rides every kind (spell/activated/triggered) safely.
func (gs *GameState) executeGoadAllEffect(caster int, filter, scope string) error {
	filter = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(filter))
	scope = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(scope))
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if c == nil {
			continue
		}
		if scope == "you" && c.Controller != caster {
			continue
		}
		if scope == "opponent" && c.Controller == caster {
			continue
		}
		if !matchesPermanentFilter(c, filter) {
			continue
		}
		c.GoadedUntilTurn = gs.TurnCount + 1
	}
	return nil
}

// executeLockCreatureEffect - "Target creature can't attack or block this turn"
func (gs *GameState) executeLockCreatureEffect(source *Card, targets []Target) error {
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.IsCard {
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			// Grant Defender keyword to prevent attacks and set CannotBlock
			// directly (CanBlock/CanAttack read direct fields, not the
			// EffectManager — task-2821 fix). The ContinuousEffect with
			// UntilEOT handles cleanup: CleanupEndOfTurnEffects resets
			// CannotBlock and strips granted Defender (below). Only set
			// GrantKeyword on the effect if we actually appended Defender
			// — otherwise cleanup would strip a NATIVE Defender keyword.
			grantDefender := !card.HasKeyword(Defender)
			if gs.EffectManager != nil {
				ce := &ContinuousEffect{
					Source:          source,
					SourceID:        source.ID,
					AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
					CannotBlock:     true,
					UntilEOT:        true,
				}
				if grantDefender {
					ce.GrantKeyword = Defender
				}
				gs.EffectManager.Register(ce)
			}
			card.CannotBlock = true
			if grantDefender {
				card.Keywords = append(card.Keywords, Defender)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeCantBlockTargetEffect - "Target creature can't block this turn."
// Registers an until-end-of-turn blocking restriction on the target. Unlike
// lock_creature this grants no Defender, so the creature can still attack.
// A player target means "creatures target player controls can't block this
// turn" (Headliner Scarlett): the restriction is applied to every creature
// that player controls.
func (gs *GameState) executeCantBlockTargetEffect(source *Card, targets []Target) error {
	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard {
			if target.PlayerIdx < 0 || target.PlayerIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
				continue
			}
			for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetCreatures(target.PlayerIdx) {
				gs.applyCannotBlockUntilEOT(source, card)
			}
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		gs.applyCannotBlockUntilEOT(source, card)
	}
	return nil
}

// applyCannotBlockUntilEOT registers the until-end-of-turn blocking
// restriction on a single creature for cant_block_target.
func (gs *GameState) applyCannotBlockUntilEOT(source *Card, card *Card) {
	if gs.EffectManager != nil {
		gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
			Source:          source,
			SourceID:        source.ID,
			AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
			CannotBlock:     true,
			UntilEOT:        true,
		})
	}
	card.CannotBlock = true
}

// executeCantBeBlockedTargetEffect - "Target creature can't be blocked this turn."
// Registers an until-end-of-turn evasion grant on the target: no creature may be
// declared as a blocker for it during this turn's combat.
func (gs *GameState) executeCantBeBlockedTargetEffect(source *Card, targets []Target) error {
	// Self shape (? round 2026-08-03): "~ can't be blocked this turn." — no
	// target at all, the restriction is on the SOURCE (Daring Saboteur
	// class). Untargeted calls were a silent no-op before.
	if len(targets) == 0 && source != nil {
		if gs.EffectManager != nil {
			gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
				Source:          source,
				SourceID:        source.ID,
				AffectsSpecific: source.ID,
				CannotBeBlocked: true,
				UntilEOT:        true,
			})
		}
		source.CannotBeBlocked = true
		return nil
	}
	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		if gs.EffectManager != nil {
			gs.EffectManager.Register(&ContinuousEffect{
				Source:          source,
				SourceID:        source.ID,
				AffectsSpecific: card.ID,
				CannotBeBlocked: true,
				UntilEOT:        true,
			})
		}
		card.CannotBeBlocked = true
	}
	return nil
}

// executeTokenChoiceEffect resolves a create-token CHOICE among several
// predefined token types (create_choice_token primitive, ticket
// engine.auto-create-choice-token-856a4c6872, Ant-Man's Army class:
// "create a Food token or a Treasure token"). EffectValue["token_type_choices"]
// is a closed list of predefined token_type names (see buildPredefinedToken);
// the controller's choice is folded to a single "token_type" here (first
// entry — mirrors the existing auto-select convention for ungated choices,
// e.g. Cayth, Famed Mechanist's Fabricate mode auto-select) and handed
// straight to executeTokenEffect, so the resolved token is created through
// the SAME buildPredefinedToken path a single create_token clause already
// uses. A plain token_type clause with no choices list is untouched.
func (gs *GameState) executeTokenChoiceEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	choices, ok := effectValue["token_type_choices"].([]interface{})
	if !ok || len(choices) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	chosen, _ := choices[0].(string)
	if chosen == "" {
		return nil
	}
	resolved := map[string]interface{}{
		"token_type": chosen,
		"count":      1,
	}
	if tapped, ok := effectValue["tapped"].(bool); ok {
		resolved["tapped"] = tapped
	}
	return gs.executeTokenEffect(controller, resolved)
}

// executeTokenEffect - "Create N X/Y creature tokens" (or predefined tokens
// like Treasure / Food / Clue when token_type is set).
func (gs *GameState) executeTokenEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	count := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 1)
	if amt := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0); amt > 0 {
		count = amt
	}

	// create_token_dynamic_count primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-create-token-dynamic-count-afe77ae1d3, Aatchik, Emerald
	// Radian class: "create a 1/1 green Insect creature token for each
	// artifact and/or creature card in your graveyard"). Kept as a
	// narrowly-scoped, self-contained check rather than widening the
	// shared amount_count fold in dynamic_amount.go, which runs before
	// this executor and already sets "amount" for every other effect
	// kind — touching it risked shifting behavior for unrelated
	// already-passing create_token callers. Only fires for the exact
	// {"count":"graveyard","card_type":"artifact_or_creature"} shape;
	// any other amount_count spec (or its absence) leaves count
	// untouched.
	if ac, ok := effectValue["amount_count"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {
		if n, resolved := gs.countGraveyardArtifactOrCreatureCards(controller, ac); resolved {
			count = n
		}
	}

	tapped, _ := effectValue["tapped"].(bool)

	// Predefined token shortcuts. These produce non-creature artifact
	// tokens with built-in activated abilities (e.g. Treasure's
	// "Sacrifice this token: Add one mana of any color").
	if tt := strings.ToLower(getStringValue(effectValue, "token_type", "")); tt != "" {
		for i := 0; i < count; i++ {
			tok := buildPredefinedToken(tt, controller)
			if tok == nil {
				continue
			}
			if tapped {
				tok.Tapped = true
			}
			gs.Battlefield.AddCard(tok)
			gs.RegisterCardAbilities(tok)
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				triggered := gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateETBEvent(tok, controller))
				for _, a := range triggered {
					gs.PutTriggeredAbilityOnStack(a)
				}
			}
		}
		return nil
	}

	power := getIntValue(effectValue, "power", 1)
	toughness := getIntValue(effectValue, "toughness", 1)
	tokenName := getStringValue(effectValue, "name", "Token")
	tokenType := getStringValue(effectValue, "subtype", "")
	tokenColor := getStringValue(effectValue, "color", "")
	cantBlock, _ := effectValue["cant_block"].(bool)

	for i := 0; i < count; i++ {
		token := NewToken(tokenName, power, toughness, controller)
		if tokenType != "" {
			token.SubTypes = []string{tokenType}
		}

		// generic_create_token_extra_card_type primitive (ticket #3129,
		// REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:create_token / "Gnome/Thopter
		// artifact creature tokens" family): the generic creature-token
		// path above (NewToken, Type==Creature only) had no value-map
		// field to stamp a SECOND primary card type (Artifact) — only the
		// hardcoded token_type=="construct" shortcut in
		// buildPredefinedToken set t.Types. extra_card_types
		// (parser-supplied, out of scope here) lists the additional
		// primary types to stamp onto token.Types alongside the implicit
		// "Creature", mirroring buildPredefinedToken's construct case
		// (t.Types = []string{"Artifact","Creature"}). "Creature" is
		// always present (it's NewToken's canonical Type) so duplicates
		// are skipped.
		if extra, ok := effectValue["extra_card_types"].([]interface{}); ok {
			types := make([]string, 0, len(extra)+1)
			for _, e := range extra {
				s, ok := e.(string)
				if !ok || s == "" || s == "Creature" {
					continue
				}
				types = append(types, s)
			}
			if len(types) > 0 {
				token.Types = append(types, "Creature")
			}
		}

		// token_secondary_type primitive (ticket #3129, REPARSE-MAP
		// verb_unmapped:create_token / double-subtype "X Y creature
		// tokens" family — e.g. "Human Soldier", "Goblin Warrior",
		// "Zombie Knight"): the generic creature-token path above stamps
		// only the SINGLE subtype from the `subtype` value
		// (token.SubTypes = []string{tokenType}), so a token whose
		// printed text names a SECOND creature type had nowhere to carry
		// it. secondary_type (parser-supplied, out of scope here) is that
		// second subtype, appended alongside the primary with duplicates
		// skipped — mirroring the extra_card_types dedup just above and
		// the predefined Eldrazi Spawn/Scion templates' SubTypes =
		// []string{"Eldrazi","Spawn"}.
		if sec, ok := effectValue["secondary_type"].(string); ok && sec != "" {
			dup := false
			for _, st := range token.SubTypes {
				if st == sec {
					dup = true
					break
				}
			}
			if !dup {
				token.SubTypes = append(token.SubTypes, sec)
			}
		}

		// Set token color(s)
		if tokenColor != "" {
			token.Colors = parseColors(tokenColor)
		}

		// task-2364 (verb_unmapped:create_token): "...creature token with
		// 'This token can't block.'" — CannotBlock is the same plain bool
		// field cards/converter.go's static "cant_block" case sets at
		// CardDefinition->Card conversion time; a token never goes through
		// that conversion, so it's set directly here instead.
		if cantBlock {
			token.CannotBlock = true
		}

		// Add keywords if specified
		if keywords, ok := effectValue["keywords"].([]interface{}); ok {
			for _, kw := range keywords {
				kwStr, ok := kw.(string)
				if !ok {
					continue
				}
				// task-2364: "Toxic N" carries a numeric value no other
				// token-grantable keyword does — Rule 702.164 damage reads
				// Card.ToxicN alongside the bare Toxic keyword (mirrors
				// cards/converter.go's top-level "toxic" ability case).
				if rest, found := strings.CutPrefix(kwStr, "Toxic "); found {
					if n, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(rest)); err == nil && n > 0 {
						token.ToxicN = n
						token.Keywords = append(token.Keywords, Toxic)
						continue
					}
				}
				token.Keywords = append(token.Keywords, Keyword(kwStr))
			}
		}

		// Tokens enter the battlefield with summoning sickness
		token.SummoningSick = true
		if tapped {
			token.Tapped = true
		}
		gs.Battlefield.AddCard(token)

		// Fire ETB event for the token
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			triggered := gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateETBEvent(token, controller))
			for _, ability := range triggered {
				gs.PutTriggeredAbilityOnStack(ability)
			}
		}
	}

	return nil
}

// buildPredefinedToken constructs the well-known token templates (treasure /
// food / clue / gold). Returns nil if the type is unknown.
func buildPredefinedToken(kind string, controller int) *Card {
	switch kind {
	case "treasure":
		t := NewCard("Treasure", TypeArtifact, ManaCost{}, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Treasure"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:             t.ID + "-treasure-mana",
				SourceCard:     t,
				Cost:           &ActivationCost{Tap: true, Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:         "add_mana",
				EffectValue:    map[string]interface{}{"color": "any", "amount": 1},
				IsManaAbility:  true,
				RequiresChoice: "color",
			},
		}
		return t
	case "food":
