		t := NewCard("Food", TypeArtifact, ManaCost{}, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Food"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:          t.ID + "-food-life",
				SourceCard:  t,
				Cost:        &ActivationCost{Mana: ManaCost{Generic: 2}, Tap: true, Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:      "life_gain",
				EffectValue: map[string]interface{}{"amount": 3},
			},
		}
		return t
	case "clue":
		t := NewCard("Clue", TypeArtifact, ManaCost{}, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Clue"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:          t.ID + "-clue-draw",
				SourceCard:  t,
				Cost:        &ActivationCost{Mana: ManaCost{Generic: 2}, Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:      "draw",
				EffectValue: map[string]interface{}{"amount": 1},
			},
		}
		return t
	case "blood":
		// Blood token: artifact with "{1}, {T}, Discard a card, Sacrifice
		// this token: Draw a card."
		t := NewCard("Blood", TypeArtifact, ManaCost{}, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Blood"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:          t.ID + "-blood-draw",
				SourceCard:  t,
				Cost:        &ActivationCost{Mana: ManaCost{Generic: 1}, Tap: true, Discard: 1, Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:      "draw",
				EffectValue: map[string]interface{}{"amount": 1},
			},
		}
		return t
	case "gold":
		// Gold token: artifact with "Sacrifice this token: Add one mana of
		// any color." (no tap, unlike Treasure — task-1923).
		t := NewCard("Gold", TypeArtifact, ManaCost{}, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Gold"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:             t.ID + "-gold-mana",
				SourceCard:     t,
				Cost:           &ActivationCost{Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:         "add_mana",
				EffectValue:    map[string]interface{}{"color": "any", "amount": 1},
				IsManaAbility:  true,
				RequiresChoice: "color",
			},
		}
		return t
	case "powerstone":
		// task-2465 (verb_unmapped:create_token): Powerstone token —
		// artifact with "{T}: Add {C}. This mana can't be spent to cast a
		// nonartifact spell." (Rule 106.4 restricted mana, Ashnod, Flesh
		// Mechanist class). No sacrifice cost, unlike Treasure/Blood — the
		// token stays on the battlefield as a repeatable mana source.
		// "restriction":"artifact" is the SAME ArtifactMana pool the
		// existing standalone Powerstone add_mana ability already uses
		// (game/activated_abilities.go executeAddManaEffect).
		t := NewCard("Powerstone", TypeArtifact, ManaCost{}, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Powerstone"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:            t.ID + "-powerstone-mana",
				SourceCard:    t,
				Cost:          &ActivationCost{Tap: true},
				Effect:        "add_mana",
				EffectValue:   map[string]interface{}{"color": "colorless", "amount": 1, "restriction": "artifact"},
				IsManaAbility: true,
			},
		}
		return t
	case "mutagen":
		// task-2364 (verb_unmapped:create_token): Mutagen token — artifact
		// with "{1}, {T}, Sacrifice this token: Put a +1/+1 counter on
		// target creature. Activate only as a sorcery." (Michelangelo,
		// Raphael the Muscle, Splinter the Mentor, The Ooze class).
		t := NewCard("Mutagen", TypeArtifact, ManaCost{}, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Mutagen"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:                t.ID + "-mutagen-counter",
				SourceCard:        t,
				Cost:              &ActivationCost{Mana: ManaCost{Generic: 1}, Tap: true, Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:            "put_counters",
				EffectValue:       map[string]interface{}{"counter_type": "+1/+1", "amount": 1},
				Target:            &TriggerTargetFilter{Type: "creature"},
				TimingRestriction: "sorcery",
			},
		}
		return t
	case "junk":
		// task-2364 (verb_unmapped:create_token): Junk token — artifact with
		// "{T}, Sacrifice this token: Exile the top card of your library.
		// You may play that card this turn. Activate only as a sorcery."
		// (Crimson Caravaneer, Junk Jet, Junktown, Veronica class).
		t := NewCard("Junk", TypeArtifact, ManaCost{}, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Junk"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:                t.ID + "-junk-impulse",
				SourceCard:        t,
				Cost:              &ActivationCost{Tap: true, Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:            "exile_top_may_play",
				EffectValue:       map[string]interface{}{"amount": 1},
				TimingRestriction: "sorcery",
			},
		}
		return t
	case "lander":
		// task-2364 (verb_unmapped:create_token): Lander token — artifact
		// with "{2}, {T}, Sacrifice this token: Search your library for a
		// basic land card, put it onto the battlefield tapped, then
		// shuffle." (Horizon Explorer class).
		t := NewCard("Lander", TypeArtifact, ManaCost{}, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Lander"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:          t.ID + "-lander-search",
				SourceCard:  t,
				Cost:        &ActivationCost{Mana: ManaCost{Generic: 2}, Tap: true, Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:      "search",
				EffectValue: map[string]interface{}{"filter": "basic land", "to": "battlefield_tapped"},
			},
		}
		return t
	case "construct":
		t := NewToken("Construct", 1, 1, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Construct"}
		t.Types = []string{"Artifact", "Creature"}
		return t
	case "eldrazi_spawn":
		t := NewToken("Eldrazi Spawn", 0, 1, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Eldrazi", "Spawn"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:            t.ID + "-spawn-mana",
				SourceCard:    t,
				Cost:          &ActivationCost{Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:        "add_mana",
				EffectValue:   map[string]interface{}{"color": "C", "amount": 1},
				IsManaAbility: true,
			},
		}
		return t
	case "eldrazi_scion":
		// task-2364 (verb_unmapped:create_token): "1/1 colorless Eldrazi
		// Scion creature token with 'Sacrifice this token: Add {C}.'" — same
		// sacrifice-for-{C} ability as Eldrazi Spawn above, just 1/1 instead
		// of 0/1 (Spawnbed Protector, Vile Redeemer).
		t := NewToken("Eldrazi Scion", 1, 1, controller)
		t.SubTypes = []string{"Eldrazi", "Scion"}
		t.IsToken = true
		t.ActivatedAbilities = []*ActivatedAbility{
			{
				ID:            t.ID + "-scion-mana",
				SourceCard:    t,
				Cost:          &ActivationCost{Sacrifice: "self"},
				Effect:        "add_mana",
				EffectValue:   map[string]interface{}{"color": "C", "amount": 1},
				IsManaAbility: true,
			},
		}
		return t
	}
	return nil
}

// executeExileArtifactGYCreateConstruct — Urza's Cave–style: exile a targeted
// artifact card from any graveyard, then create a 1/1 colorless Construct
// artifact creature token. The exile half is optional on resolution (if the
// target card is no longer in any graveyard it simply doesn't happen), but
// the Construct token is always created.
func (gs *GameState) executeExileArtifactGYCreateConstruct(controller int, targets []Target) error {
	if len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard {
		cardID := targets[0].CardID
		for _, p := range gs.Players {
			if c, err := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(cardID); err == nil {
				owner := c.Owner
				if owner < 0 || owner >= len(gs.Players) {
					owner = controller
				}
				gs.Players[owner].Exile.AddCard(c)
				break
			}
		}
	}
	return gs.executeTokenEffect(controller, map[string]interface{}{
		"token_type": "construct",
		"count":      1,
	})
}

// matchesActivatedSearchFilter handles the small filter vocabulary used by
// activated searches like fetchlands ("Island|Mountain") plus the same
// keywords matchesTutorFilter understands (creature/land/instant/...).
func matchesActivatedSearchFilter(c *Card, filter string) bool {
	if c == nil || filter == "" {
		return false
	}
	// "Island|Mountain" — match if the card's basic land type or any
	// subtype equals one of the alternatives.
	if strings.Contains(filter, "|") {
		for _, alt := range strings.Split(filter, "|") {
			if matchesTutorFilter(c, strings.TrimSpace(alt)) {
				return true
			}
		}
		return false
	}
	return matchesTutorFilter(c, filter)
}

// executeCopyTokensEnteredThisTurn implements Ocelot Pride's city's blessing
// clause: "for each token you control that entered this turn, create a token
// that's a copy of it." The city's blessing check is done here (not in the
// if_condition gate) because the oracle text uses a non-intervening "then if".
func (gs *GameState) executeCopyTokensEnteredThisTurn(controller int) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	if !gs.Players[controller].HasCitysBlessing {
		return nil
	}
	// Snapshot the list so newly created copies don't also get copied.
	tokenIDs := append([]string(nil), gs.Players[controller].TokensEnteredThisTurn...)
	for _, tokenID := range tokenIDs {
		src, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(tokenID)
		if err != nil || src == nil || src.Controller != controller {
			continue
		}
		tok := cloneToken(src, controller)
		tok.SummoningSick = true
		gs.Battlefield.AddCard(tok)
		gs.RegisterCardAbilities(tok)
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			triggered := gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateETBEvent(tok, controller))
			for _, a := range triggered {
				gs.PutTriggeredAbilityOnStack(a)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeCopyAllTokensYouControl implements Second Harvest's
// "For each token you control, create a token that's a copy of that permanent."
// Every token the controller currently has on the battlefield is copied once.
func (gs *GameState) executeCopyAllTokensYouControl(controller int) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	// Snapshot the matching tokens first so newly created copies aren't copied.
	var sources []*Card
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if c != nil && c.IsToken && c.Controller == controller {
			sources = append(sources, c)
		}
	}
	for _, src := range sources {
		tok := cloneToken(src, controller)
		tok.SummoningSick = true
		gs.Battlefield.AddCard(tok)
		gs.RegisterCardAbilities(tok)
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			triggered := gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateETBEvent(tok, controller))
			for _, a := range triggered {
				gs.PutTriggeredAbilityOnStack(a)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeCreateTokenCopyOfTrigger implements Necroduality's
// "Whenever a nontoken Zombie you control enters, create a token that's a copy
// of that creature." The entering creature is threaded in as targets[0] by
// PutTriggeredAbilityOnStackWithEvent. The copy is itself a token, so the
// trigger's nontoken gate keeps it from re-triggering indefinitely.
func (gs *GameState) executeCreateTokenCopyOfTrigger(controller int, targets []Target) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	if len(targets) == 0 || !targets[0].IsCard || targets[0].CardID == "" {
		return nil
	}
	src, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(targets[0].CardID)
	if err != nil || src == nil {
		// sacrifice_trigger_create_token_copy (ticket #3859, Ashling the
		// Limitless class): "Whenever you sacrifice a nontoken Elemental,
		// create a token that's a copy of it." By resolution time the
		// sacrificed permanent has left the battlefield — Sacrifice moves it
		// to its owner's graveyard BEFORE publishing EventSacrifice — so fall
		// back to a graveyard lookup by the CardID putTriggeredAbilityOnStackOnce
		// threaded in from event.Source. Same atomic copy-token behavior as
		// the battlefield path above; only the copy-source zone differs.
		for _, p := range gs.Players {
			if c, gErr := p.Graveyard.GetCard(targets[0].CardID); gErr == nil && c != nil {
				src = c
				break
			}
		}
	}
	if src == nil {
		return nil
	}
	tok := cloneToken(src, controller)
	tok.SummoningSick = true
	gs.Battlefield.AddCard(tok)
	gs.RegisterCardAbilities(tok)
	if gs.EventBus != nil {
		triggered := gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateETBEvent(tok, controller))
		for _, a := range triggered {
			gs.PutTriggeredAbilityOnStack(a)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// cloneToken creates a new token whose visible characteristics mirror src.
// Used by Ocelot Pride's "create a token that's a copy of it" clause.
func cloneToken(src *Card, controller int) *Card {
	p, t := 0, 0
	if src.BasePower != nil {
		p = *src.BasePower
	}
	if src.BaseToughness != nil {
		t = *src.BaseToughness
	}
	tok := NewToken(src.Name, p, t, controller)
	tok.Type = src.Type
	tok.SubTypes = append([]string(nil), src.SubTypes...)
	tok.Types = append([]string(nil), src.Types...)
	tok.Colors = append([]Color(nil), src.Colors...)
	tok.Keywords = append([]Keyword(nil), src.Keywords...)
	tok.Text = src.Text
	return tok
}

// parseColors converts color string(s) to Color slice
func parseColors(colorStr string) []Color {
	colors := []Color{}
	// Handle single or multiple colors
	for _, c := range colorStr {
		switch c {
		case 'W', 'w':
			colors = append(colors, White)
		case 'U', 'u':
			colors = append(colors, Blue)
		case 'B', 'b':
			colors = append(colors, Black)
		case 'R', 'r':
			colors = append(colors, Red)
		case 'G', 'g':
			colors = append(colors, Green)
		}
	}
	return colors
}

// executeMillEffect - "Target player mills N cards"
func (gs *GameState) executeMillEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)

	// Determine target player
	targetPlayer := 1 - controller // Default to opponent
	if b, _ := effectValue["self"].(bool); b {
		targetPlayer = controller // "mill N cards" with no stated subject means you mill
	}
	if len(targets) > 0 && !targets[0].IsCard {
		targetPlayer = targets[0].PlayerIdx
	}

	if targetPlayer < 0 || targetPlayer >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}

	player := gs.Players[targetPlayer]
	milled := 0
	for i := 0; i < amount; i++ {
		card, err := player.Library.DrawCard()
		if err != nil {
			break // Library empty
		}
		player.Graveyard.AddCard(card)
		milled++
	}
	// linked_amount tally (dynamic-amount round): the count ACTUALLY milled
	// (library may run out short of amount) for a later "draw that many
	// cards" consumer; no-op outside a sequence walk.
	gs.noteSeqTally("cards_milled", milled)

	return nil
}

// executeMillGainLifePerCreature - "Mill N cards. You gain X life for each
// creature card milled this way." (Gorging Vulture). The controller mills
// their own library; the life gain depends on what was actually milled, so
// both happen in one effect.
func (gs *GameState) executeMillGainLifePerCreature(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)
	lifePerCard := getIntValue(effectValue, "life_per_card", 1)

	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}

	player := gs.Players[controller]
	creatures := 0
	for i := 0; i < amount; i++ {
		card, err := player.Library.DrawCard()
		if err != nil {
			break // Library empty
		}
		if card.IsCreature() {
			creatures++
		}
		player.Graveyard.AddCard(card)
	}

	gs.GainLife(controller, creatures*lifePerCard)
	return nil
}

// executeDiscardEffect - "Target player discards N cards"
func (gs *GameState) executeDiscardEffect(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 1)

	if targetType, ok := effectValue["target"].(string); ok && targetType == "each_player" {
		// each_player_discard primitive (task-2485, verb_unmapped:p_discard):
		// "each player discards a card[ at random]" (Stronghold Rats class) —
		// genuine per-player-scoped discard, distinct from the self/opponent
		// binary below. Mirrors executeLifeLossEffect/executeDamageEffect's
		// identical each_player branches above. Auto-selects the first N
		// cards in hand order per player rather than opening a PendingChoice
		// (same no-interactive-chooser convention as cardfns/lib_otj.go's
		// Discard helper) — PendingChoice only tracks one player's pick at a
		// time and this effect needs every player to choose independently.
		random := getBoolValue(effectValue, "random", false)
		for pi := range gs.Players {
			player := gs.Players[pi]
			if player.Hand.Count() == 0 {
				continue
			}
			count := amount
			if player.Hand.Count() < count {
				count = player.Hand.Count()
			}
			candidates := make([]string, 0, player.Hand.Count())
			for _, c := range player.Hand.GetAll() {
				candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
			}
			for i := 0; i < count && len(candidates) > 0; i++ {
				idx := 0
				if random {
					idx = rand.Intn(len(candidates))
				}
				cardID := candidates[idx]
				candidates = append(candidates[:idx], candidates[idx+1:]...)
				if err := gs.Discard(pi, cardID); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
		}
		return nil
	}

	// Determine target player.
	targetPlayer := 1 - controller // Default to opponent.
	if targetType, ok := effectValue["target"].(string); ok && targetType == "you" {
		// Self-discard ("Draw a card, then discard a card." / "discard
		// your hand"), reparse.py's bare (non-p_discard) 'discard' verb —
		// distinct from the no-target default above, which existing
		// each/target-opponent records rely on.
		targetPlayer = controller
	}
	if len(targets) > 0 && !targets[0].IsCard {
		targetPlayer = targets[0].PlayerIdx
	}

	if targetPlayer < 0 || targetPlayer >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}

	player := gs.Players[targetPlayer]
	if player.Hand.Count() == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	// Clamp to how many cards the player actually holds.
	count := amount
	if player.Hand.Count() < count {
		count = player.Hand.Count()
	}

	candidates := make([]string, 0, player.Hand.Count())
	for _, c := range player.Hand.GetAll() {
		candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
	}

	if getBoolValue(effectValue, "random", false) {
		// "discard[s] a/N card(s) at random" — pick without the discarding
		// player's input (Burning Inquiry, Cool but Rude, Zurzoth, Chaos
		// Rider). Re-sample the shrinking candidate pool each pick so a
		// multi-card random discard never picks the same card twice; each
		// pick routes through gs.Discard so "whenever you discard" triggers
		// still fire per card, same as the hand_discard PendingChoice path.
		for i := 0; i < count && len(candidates) > 0; i++ {
			idx := rand.Intn(len(candidates))
			cardID := candidates[idx]
			candidates = append(candidates[:idx], candidates[idx+1:]...)
			if err := gs.Discard(targetPlayer, cardID); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	}

	gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
		PlayerIdx:  targetPlayer,
		ChoiceType: "hand_discard",
		Candidates: candidates,
		Count:      count,
	}
	return nil
}

// executeTargetPlayerDrawDiscard handles "{T}: Target player draws N cards,
// then discards M cards." (Reckless Scholar). The chosen player both draws and
// discards; the discard opens a PendingChoice for that same player. Falls back
// to the controller when no player target was supplied.
func (gs *GameState) executeTargetPlayerDrawDiscard(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	drawN := getIntValue(effectValue, "draw", 1)
	discardN := getIntValue(effectValue, "discard", 1)

	targetPlayer := controller
	if len(targets) > 0 && !targets[0].IsCard {
		targetPlayer = targets[0].PlayerIdx
	}
	if targetPlayer < 0 || targetPlayer >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}

	if drawN > 0 {
		if err := gs.DrawCardsWithEvent(targetPlayer, drawN); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	if discardN <= 0 {
		return nil
	}

	player := gs.Players[targetPlayer]
	if player.Hand.Count() == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	count := discardN
	if player.Hand.Count() < count {
		count = player.Hand.Count()
	}

	candidates := make([]string, 0, player.Hand.Count())
	for _, c := range player.Hand.GetAll() {
		candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
	}

	gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
		PlayerIdx:  targetPlayer,
		ChoiceType: "hand_discard",
		Candidates: candidates,
		Count:      count,
	}
	return nil
}

// executeCounterEffect - "Counter target spell"
func (gs *GameState) executeCounterEffect(targets []Target) error {
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.StackID != "" {
			// counter-target-ability round (park 2400): StackID reaches
			// abilities AND spells. A countered ability simply ceases; a
			// countered spell goes to its owner's graveyard — unless it is
			// a copy, which ceases to exist (Rule 706.10, task-2387).
			obj, err := gs.Stack.RemoveByStackID(target.StackID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			if !obj.IsAbility && !obj.IsCopy && obj.Card != nil &&
				obj.Card.Owner >= 0 && obj.Card.Owner < len(gs.Players) {
				gs.Players[obj.Card.Owner].Graveyard.AddCard(obj.Card)
			}
			continue
		}
		if target.SpellID != "" {
			obj, err := gs.Stack.RemoveByID(target.SpellID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			// Countered spell goes to graveyard (copies cease, Rule 706.10).
			if !obj.IsCopy && obj.Card != nil && obj.Card.Owner >= 0 && obj.Card.Owner < len(gs.Players) {
				gs.Players[obj.Card.Owner].Graveyard.AddCard(obj.Card)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeCounterMaxManaValueReferent implements the
// counter_with_dynamic_mana_value_referent primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-counter-with-dynamic-mana-value-referent-9b29c53675,
// Spellstutter Sprite class: "When this creature enters, counter target
// spell with mana value X or less, where X is the number of Faeries you
// control."). Unlike the unconditional executeCounterEffect above, the
// mana-value cap here is a LIVE state-dependent referent — already folded
// into effectValue["amount"] by resolveDynamicAmountFull's generic
// "battlefield"+"subtype" amount_count kind (dynamic_amount.go) before this
// executor runs, exactly like executeDestroyUpToDynamicPermanentCount's own
// X. A target spell whose mana value exceeds that live count is simply left
// on the stack (Rule 608.2b: an illegal-to-counter target is skipped, not
// an error) rather than countered anyway — distinguishing this from both a
// fixed numeric cap (Counterspell) and a cap sourced from the countered
// spell's own mana value (Spellstutter Pixie's X spent).
func (gs *GameState) executeCounterMaxManaValueReferent(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	maxManaValue := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", -1)
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.SpellID == "" {
			continue
		}
		spellObj, err := gs.Stack.GetByID(target.SpellID)
		if err != nil || spellObj == nil {
			continue
		}
		if maxManaValue >= 0 && spellObj.Card != nil && spellObj.Card.ManaCost.CMC() > maxManaValue {
			continue
		}
		if err := gs.CounterSpell(target.SpellID); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeTapEffect - "Tap target permanent" (also "tap enchanted permanent"
// via target_attached — task-3188, mirrors executePutCounterEffect's/
// executeTokenCopyEffect's AttachedTo back-reference convention, task-1979).
// effectValue["counter_type"], when non-empty, additionally puts
// effectValue["amount"] (default 1) counters of that type on each object
// THIS SAME effect just tapped (ticket
// engine.auto-tap-put-counters-on-target-58f2ddfb82, Meat Locker // Drowned
// Diner class: "tap up to one target creature and put two stun counters on
// it") — one atomic step rather than a separate put_counters atom,
// mirroring executeTapWithStunCounterEffect's fixed single-stun-counter
// case but with a caller-supplied type/amount. Plain "tap" (no
// counter_type key) is unaffected and stays tap-only.
//
// effectValue["max_targets"], when > 0, caps how many of the supplied
// targets actually get tapped (ticket
// engine.auto-up-to-n-target-creatures-tap-492924d0eb, Feeling of Dread
// class: "Tap up to two target creatures."). Cardinality below max_targets
// — including zero — is a legal resolution: the loop below already skips
// any target slot the caster declined (IsCard false / an unresolved
// CardID), so "up to N" just means "never tap past N" on top of that.
// Records with no max_targets key (the plain fixed-count shape
// TestShape_Activated_TapUntapTarget covers) are unaffected — every
// supplied target is tapped as before.
func (gs *GameState) executeTapEffect(source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	counterType := getStringValue(effectValue, "counter_type", "")
	counterAmount := 1
	switch v := effectValue["amount"].(type) {
	case int:
		counterAmount = v
	case float64:
		counterAmount = int(v)
	}
	maxTargets := 0
	switch v := effectValue["max_targets"].(type) {
	case int:
		maxTargets = v
	case float64:
		maxTargets = int(v)
	}
	if effectValue["target_attached"] != nil {
		if source != nil && source.AttachedTo != "" {
			if card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(source.AttachedTo); err == nil {
				card.Tap()
				if gs.EventBus != nil {
					gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
					gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
				}
				if counterType != "" && counterAmount > 0 {
					gs.AddCounters(card, CounterType(counterType), counterAmount)
				}
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	tapped := 0
	for _, target := range targets {
		if maxTargets > 0 && tapped >= maxTargets {
			break
		}
		if target.IsCard {
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			card.Tap()
			tapped++

			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
			}
			if counterType != "" && counterAmount > 0 {
				gs.AddCounters(card, CounterType(counterType), counterAmount)
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeTapAllEffect - mass tap (primitive tap_mass_or_multitarget, ticket
// 3187). "tap all creatures your opponents control" (Subjugator Angel) is the
// original shape, but value.filter is the SAME type-bucket vocabulary
// destroyAllMatching/exileAllMatching/executeUntapAllEffect already honor, so
// this now taps EVERY battlefield permanent matching filter — not just
// creatures — unlocking "tap all lands you control" (the old GetCreatures-only
// walk left every non-creature mass tap silently no-op'ing despite the
// converter already emitting value.filter). Non-targeted: resolves affected
// permanents at resolution time using the controller scope in effectValue
// ("opponent" = every player other than the ability's controller, "you" = the
// controller only, absent/"any" = both). Mirrors executeUntapAllEffect:
// value.other excludes the source by ID ("tap all OTHER ..."), and
// value.untapped restricts to currently-untapped permanents ("tap all untapped
// ..."). filter defaults to "creature" so pre-existing creature-only records
// keep their original behavior.
func (gs *GameState) executeTapAllEffect(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	scope := getStringValue(effectValue, "controller", "opponent")
	filter := getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "creature")
	excludeSelf := effectValue["other"] != nil
	untappedOnly, _ := effectValue["untapped"].(bool)
	// "tapped THIS WAY" producer tally (ticket #5021, REPARSE-MAP
	// verb_unmapped:damage / deal_damage_countqual, capability #120 — the
	// "deals damage ... equal to the number of X tapped this way" shape,
	// Angel's Trumpet class): count what THIS atom actually tapped and report
	// it under seqTally["tapped_"+filter] for a LATER damage atom in the same
	// sequence to read back through the "tapped_this_way" count spec
	// (dynamic_amount.go). The untappedOnly guard above already skips
	// permanents that were tapped BEFORE this atom, so they are NOT counted —
	// matching "tapped this way" (not "total tapped"). noteSeqTally is a
	// no-op outside a sequence walk, so this is safe to call unconditionally.
	tappedThisWay := 0
	for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		if card == nil {
			continue
		}
		switch scope {
		case "you":
			if card.Controller != controller {
				continue
			}
		case "opponent":
			if card.Controller == controller {
				continue
			}
		}
		if excludeSelf && source != nil && card.ID == source.ID {
			continue
		}
		if !matchesPermanentFilter(card, filter) {
			continue
		}
		if untappedOnly && card.Tapped {
			continue
		}
		card.Tap()
		tappedThisWay++
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
		}
	}
	gs.noteSeqTally("tapped_"+filter, tappedThisWay)
	return nil
}

// executeUntapEffect - "Untap target permanent"
func (gs *GameState) executeUntapEffect(targets []Target) error {
	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.IsCard {
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			card.Untap()

			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventUntapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeTapOrUntapChoiceTarget implements the choose_tap_or_untap_target
// primitive (ticket engine.auto-choose-tap-or-untap-target-79d61adb7e, Mind
// Over Matter class): "You may tap or untap target artifact, creature, or
// land." The controller's choice between the two mutually exclusive
// outcomes is captured in EffectValue["mode"] at effect-definition time —
// the same "mode" key executeTapUntapSharedCreatureTypeReferent reads for
// its own battlefield-wide tap/untap sweep — applied here to a single
// targeted permanent instead. mode=="untap" untaps the target (mirroring
// executeUntapEffect); any other value (default "tap") taps it (mirroring
// executeTapWithStunCounterEffect's direct Tapped=true, since no card in
// this primitive's class reacts to becoming tapped beyond the normal
// EventTapped/EventBecomesTapped pair). A target that isn't a card, or that
// no longer resolves on the battlefield, is silently skipped (Rule 601.2c).
func (gs *GameState) executeTapOrUntapChoiceTarget(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	mode, _ := effectValue["mode"].(string)
	// The whole effect is optional (Tideforce Elemental: "You may tap or
	// untap another target creature.") — a mode that is neither "tap" nor
	// "untap" means the controller declined, so the target's tap state
	// must be left unchanged rather than defaulting to a tap.
	if mode != "tap" && mode != "untap" {
		return nil
	}
	for _, target := range targets {
		if !target.IsCard || target.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
		if err != nil || card == nil {
			continue
		}
		if mode == "untap" {
			card.Untap()
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventUntapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
			}
		} else {
			card.Tapped = true
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeUntapAllEffect - "Untap all/each <filter> [you control/an opponent
// controls]" (Reset, Wilderness Reclamation, Aggravated Assault, Karrthus
// family). Unlike executeUntapEffect this is a non-targeted mass effect,
// resolved at execution time straight off the battlefield — controller
// scope defaults to "any" (both players) since a bare "untap all creatures"
// with no "you control"/"an opponent controls" suffix (Intruder Alarm) means
// executeDestroyUpToArtifactCreatureLand destroys every target actually
// supplied in targets — up to one artifact, one creature, and one land,
// each chosen (or declined) independently of the other two (Boom Box
// class: "Destroy up to one target artifact, up to one target creature,
// and up to one target land."). Declining a slot simply omits it from
// targets rather than erroring; any subset (including none) is legal, and
// destroying one slot never depends on whether the other slots were
// chosen.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyUpToArtifactCreatureLand(targets []Target) error {
	return gs.executeDestroyEffect(targets, false)
}

// executeDestroyUpToDynamicPermanentCount implements the
// destroy_up_to_dynamic_permanent_count primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-destroy-up-to-dynamic-permanent-count-8e715d81dc, Midnight
// Arsonist class: "destroy up to X target artifacts without mana
// abilities, where X is the number of Vampires you control"). Unlike a
// fixed "up to N target" shape (executeDestroyUpToArtifactCreatureLand,
// N baked into the number of slots) or a filter-scoped mass destroy
// ("destroy all X"), X here is a LIVE count resolved at resolution time
// via the amount_count fold (effectValue["amount"], already folded by
// the caller before this executor runs — see the "battlefield"+"subtype"
// amount_count kind in dynamic_amount.go). Individual target legality
// (e.g. "artifacts without mana abilities") is enforced upstream at
// targeting time, the same convention executeDestroyTargetWithManaValueEqualToX
// documents; this executor only enforces the numeric cap — at most
// `amount` of the supplied targets are destroyed, in the order given,
// with any excess simply left alone (Rule 601.2c partial legality: "up
// to X" never requires exactly X, and X==0 is a legal no-op).
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyUpToDynamicPermanentCount(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0)
	if amount <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	destroyed := 0
	for _, t := range targets {
		if destroyed >= amount {
			break
		}
		if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		if err := DestroyTarget(t, gs); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		destroyed++
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDestroyUpToLandsBySourceCounter implements the
// destroy_up_to_lands_by_source_counter primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-destroy-up-to-dynamic-targets-0722caa971, Rumbling Crescendo
// class: "Destroy up to X target lands, where X is the number of verse
// counters on this enchantment."). Unlike
// executeDestroyUpToDynamicPermanentCount, whose X arrives pre-folded into
// effectValue["amount"] via the generic amount_count pipeline, X here is
// read directly off the ability's OWN SOURCE permanent's counters
// (source.Counters.GetCount, Rule 122.3) at resolution time -- the counter
// type name comes from effectValue["counter_type"] (falling back to
// "verse" when absent, since this primitive exists specifically for
// verse-counter cards). Mirrors executeDestroyUpToDynamicPermanentCount's
// "at most X of the supplied targets, in the order given, excess left
// alone" cap (Rule 601.2c: "up to X" never requires exactly X, and X==0
// is a legal no-op) and only enforces the numeric cap -- individual
// target legality (must be a land) is enforced upstream at targeting
// time, the same convention executeDestroyUpToDynamicPermanentCount
// documents.
func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyUpToLandsBySourceCounter(source *Card, targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	if source == nil {
		return nil
	}
	counterType, _ := effectValue["counter_type"].(string)
	if counterType == "" {
		counterType = "verse"
	}
	amount := source.Counters.GetCount(CounterType(counterType))
	if amount <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	destroyed := 0
	for _, t := range targets {
		if destroyed >= amount {
			break
		}
		if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		if err := DestroyTarget(t, gs); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		destroyed++
	}
	return nil
}

func (gs *GameState) executeDestroyUpToCounterCountTargets(source *Card, targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	if source == nil {
		return nil
	}
	counterType, _ := effectValue["counter_type"].(string)
	if counterType == "" {
		return nil
	}
	amount := source.Counters.GetCount(CounterType(counterType))
	if amount <= 0 {
		return nil
	}

	filter := getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "")
	victims := make([]Target, 0, amount)
	for _, target := range targets {
		if len(victims) >= amount || !target.IsCard || target.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
		if err != nil || card == nil || !matchesPermanentFilter(card, filter) {
			continue
		}
		victims = append(victims, target)
	}
	for _, target := range victims {
		if err := DestroyTarget(target, gs); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// noteDestroyedArtifactsPerController records, per controller, how many of
// the permanents about to be destroyed by this atom are artifacts, keyed
// into gs.seqTally (the existing single-map-multi-key sequence tally,
// effect_sequence.go's noteSeqTally) under a per-player key so a later
// "damage to each player equal to X" atom in the same sequence can read
// back the count for its own specific recipient rather than one flat
// global total (ticket
// engine.auto-destroyed-this-way-per-player-count-58304e2f98,
// destroyed_this_way_per_player_count: Builder's Bane class). Must run
// BEFORE the targets are actually destroyed, since Controller is read off
// the still-live battlefield permanent.
func (gs *GameState) noteDestroyedArtifactsPerController(targets []Target) {
	if gs.seqTally == nil || gs.Battlefield == nil {
		return
	}
	counts := map[int]int{}
	for _, t := range targets {
		if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		perm, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(t.CardID)
		if err != nil || perm == nil {
			continue
		}
		if isArtifactPermanent(perm) {
			counts[perm.Controller]++
		}
	}
	for idx, n := range counts {
		gs.seqTally["destroyed_artifact_p"+strconv.Itoa(idx)] = n
	}
}

// executeDamageEachPlayerArtifactsDestroyedThisWay implements
// destroyed_this_way_per_player_count's damage side: each player is dealt
// damage equal to THEIR OWN count from noteDestroyedArtifactsPerController
// above, read back from gs.seqTally keyed by that specific player's index
// -- not one flat total applied to every player alike.
func (gs *GameState) executeDamageEachPlayerArtifactsDestroyedThisWay() error {
	if gs.seqTally == nil {
		return nil
	}
	for idx, p := range gs.Players {
		if p == nil {
			continue
		}
		n, ok := gs.seqTally["destroyed_artifact_p"+strconv.Itoa(idx)]
		if !ok || n <= 0 {
			continue
		}
		p.Life -= n
	}
	gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
	return nil
}

// literally every creature, matching executeTapAllEffect's "opponent"-scope
// convention for the analogous tap_all effect. "amount"/"up_to" (partial-N
// selection, e.g. "untap up to four lands you control") would need a real
// PendingChoice the engine doesn't have yet for this effect — rather than
// silently untap MORE permanents than the card says, such records are a
// deliberate no-op here until that choice flow exists.
func (gs *GameState) executeUntapAllEffect(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	if _, ok := effectValue["up_to"]; ok {
		return nil
	}
	if _, ok := effectValue["amount"]; ok {
		return nil
	}
	scope := getStringValue(effectValue, "controller", "any")
	filter := getStringValue(effectValue, "filter", "permanent")
	subtype := getStringValue(effectValue, "subtype", "")
	attackingOnly, _ := effectValue["attacking"].(bool)
	// task-2381 (verb_unmapped:untap): "untap each <X> [you control] with a
	// counter on it" (The Thirteenth Doctor, Xolatoyac the Smiling Flood) —
	// restrict the mass untap to cards carrying at least one counter of any
	// type. HasCounters covers +1/+1, -1/-1, loyalty, charge, and keyword
	// counters alike, matching the printed "a counter" (any counter) wording.
	withCounterOnly, _ := effectValue["has_counter"].(bool)
	// task-2435 (verb_unmapped:untap): "untap all modified creatures you
	// control" (Akki Battle Squad, Rule 700.15) — reuses isModifiedPermanent,
	// the same has-a-counter-or-an-Equipment/Aura-attached check the
	// AffectsModified continuous-effect scope already ships live for.
	modifiedOnly, _ := effectValue["modified"].(bool)
	// task-2435 (verb_unmapped:untap): "untap each OTHER <X> you control"
	// (Myr Galvanizer) — same self-exclusion idiom as put_counters' "each"
	// mass shape just above (effectValue["other"] != nil, excludes source by
	// ID), now proven for executeUntapAllEffect too.
	excludeSelf := effectValue["other"] != nil
	for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		switch scope {
		case "you":
			if card.Controller != controller {
				continue
			}
		case "opponent":
			if card.Controller == controller {
				continue
			}
		}
		if excludeSelf && source != nil && card.ID == source.ID {
			continue
		}
		if !matchesUntapAllFilter(card, filter) {
			continue
		}
		if subtype != "" && !card.HasSubType(subtype) {
			continue
		}
		if attackingOnly && !card.Attacking {
			continue
		}
		if withCounterOnly && !card.Counters.HasCounters() {
			continue
		}
		if modifiedOnly && (gs.EffectManager == nil || !isModifiedPermanent(gs.EffectManager, card)) {
			continue
		}
		if !card.Tapped {
			continue
		}
		card.Untap()
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventUntapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// matchesUntapAllFilter checks a battlefield card's type against untap_all's
// "filter" value — the same coarse buckets matchesConditionFilter already
// recognizes (creature/artifact/enchantment/land/permanent), plus
// "nonland_permanent" ("nonland permanents you control", Unstoppable Plan).
// An "_or_"-joined union (task-2381: Awakening "untap all creatures and
// lands" -> "creature_or_land") splits and honors each component, mirroring
// matchesPermanentFilter's already-proven union handling for single-target
// destroy/exile.
func matchesUntapAllFilter(card *Card, filter string) bool {
	if strings.Contains(filter, "_or_") {
		for _, part := range strings.Split(filter, "_or_") {
			if matchesUntapAllFilter(card, part) {
				return true
			}
		}
		return false
	}
	switch filter {
	case "creature":
		return card.IsCreature()
	case "artifact":
		return card.Type == TypeArtifact
	case "enchantment":
		return card.Type == TypeEnchantment
	case "land":
		return card.IsLand()
	case "nonland_permanent":
		return card.IsPermanent() && !card.IsLand()
	case "permanent", "":
		return card.IsPermanent()
	default:
		return false
	}
}

// executeTapAndPutCounterEffect - "Tap target permanent and put a counter on it"
func (gs *GameState) executeTapAndPutCounterEffect(targets []Target, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	counterType := getStringValue(effectValue, "counter_type", "stun")

	for _, target := range targets {
		if target.IsCard {
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}

			// Tap the card
			card.Tap()
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
				gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(NewEvent(EventBecomesTapped).WithSource(card).WithPlayer(card.Controller))
			}

			// Put the counter on the card (via gs.AddCounters so counter-
			// placement replacement effects like Hardened Scales apply).
			gs.AddCounters(card, CounterType(counterType), 1)
		}
	}

	gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
	return nil
}

// executeSacAllOpponentCreatures — Emrakul-style mass sacrifice: the opponent
// (1-controller in a two-player game) sacrifices every creature they control.
func (gs *GameState) executeSacAllOpponentCreatures(controller int) error {
	oppIdx := 1 - controller
	if oppIdx < 0 || oppIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	// Snapshot the list first; sacrificing mutates the battlefield.
	var toSac []*Card
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.Cards {
		if c.Controller == oppIdx && c.IsCreature() {
			toSac = append(toSac, c)
		}
	}
	for _, c := range toSac {
		gs.sacrificeCard(c, oppIdx)
	}
	return nil
}

// executeCombatDamageEachOpponentCreature — Balefire Dragon-style: deal N damage
// to each creature the opponent (1-controller) controls.
func (gs *GameState) executeCombatDamageEachOpponentCreature(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	amount := getIntValue(effectValue, "amount", 0)
	if amount <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	oppIdx := 1 - controller
	if oppIdx < 0 || oppIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	// Snapshot targets first to avoid mutation during iteration.
	var targets []*Card
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.Cards {
		if c.Controller == oppIdx && c.IsCreature() {
			targets = append(targets, c)
		}
	}
	for _, c := range targets {
		// Card-level damage redirect ([VOCAB] #54): no-op absent a
		// registered redirect effect on c.
		recipient := c
		if gs.EffectManager != nil {
			recipient = gs.EffectManager.RedirectDamageTarget(c)
		}
		recipient.DealDamage(amount)
		if gs.EventBus != nil {
			gs.ProcessTriggeredAbilities(CreateDamageEvent(source, recipient, amount, controller))
		}
	}
	gs.CheckStateBasedActions()
	return nil
}

// executeAnnihilatorSacrifice — Rule 702.86a: defending player sacrifices N
// permanents of their choice. If they control fewer than N, they sacrifice all.
func (gs *GameState) executeAnnihilatorSacrifice(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	n := getIntValue(effectValue, "n", 0)
	if n <= 0 {
		return nil
	}
	oppIdx := 1 - controller
	if oppIdx < 0 || oppIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}

	// Collect all permanents the defending player controls.
	var candidates []string
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.Cards {
		if c.Controller == oppIdx {
			candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
		}
	}
	if len(candidates) == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	// If the player controls fewer permanents than N, sacrifice all automatically.
	count := n
	if len(candidates) <= count {
		for _, id := range candidates {
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(id)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			gs.sacrificeCard(card, oppIdx)
		}
		return nil
	}

	// Otherwise prompt the defending player to choose which N to sacrifice.
	gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
		PlayerIdx:  oppIdx,
		ChoiceType: "sacrifice",
		Candidates: candidates,
		Count:      count,
		SourceName: "Annihilator",
	}
	return nil
}

// executeSacrificeChoiceEffect — "[player] sacrifices a permanent of their
// choice" (Spelltithe Enforcer's unless-pay consequence, [VOCAB]
// generic-cost-payment-choice). Unlike executeAnnihilatorSacrifice (always
// the ATTACKER's opponent), the affected player here is
// stackObj.UnlessPayPlayerIdx when the triggering ability set an override
// (an event-triggered "unless pay" whose payer isn't the source's
// controller), falling back to stackObj.Controller otherwise. Mirrors
// executeAnnihilatorSacrifice's choice-vs-auto-sacrifice shape: with count
// or fewer matching permanents, they're all sacrificed automatically.
func (gs *GameState) executeSacrificeChoiceEffect(stackObj *StackObject) error {
	playerIdx := stackObj.Controller
	if stackObj.UnlessPayPlayerIdx != nil {
		playerIdx = *stackObj.UnlessPayPlayerIdx
	}
	if playerIdx < 0 || playerIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	count := getIntValue(stackObj.EffectValue, "n", 1)
	if count <= 0 {
		return nil
	}

	var candidates []string
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.Cards {
		if c.Controller == playerIdx {
			candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
		}
	}
	if len(candidates) == 0 {
		return nil
	}

	if len(candidates) <= count {
		for _, id := range candidates {
			card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(id)
			if err != nil {
				continue
			}
			gs.sacrificeCard(card, playerIdx)
		}
		return nil
	}

	sourceName := ""
	if stackObj.Card != nil {
		sourceName = stackObj.Card.Name
	}
	gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
		PlayerIdx:  playerIdx,
		ChoiceType: "sacrifice",
		Candidates: candidates,
		Count:      count,
		SourceName: sourceName,
	}
	return nil
}

// executeSacrificeUnlessSacrifice implements "sacrifice [source] unless you
// sacrifice a [filter] permanent" (ticket
// engine.auto-sacrifice-unless-sacrifice-d9e71faebd, Scythe Tiger class): the
// controller either gives up a filter-matching permanent they control and
// keeps the source, or the source itself is sacrificed. Modeled as a single
// forced choice of exactly 1 permanent from {source} ∪ filter-matches,
// reusing the existing PendingChoice ChoiceType "sacrifice" — its general
// N>=1 case in ResolvePendingChoice already sacrifices whichever IDs the
// controller picks, so no new choice type or resolution wiring is needed.
func (gs *GameState) executeSacrificeUnlessSacrifice(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) || source == nil {
		return nil
	}
	filter, _ := effectValue["unless_filter"].(string)

	candidates := []string{source.ID}
	for _, c := range gs.Battlefield.Cards {
		if c.ID == source.ID {
			continue
		}
		if c.Controller == controller && matchesPermanentFilter(c, filter) {
			candidates = append(candidates, c.ID)
		}
	}

	if len(candidates) <= 1 {
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(source.ID)
		if err != nil {
			return nil
		}
		gs.sacrificeCard(card, controller)
		return nil
	}

	gs.PendingChoice = &PendingChoice{
		PlayerIdx:  controller,
		ChoiceType: "sacrifice",
		Candidates: candidates,
		Count:      1,
		SourceName: source.Name,
	}
	return nil
}

// executeSacrificeUnlessReturnBasicLand implements the "sacrifice ~ unless
// you return a basic land card from your graveyard to your hand" pattern
// (Harvest Wurm class): the controller keeps the source permanent by
// returning a basic land from their graveyard; if none is available, the
// source is sacrificed instead. Auto-selects the first matching basic land,
// mirroring executeReturnFromGraveyard's auto-select-first-match convention.
func (gs *GameState) executeSacrificeUnlessReturnBasicLand(controller int, source *Card) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) || source == nil {
		return nil
	}
	player := gs.Players[controller]
	for _, c := range player.Graveyard.Cards {
		if c.IsBasic && c.Type == TypeLand {
			if returned, err := player.Graveyard.RemoveCard(c.ID); err == nil {
				player.Hand.AddCard(returned)
				return nil
			}
		}
	}
	gs.sacrificeCard(source, controller)
	return nil
}
