// executeDiscardOpponentHand — Emrakul-style forced discard: the opponent
// (1-controller in a two-player game) discards their entire hand.
func (gs *GameState) executeDiscardOpponentHand(controller int) error {
	oppIdx := 1 - controller
	if oppIdx < 0 || oppIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	opp := gs.Players[oppIdx]
	// Snapshot IDs; Discard mutates the hand.
	cardIDs := make([]string, 0, opp.Hand.Count())
	for _, c := range opp.Hand.GetAll() {
		cardIDs = append(cardIDs, c.ID)
	}
	for _, id := range cardIDs {
		_ = gs.Discard(oppIdx, id)
	}
	return nil
}

// executeDiscardEachOpponentCard — Each opponent discards one card.
// Works for any number of opponents (multiplayer).
func (gs *GameState) executeDiscardEachOpponentCard(controller int) error {
	for i := range gs.Players {
		if i == controller {
			continue
		}
		opp := gs.Players[i]
		cards := opp.Hand.GetAll()
		if len(cards) == 0 {
			continue
		}
		// Auto-pick the first card (UI can enhance this later)
		_ = gs.Discard(i, cards[0].ID)
	}
	return nil
}

// ============================================================================
// HELPER FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================================

// getIntValue extracts an int from effect value map
func getIntValue(m map[string]interface{}, key string, defaultVal int) int {
	if m == nil {
		return defaultVal
	}
	if v, ok := m[key]; ok {
		switch val := v.(type) {
		case int:
			return val
		case float64:
			return int(val)
		case int64:
			return int(val)
		}
	}
	return defaultVal
}

// getBoolValue extracts a bool from an effect value map
func getBoolValue(m map[string]interface{}, key string, defaultVal bool) bool {
	if m == nil {
		return defaultVal
	}
	if v, ok := m[key]; ok {
		if b, ok := v.(bool); ok {
			return b
		}
	}
	return defaultVal
}

// getStringSliceValue extracts a []string from an effect value map, handling
// both native []string (from DSL construction) and []interface{} (from JSON
// round-trips where all arrays decode as []interface{}).
func getStringSliceValue(m map[string]interface{}, key string) []string {
	if m == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v, ok := m[key]
	if !ok {
		return nil
	}
	switch val := v.(type) {
	case []string:
		return val
	case []interface{}:
		result := make([]string, 0, len(val))
		for _, item := range val {
			if s, ok := item.(string); ok {
				result = append(result, s)
			}
		}
		return result
	}
	return nil
}

// getStringValue extracts a string from effect value map
func getStringValue(m map[string]interface{}, key string, defaultVal string) string {
	if m == nil {
		return defaultVal
	}
	if v, ok := m[key]; ok {
		if s, ok := v.(string); ok {
			return s
		}
	}
	return defaultVal
}

// ============================================================================
// removeKeyword returns a new slice with the first occurrence of kw removed.
func removeKeyword(keywords []Keyword, kw Keyword) []Keyword {
	for i, k := range keywords {
		if k == kw {
			return append(keywords[:i], keywords[i+1:]...)
		}
	}
	return keywords
}

// END OF TURN CLEANUP
// ============================================================================

// CleanupEndOfTurnEffects removes effects that last "until end of turn"
func (gs *GameState) CleanupEndOfTurnEffects() {
	// Reset AnimatedAsCreature on any cards whose creature-type grant is expiring.
	// Also reset CannotBlock on any cards that had blocking restrictions.
	if gs.EffectManager != nil {
		for _, effect := range gs.EffectManager.GetAllEffects() {
			if effect.UntilEOT && effect.AffectsSpecific != "" {
				if card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(effect.AffectsSpecific); err == nil {
					if effect.GrantsCreatureType {
						card.AnimatedAsCreature = false
					}
					if effect.CannotBlock {
						card.CannotBlock = false
					}
					// task-2821: strip Defender granted by lock_creature
					// (executeLockCreatureEffect appends it directly to
					// card.Keywords so CanAttack sees it without the
					// EffectManager; remove it here on cleanup).
					if effect.GrantKeyword == Defender {
						card.Keywords = removeKeyword(card.Keywords, Defender)
					}
					// Valued keyword grants (Toxic N until end of turn, Rule
					// 702.164b compounding — ticket engine.auto-grant-valued-
					// keyword-until-eot-1312f3366b) mutate Card.ToxicN
					// directly at grant time (combat.go reads that plain
					// field unconditionally, not through the EffectManager),
					// so cleanup must subtract the same delta back off
					// rather than relying on RemoveUntilEOT alone.
					if effect.GrantKeywordValue != 0 && effect.GrantKeyword == Toxic {
						card.ToxicN -= effect.GrantKeywordValue
					}
					if effect.CannotBeBlocked {
						card.CannotBeBlocked = false
					}
					if effect.CantBeBlockedExceptBySubtype != "" {
						card.CantBeBlockedExceptBySubtype = ""
					}
					if len(effect.SetSubTypes) > 0 {
						card.SubTypes = effect.PreviousSubTypes
					}
					if len(effect.SetColors) > 0 {
						card.Colors = effect.PreviousColors
					}
					if effect.SetLandType != "" {
						card.LandType = effect.PreviousLandType
						if effect.GrantsLandType {
							card.AnimatedAsLand = false
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		gs.EffectManager.RemoveUntilEOT()
	}
	// Also clean up replacement effects (regeneration shields, damage prevention)
	if gs.ReplacementManager != nil {
		gs.ReplacementManager.RemoveUntilEOT()
		gs.ReplacementManager.RemoveUsedOneShotEffects()
	}
	// Tear down "until end of turn" triggered abilities installed by spells
	// resolved this turn (e.g. Glimpse of Nature).
	gs.cleanupEOTTriggers()
	// Strip v2 grant_ability activated abilities (re-parse R0d).
	for _, card := range gs.Battlefield.GetAll() {
		kept := card.ActivatedAbilities[:0]
		for _, ab := range card.ActivatedAbilities {
			if !ab.GrantedUntilEOT {
				kept = append(kept, ab)
			}
		}
		card.ActivatedAbilities = kept
	}
	// Rule 514.2 - EOT color-filtered spell cost reductions ([VOCAB] #1222)
	// expire unconditionally during cleanup, regardless of whether the
	// granting permanent is still on the battlefield.
	gs.EOTColorSpellCostReductions = nil
	// Rule 514.2 - EOT zone-filtered "cast from exile" cost reductions
	// ([VOCAB] #1555) expire unconditionally during cleanup, regardless of
	// whether the granting permanent is still on the battlefield.
	gs.EOTExileCastCostReductions = nil
	// Rule 514.2 - EOT controller-filtered damage multipliers ([VOCAB] #59)
	// expire unconditionally during cleanup, regardless of whether the
	// granting permanent (typically a resolved sorcery) is still on the
	// battlefield.
	gs.EOTDamageMultipliers = nil
	// Rule 514.2 - one-shot "your opponents can't cast spells this turn"
	// prohibitions ([VOCAB] #1258) expire unconditionally during cleanup,
	// regardless of whether any granting permanent/spell is still around —
	// there never was one enforcing it on an ongoing basis to begin with.
	gs.ForbiddenCastersThisTurn = nil
	// Rule 514.2 - one-shot "that player can't activate abilities this turn"
	// prohibitions (Sen Triplets class) expire unconditionally during
	// cleanup, same shape as ForbiddenCastersThisTurn immediately above.
	gs.ForbiddenAbilityActivatorsThisTurn = nil
	// Rule 514.2 - one-shot "plays with their hand revealed" this turn
	// (Sen Triplets class) expires unconditionally during cleanup.
	gs.HandRevealedThisTurn = nil
	// Rule 514.2 - EOT-granted "whenever this creature attacks, create a
	// token tapped and attacking" grants ([VOCAB] #1193) expire
	// unconditionally during cleanup, regardless of whether the granting
	// source (typically a resolved sorcery, long gone) is still around.
	gs.EOTGrantedAttackTokenTriggers = nil
	// Rule 514.2 - a turn-scoped "you choose how each player votes" override
	// ([VOCAB] #1269) expires unconditionally during cleanup, regardless of
	// whether the granting source is still around.
	gs.VoteOverrideController = nil
}

// AddUntilEOT field to ContinuousEffect if not already present
// This is handled in the effects.go file - we just need to update the cleanup phase
func init() {
	// Ensure the cleanup method exists
	_ = fmt.Sprintf("ability_effects initialized")
}

// executeReturnGraveyardToHand — "return target <filter> card from your
// graveyard to your hand." Moves the targeted graveyard card to the
// controller's hand. If explicit targets are provided those are used;
// otherwise the first matching card in the graveyard is chosen automatically.
// executeReturnFromGraveyard is the generalized graveyard-return executor
// (schema-v2 3c round 7). Record shapes (103 in carddb): value.card_type
// ("creature card"/"card"/...) as filter, value.to or value.destination
// ("hand" default | "battlefield" | "library_top" | "library_bottom"),
// value.self==true or target=="self" (return the SOURCE itself, Sanitarium
// Skeleton), value.tapped for enters-tapped battlefield returns. Explicit
// targets win over auto-select. library_top/library_bottom added by the
// put_zone re-parse family (task 1875): "put target X card from your
// graveyard on top of/on the bottom of your library" (Reclaim, Arcbound
// Reclaimer, ...) — reuses the same auto-select/explicit-target machinery,
// just a different landing zone via Zone.PutOnTop/PutOnBottom.
//
// value.source ("graveyard" default | "hand") extends the SAME auto-select
// convention to "put [a/an] <filter> card from your hand onto the
// battlefield" (Elvish Piper, Quicksilver Amulet, Sneak Attack, Burgeoning,
// ...) — the put_battlefield re-parse family (task 2377). Pulled from the
// controller's HAND instead of graveyard; deliver/destination are unchanged
// (the battlefield path, summoning-sickness, tapped, and ETB triggers are
// identical regardless of source zone). Hand-source records are never
// self-returns and never targeted across players, so only the controller's
// own hand is consulted.
// executeBounceExiledCard implements the bounce_target_exiled_card primitive
// (ticket engine.auto-bounce-target-exiled-card-c42e060c66, Runic Repetition
// class): "Return target exiled card with flashback you own to your hand."
// Unlike the plain "bounce" case (a battlefield permanent, executeBounceEffectTo)
// or executeReturnFromGraveyard's graveyard/hand/exile sources (governed by a
// free-form card_type filter across any owner's zone via explicit target),
// this is scoped to the flashback-keyword filter AND the caster's OWN exile
// zone only — Exile is a per-player zone (Rule 400.4), so scanning only
// controller's own Exile.GetAll() enforces the "you own" restriction without
// a separate ownership check. Auto-selects the first matching card, mirroring
// the auto-select convention converter.go documents for zone-aware
// graveyard/exile targets (real interactive targeting into a non-battlefield
// zone doesn't exist yet). No-op if controller is invalid or no exiled card
// the controller owns satisfies filter.
func (gs *GameState) executeBounceExiledCard(controller int, filter string) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	player := gs.Players[controller]
	for _, c := range player.Exile.GetAll() {
		if c == nil {
			continue
		}
		if filter == "flashback" && c.FlashbackCost == nil {
			continue
		}
		if _, err := player.Exile.RemoveCard(c.ID); err == nil {
			player.Hand.AddCard(c)
		}
		return nil
	}
	return nil
}

func (gs *GameState) executeReturnFromGraveyard(controller int, source *Card, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	player := gs.Players[controller]

	dest := getStringValue(effectValue, "to", "")
	if dest == "" {
		dest = getStringValue(effectValue, "destination", "hand")
	}
	tapped := false
	if b, _ := effectValue["tapped"].(bool); b {
		tapped = true
	}
	// engine.auto-reanimate-with-finality-counter-055a364b1d: value.finality_counter
	// (Balustrade Wurm/Relentless X-ATM092/Grim Reaper's Scythe class) was
	// already parsed by cards/patterns_complex.go but never consumed — wire it
	// to actually stamp CounterFinality (Rule 122.1b marker counter: "If a
	// permanent with a finality counter on it would die, exile it instead") on
	// battlefield delivery below.
	finalityCounter := false
	if b, _ := effectValue["finality_counter"].(bool); b {
		finalityCounter = true
	}
	// auto_return_from_graveyard_attacking_42d259e0f5: "return this card
	// from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped and attacking"
	// (Interceptor, Shadow's Hound class) — the returned permanent joins
	// combat as an attacker in the resolution's combat phase, mirroring
	// ActivateNinjutsu's Rule 702.49c bookkeeping (Attacking flag plus a
	// CombatState.Attackers entry). Battlefield-only; every other
	// destination ignores the flag.
	attacking := false
	if b, _ := effectValue["attacking"].(bool); b {
		attacking = true
	}
	// auto_return_from_graveyard_tapped_attacking_with_counter (ticket
	// engine.auto-return-from-graveyard-tapped-attacking-with-coun-e553475ba6,
	// Phoenix Chick class): "return this card from your graveyard to the
	// battlefield tapped and attacking with a +1/+1 counter on it" — reuses
	// the SAME tapped/attacking battlefield-delivery path above, plus a
	// counter placed via AddCounters (counter_placement.go), mirroring
	// executeReturnFromGraveyardWithCounter's counter_type/counter_count
	// reading so Rule 614 counter-placement replacement effects still
	// apply. A missing or non-positive counter_count is "no counter"
	// (negative example: a plain tapped return with no attacking status
	// and no counter must stay unaffected).
	counterType, _ := effectValue["counter_type"].(string)
	counterCount := getIntValue(effectValue, "counter_count", 0)
	// ticket-2827 ("return it to" family, dies-trigger sub-shape): "return
	// it to the battlefield ... under ITS OWNER'S control" (Fungal
	// Fortitude, Luminous Broodmoth, Ojer Taq, Yedora) is a DIFFERENT
	// controller than the default "under your/their control" (Changing
	// Loyalty) — the ability's controller and the returning card's owner
	// are usually the same player but aren't required to be (a
	// control-changed or opponent-owned creature dying under this
	// controller's Aura, for instance). Default false preserves the
	// existing "under controller's control" behavior every prior emitter
	// of this destination relied on.
	ownerControl := false
	if b, _ := effectValue["owner_control"].(bool); b {
		ownerControl = true
	}
	// transformed (ticket-2827, Ojer Taq: "return it to the battlefield
	// tapped and transformed under its owner's control") — the returning
	// object enters the battlefield ALREADY on its back face (Rule 712.8-
	// style substitute-enter), not front-face-then-transform, so the face
	// swap must happen via the same snapshotFace/applyFace primitives
	// exile_self_return_transformed already uses, BEFORE the card is
	// re-registered on the battlefield (not via TransformCard, which
	// assumes the card is already live-registered).
	transformed := false
	if b, _ := effectValue["transformed"].(bool); b {
		transformed = true
	}
	src := getStringValue(effectValue, "source", "graveyard")
	// auto_damage_amount_from_prior_effect_card_mana_value_e74cf97c36 primitive
	// (ticket engine.auto-damage-amount-from-prior-effect-card-mana-value-e74cf97c36,
	// Pyretic Rebirth class): "~ deals damage equal to that card's mana
	// value" needs a LATER atom in the same sequence to read the mana value
	// of the card THIS atom just returned. mv_slot mirrors manifest_dread's
	// seqSlotsStr producer / amount_ref consumer pattern (effect_sequence.go)
	// but for a numeric stat instead of a card ID — the existing amount_ref
	// fold already reads gs.seqSlots[slot] into a later atom's "amount", so
	// no new consumer code is needed here.
	mvSlot := getStringValue(effectValue, "mv_slot", "")
	deliver := func(c *Card) {
		if mvSlot != "" {
			if gs.seqSlots == nil {
				gs.seqSlots = map[string]int{}
			}
			gs.seqSlots[mvSlot] = c.ManaCost.CMC()
		}
		switch dest {
		case "exile":
			// task-2464 (verb_unmapped:exile): "exile target/a <filter> card
			// from a/your/an opponent's/... graveyard" (Cemetery Reaper,
			// Klothys, God of Destiny, Wizard's Spellbook, ...) — reuses the
			// SAME auto-select-first-match convention this executor already
			// ships for the reanimate/put_battlefield ("...onto the
			// battlefield") and put_zone ("...onto the library") families,
			// just landing in exile instead. Exiled cards go to their OWNER's
			// exile zone (mirrors executeExileEffect), not the ability
			// controller's — matters for the opponent_graveyard source case.
			owner := c.Owner
			if owner < 0 || owner >= len(gs.Players) {
				owner = controller
			}
			gs.Players[owner].Exile.AddCard(c)
		case "battlefield":
			if ownerControl && c.Owner >= 0 && c.Owner < len(gs.Players) {
				c.Controller = c.Owner
			} else {
				c.Controller = controller
			}
			c.Tapped = tapped
			if attacking && c.IsCreature() {
				c.Attacking = true
				if gs.CombatState != nil {
					gs.CombatState.Attackers = append(gs.CombatState.Attackers, c)
				}
			}
			if counterCount > 0 && counterType != "" {
				gs.AddCounters(c, CounterType(counterType), counterCount)
			}
			if transformed && c.BackFace != nil {
				incomingIsPlaneswalker := c.BackFace.Type == TypePlaneswalker
				incomingStartingLoyalty := c.BackFace.StartingLoyalty
				pending := snapshotFace(c)
				applyFace(c, c.BackFace)
				c.BackFace = pending
				c.FaceUp = otherFace(c.FaceUp)
				if c.FaceUp == "" {
					c.FaceUp = "back"
				}
				if incomingIsPlaneswalker && incomingStartingLoyalty > 0 {
					c.Counters.Loyalty = incomingStartingLoyalty
				}
			}
			if c.IsCreature() {
				c.SummoningSick = true
			}
			if finalityCounter {
				c.Counters.AddCounter(CounterFinality, 1)
			}
			gs.Battlefield.AddCard(c)
			gs.RegisterCardAbilities(c)
			if gs.EventBus != nil {
				triggered := gs.EventBus.Publish(CreateETBEvent(c, controller))
				for _, a := range triggered {
					gs.PutTriggeredAbilityOnStack(a)
				}
			}
		case "library_top":
			player.Library.PutOnTop(c) //nolint:errcheck
		case "library_bottom":
			player.Library.PutOnBottom(c) //nolint:errcheck
		default:
			player.Hand.AddCard(c)
		}
	}

	// bounce_from_graveyard_or_exile_to_hand (ticket
	// engine.auto-bounce-from-graveyard-or-exile-to-hand-d8f4269355,
	// Sorceress's Schemes class): "Return target instant or sorcery card
	// from your graveyard or exiled card with flashback you own to your
	// hand." Unlike every fixed-src value above, the target can be in
	// EITHER zone, so each target is resolved by ID against the
	// controller's graveyard first and their exile zone second, then
	// delivered via the same deliver closure (default destination hand)
	// every other branch in this executor already uses. A target found
	// in neither zone is simply skipped.
	if b, _ := effectValue["graveyard_or_exile_flashback"].(bool); b {
		for _, target := range targets {
			if !target.IsCard || target.CardID == "" {
				continue
			}
			if c, err := player.Graveyard.RemoveCard(target.CardID); err == nil {
				deliver(c)
				continue
			}
			if c, err := player.Exile.RemoveCard(target.CardID); err == nil {
				deliver(c)
			}
		}
		return nil
	}

	// Self-return: "Return ~ from your graveyard to your hand" — the source
	// card itself is in the graveyard. ticket-2827 ("return it to" family,
	// blink sub-shape): src=="exile" is the SAME self-return idiom off a
	// same-ability "Exile ~, then return it..." pair (Aethergeode Miner —
	// untransformed, unlike exile_self_return_transformed's DFC-only path)
	// — the source card is in ITS OWN exile zone (Exile is always owner-
	// scoped, matching the graveyard branch's player.Graveyard convention).
	selfReturn := false
	if b, _ := effectValue["self"].(bool); b {
		selfReturn = true
	}
	if getStringValue(effectValue, "target", "") == "self" {
		selfReturn = true
	}
	if selfReturn && source != nil {
		if src == "hand" {
			if c, err := player.Hand.RemoveCard(source.ID); err == nil {
				deliver(c)
			}
		} else if src == "exile" {
			if c, err := player.Exile.RemoveCard(source.ID); err == nil {
				deliver(c)
			}
		} else if c, err := player.Graveyard.RemoveCard(source.ID); err == nil {
			deliver(c)
		}
		// auto_return_from_graveyard_self_and_target_3b115e38a3 (Sandman,
		// Shifting Scoundrel class): "Return this card and target <type>
		// card from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped." A
		// self-return ability can ALSO carry an explicit target (a
		// different card from the SAME graveyard) — deliver it through
		// the identical deliver()/dest/tapped path used above instead of
		// dropping it, so both returns land as one atomic resolution.
		// Self-only abilities never populate targets, so this is a
		// strict addition with no effect on that existing negative
		// example.
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" || t.CardID == source.ID {
				continue
			}
			if c, err := player.Graveyard.RemoveCard(t.CardID); err == nil {
				deliver(c)
			}
		}
		return nil
	}

	// ticket-2827 ("return it to" family, blink sub-shape): target_id is a
	// target_scratch_ref already folded to a concrete CardID by
	// executeSequenceAtomEv (game/effect_sequence.go) — "exile TARGET
	// creature, then return it to the battlefield..." (Distinguished
	// Conjurer class) where the SAME freshly-chosen target must be
	// referenced by a LATER atom in the sequence with no other binding
	// mechanism (mirrors executePutCounterEffect's identical target_id
	// check). Checked before the plain explicit-target branch below since
	// a scratch-bound atom carries no Targets of its own.
	if tid := getStringValue(effectValue, "target_id", ""); tid != "" {
		for _, p := range gs.Players {
			zone := p.Graveyard
			if src == "exile" {
				zone = p.Exile
			}
			if c, err := zone.RemoveCard(tid); err == nil {
				deliver(c)
				return nil
			}
		}
	}

	// return_from_graveyard_total_mana_value_cap primitive (ticket
	// engine.auto-return-from-graveyard-total-mana-value-cap-f3dac44f3a,
	// Patch Up class): "Return up to three target creature cards with
	// total mana value 3 or less from your graveyard to the battlefield."
	// Unlike the single explicit-target branch below (which only ever
	// takes targets[0]), a max_total_mana_value cap implies MULTIPLE
	// simultaneously-chosen targets whose SUMMED mana value must not
	// exceed the cap — a cross-card constraint distinct from the existing
	// per-card "mana_value"/property filter elsewhere in this executor.
	// Cards are accepted in target order up to the cap; any card that
	// would push the running total over the cap is skipped and left in
	// its original zone, mirroring executeReturnCreatureAndLandFromGraveyard's
	// per-target skip-don't-fail convention (Rule 601.2c). Absent this
	// key, behavior is completely unchanged (negative example:
	// max_total_mana_value absent).
	if maxTotal := getIntValue(effectValue, "max_total_mana_value", -1); maxTotal >= 0 && len(targets) > 0 {
		running := 0
		for _, t := range targets {
			if !t.IsCard || t.CardID == "" {
				continue
			}
			var found *Card
			var foundZone *Zone
			if src == "hand" {
				if c, err := player.Hand.RemoveCard(t.CardID); err == nil {
					found, foundZone = c, player.Hand
				}
			} else if src == "exile" {
				for _, p := range gs.Players {
					if c, err := p.Exile.RemoveCard(t.CardID); err == nil {
						found, foundZone = c, p.Exile
						break
					}
				}
			} else {
				for _, p := range gs.Players {
					if c, err := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(t.CardID); err == nil {
						found, foundZone = c, p.Graveyard
						break
					}
				}
			}
			if found == nil {
				continue
			}
			if running+found.ManaCost.CMC() > maxTotal {
				foundZone.AddCard(found)
				continue
			}
			running += found.ManaCost.CMC()
			deliver(found)
		}
		return nil
	}

	// Explicit target wins.
	if len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard && targets[0].CardID != "" {
		if src == "hand" {
			// Hand-source puts are always the controller's own card; no
			// cross-player hand search (unlike graveyard owner lookup).
			if c, err := player.Hand.RemoveCard(targets[0].CardID); err == nil {
				deliver(c)
			}
			return nil
		}
		if src == "exile" {
			for _, p := range gs.Players { // owner's exile may not be the controller's
				if c, err := p.Exile.RemoveCard(targets[0].CardID); err == nil {
					deliver(c)
					return nil
				}
			}
			return nil
		}
		for _, p := range gs.Players { // owner's graveyard may not be the controller's
			if c, err := p.Graveyard.RemoveCard(targets[0].CardID); err == nil {
				deliver(c)
				return nil
			}
		}
	}

	// Auto-select first match by card_type filter (strip trailing " card").
	filter := getStringValue(effectValue, "card_type", "")
	filter = strings.TrimSuffix(strings.TrimSpace(filter), " cards")
	filter = strings.TrimSuffix(filter, " card")
	sourcePlayer := controller
	if src == "graveyard" && len(targets) > 0 && !targets[0].IsCard &&
		targets[0].PlayerIdx >= 0 && targets[0].PlayerIdx < len(gs.Players) {
		sourcePlayer = targets[0].PlayerIdx
	}

	zone := player.Graveyard
	if sourcePlayer != controller {
		zone = gs.Players[sourcePlayer].Graveyard
	}
	if src == "hand" {
		zone = player.Hand
	} else if src == "exile" {
		// ticket-2887 (verb_unmapped:put_battlefield, primitive
		// put_from_exile): "put a <filter> card exiled with ~ onto the
		// battlefield under your control" (Yggdrasil, Rebirth Engine
		// class) — the SAME auto-select-first-match convention this
		// executor already ships for the graveyard/hand sources, pulled
		// from the controller's OWN exile zone. Exile is owner-scoped
		// (mirrors the self-return and explicit-target exile branches
		// above), so the common "exiled with ~" shape consults the
		// controller's exile; cross-owner exile selection already routes
		// through the explicit-target branch above. Without this the
		// auto-select path fell through to the graveyard and no-op'd.
		zone = player.Exile
	} else if src == "opponent_graveyard" {
		// task-2433 (verb_unmapped:put_battlefield): "put target creature
		// card from [an opponent's/that player's/defending player's]
		// graveyard onto the battlefield under your control" (Ink-Eyes,
		// Puppeteer Clique, ...) — 1v1-only opponent lookup, same
		// oppIdx := 1 - controller convention as executeDiscardOpponentHand.
		oppIdx := 1 - controller
		if oppIdx < 0 || oppIdx >= len(gs.Players) {
			return nil
		}
		zone = gs.Players[oppIdx].Graveyard
	}
	// Mass "all"/"each" mode (task-2430): "return all/each <filter> cards
	// from your graveyard to the battlefield" (Splendid Reclamation,
	// Replenish, Storm of Souls, Knights' Charge, ...) delivers EVERY
	// matching card instead of auto-selecting the first. Snapshot the
	// matches before delivering any of them — delivering onto the
	// battlefield can trigger ETB abilities that mutate the graveyard
	// mid-loop, which would otherwise skew a live GetAll() scan.
	if getStringValue(effectValue, "target", "") == "all" {
		var matched []*Card
		for _, c := range zone.GetAll() {
			if filter == "" || filter == "card" || matchesTutorFilter(c, filter) {
				matched = append(matched, c)
			}
		}
		for _, c := range matched {
			zone.RemoveCard(c.ID) //nolint:errcheck
			deliver(c)
		}
		return nil
	}
	// task-2451 (verb_unmapped:regrow): "return a/an <filter> card at random
	// from your graveyard to your hand" (Charmbreaker Devils, Haunted
	// Fengraf, ...) — same random-pick convention as executeDiscardEffect's
	// "random" bool (line ~2960), applied to the single-card auto-select
	// path instead of always taking the first match in zone order.
	if getBoolValue(effectValue, "random", false) {
		var matched []*Card
		for _, c := range zone.GetAll() {
			if filter == "" || filter == "card" || matchesTutorFilter(c, filter) {
				matched = append(matched, c)
			}
		}
		if len(matched) == 0 {
			return nil
		}
		// return_from_graveyard_random_amount primitive (ticket
		// engine.auto-return-from-graveyard-random-amount-b589e1d5d6, Moldgraf
		// Monstrosity class): "return two creature cards at random from your
		// graveyard to the battlefield" — a FIXED count (not "up to") chosen
		// RANDOMLY, unlike the "count" branch below which takes the first N in
		// zone order. count<=1 (the default) preserves the pre-existing
		// single-random-pick behavior exactly.
		count := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 1)
		if count < 1 {
			count = 1
		}
		if count > len(matched) {
			count = len(matched)
		}
		rand.Shuffle(len(matched), func(i, j int) { matched[i], matched[j] = matched[j], matched[i] })
		for _, c := range matched[:count] {
			zone.RemoveCard(c.ID) //nolint:errcheck
			deliver(c)
		}
		return nil
	}
	// return_from_graveyard_bounded_multi_count primitive (ticket #3570,
	// REPARSE-MAP verb_unmapped:regrow / "return up to N <filter> cards from
	// your graveyard to your hand" family, N>1 — Mythos of Brokkos class:
	// "Return up to two permanent cards from your graveyard to your hand").
	// Mirrors the "all" branch above but bounded by a count instead of
	// unbounded, and the "random"/single-match branches which only ever
	// take one card. Auto-selects the first N matches in zone order — same
	// no-player-choice convention this executor already uses for "all" and
	// "random" (true player-choice-of-which-N is a separate, unbuilt
	// capability). count<=0 falls through to the single-card auto-select
	// path below unchanged.
	if count := getIntValue(effectValue, "count", 0); count > 0 {
		var matched []*Card
		for _, c := range zone.GetAll() {
			if filter == "" || filter == "card" || matchesTutorFilter(c, filter) {
				matched = append(matched, c)
				if len(matched) >= count {
					break
				}
			}
		}
		for _, c := range matched {
			zone.RemoveCard(c.ID) //nolint:errcheck
			deliver(c)
		}
		return nil
	}
	// put_battlefield_property_filter_match (ticket #3220, verb_unmapped:
	// put_battlefield): "put a creature card with mana value equal to the
	// number of charge counters on this artifact from your hand onto the
	// battlefield" (Aether Vial class). matchesTutorFilter's comparator
	// suffix (tutorFilterCmpRe) only carries a LITERAL mana value (\d+),
	// so a DYNAMIC property target — resolved from amount_count (e.g.
	// counters_on_source/charge) into effectValue["amount"] by
	// resolveDynamicAmountFull before the effect switch — is matched here
	// via the optional "property" key instead. Absent for every prior
	// return_from_graveyard emitter, so this is purely additive.
	propertyMatch := func(c *Card) bool {
		prop, _ := effectValue["property"].(string)
		if prop == "" {
			return true
		}
		n := 0
		switch v := effectValue["amount"].(type) {
		case int:
			n = v
		case int64:
			n = int(v)
		case float64:
			n = int(v)
		default:
			return false
		}
		if prop == "mana_value" {
			return c.ManaCost.CMC() == n
		}
		return false
	}
	for _, c := range zone.GetAll() {
		if (filter == "" || filter == "card" || matchesTutorFilter(c, filter)) && propertyMatch(c) {
			zone.RemoveCard(c.ID) //nolint:errcheck
			deliver(c)
			return nil
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (gs *GameState) executeReturnGraveyardToHand(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	player := gs.Players[controller]

	// Prefer explicit target if provided.
	if len(targets) > 0 && targets[0].IsCard && targets[0].CardID != "" {
		if c, err := player.Graveyard.RemoveCard(targets[0].CardID); err == nil {
			player.Hand.AddCard(c)
			return nil
		}
	}

	// Auto-select: find the first matching card in the graveyard.
	filter := ""
	if effectValue != nil {
		if f, ok := effectValue["filter"].(string); ok {
			filter = f
		}
	}
	for _, c := range player.Graveyard.GetAll() {
		if filter == "" || matchesTutorFilter(c, filter) {
			player.Graveyard.RemoveCard(c.ID) //nolint:errcheck
			player.Hand.AddCard(c)
			return nil
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// executeControlEnchantedEffect handles "You control enchanted permanent" for Auras
// This changes the controller of the enchanted permanent to the Aura's controller
// TODO: Implement controller change when attachments are properly tracked
func (gs *GameState) executeControlEnchantedEffect(source *Card, controller int) error {
	if source == nil {
		return nil
	}

	// Find the permanent that this Aura is attached to
	// The source card (Aura) should have an attachment pointing to the enchanted permanent
	if source.AttachedTo == "" {
		return nil
	}

	card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(source.AttachedTo)
	if err != nil {
		return nil
	}

	// Change the controller of the enchanted permanent to the Aura's controller
	card.Controller = controller

	return nil
}

// executeEnchantedDoesntUntapEffect handles "Enchanted permanent doesn't untap during its controller's untap step"
// This prevents the enchanted permanent from untapping during the untap step
// TODO: Implement untap prevention once the untap step is implemented
func (gs *GameState) executeEnchantedDoesntUntapEffect(source *Card) error {
	if source == nil {
		return nil
	}

	// Find the permanent that this Aura is attached to
	if source.AttachedTo == "" {
		return nil
	}

	card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(source.AttachedTo)
	if err != nil {
		return nil
	}

	// Mark the card as unable to untap
	// For now, this is a placeholder - proper implementation would register
	// a continuous effect that prevents untapping
	_ = card // Use card to avoid unused variable error

	return nil
}

// executeBounceUpToNOpponentCreatures implements the
// bounce_up_to_n_opponent_creatures primitive (ticket
// engine.auto-bounce-up-to-n-opponent-creatures-14cd3cc1c0, Roiling Waters
// class: "Return up to two target creatures your opponents control to
// their owners' hands."). Unlike executeBounceEffectTo's plain per-target
// bounce (Unsummon/Disperse — a single mandatory target with no controller
// filter) and executeBounceFiltered's self-controller choice shape
// (bounce_filtered.go), this caps the resolved target list at
// effectValue["n"] (mirroring executeTapEffect's max_targets cardinality
// cap above) and, as a safety net independent of upstream targeting
// validation, skips any target slot that isn't an opponent-controlled
// creature — so "up to N" really does mean anywhere from zero to N, and a
// caster's own creature among the supplied targets is never bounced even
// if room remains under the cap.
func (gs *GameState) executeBounceUpToNOpponentCreatures(controller int, effectValue map[string]interface{}, targets []Target) error {
	if controller < 0 || controller >= len(gs.Players) {
		return nil
	}
	n := getIntValue(effectValue, "n", 0)
	bounced := 0
	for _, target := range targets {
		if n > 0 && bounced >= n {
			break
		}
		if !target.IsCard || target.CardID == "" {
			continue
		}
		card, err := gs.Battlefield.GetCard(target.CardID)
		if err != nil || card == nil || !card.IsCreature() || card.Controller == controller {
			continue
		}
		if err := gs.Bounce(card.ID); err != nil {
			continue
		}
		bounced++
	}
	return nil
}
